## A1 — 4:10 — وَسَيَصْلَوْنَ سَعِيرًا

**ARABIC:** إِنَّ الَّذِينَ يَأْكُلُونَ أَمْوَالَ الْيَتَامَىٰ ظُلْمًا إِنَّمَا يَأْكُلُونَ فِي بُطُونِهِمْ نَارًا ۖ وَسَيَصْلَوْنَ سَعِيرًا

**WORD FORM:** وَسَيَصْلَوْنَ — root Ṣ-L-W, future tense (sīn + yaṣlawna), active, plural. Object: سَعِيرًا ("a blazing fire").

**CONTEXT:** MADANI (Surah 4). The verse warns those who devour the property of orphans unjustly: what they swallow is fire — and the consequence line completes the scene with the future ṣ-l-w: "and they will soon be scorched in a Blaze."

**GRAMMAR & LEXICON:**
- The ṣ-l-w verb here takes the FIRE ITSELF as its direct object (يَصْلَوْنَ سَعِيرًا). The same construction recurs at 4:30 (نُصْلِيهِ نَارًا), 4:56 (نُصْلِيهِمْ نَارًا), 4:115 (وَنُصْلِهِ جَهَنَّمَ), 87:12 (النَّارَ الكُبْرَى), 111:3 (نَارًا ذات لَهَبٍ), and with discriminative objects (اُصْلُوهَا "burn therein" 36:64/52:16; يَصْلَوْنَهَا "in it" 14:29, 38:56, 58:8, 82:15). So the root verb's established usage in the Quran = "to be put into the full, sustaining grip of fire's burning" — a state, not a momentary spark.
- Translators consistently pick "burn/roast/scorched therein" (e.g. "Burn in it", Pickthall; "Roast in it", Ghali) — supporting: al-maʿānī lists اُصْلُوهَا under root صَلَى meaning "ادخلوها أو قاسوا حرَّها" (enter it / taste its heat).

**PARALLELS:**
- 2:174 uses the identical devouring idiom for those concealing God's revelation: "…يَأْكُلُونَ إِلَىٰ بُطُونِهِمُ النَّارَ" — swallowing fire inside the belly. 4:10 is the second and only other occurrence of the "eating fire in the belly" idiom, both about profiting wrongfully from a sacred trust.
- The metaphor chain in 4:10: devour → fire inside → future scorching. The fire you store becomes the fire that stores you.

**FINDING FOR "ṢALĀH" (root semantics):**
The root ṣ-l-w (in this usage) characterizes a person who is DELIVERED INTO AND HELD WITHIN the action of a power — he stands inside its reach while it works upon him completely. In the fire-usage this is destruction; but it is precisely the mirror image of the worship usage: at 87:14–15 the same verb فَصَلَّى is what a human does after remembering the name of his Lord — he places himself held-within the reach of God, as the ṣ-l-w-ed person stands within the fire. Ṣalāh, then, is the willing placement of oneself in sustained, uninterrupted presence before God — the same total-exposure root turned from compulsion (fire) to devotion (prayer).

**CARRIED FORWARD:**
1. Confirm the "state not act" reading against the worship uses (87:15, 96:10, 74:43-45, 107:4-7).
2. 2:174–175's إِلَّا مَا يَقْطَعُو? recital texts belongs to the same swallow-fire theme — cite both when compiling.
3. Note for final write-up: the Quran never uses ṣ-l-w for a fleeting act disconnected from a relationship — collect counter-evidence if found in later verses (e.g. 5:106's "بعد الصلاة" usage — D16).

**VERDICT:** studied — see A4 (4:115) next.

## A2 - 4:30 - fa-sawfa nuslihi naran

ARABIC (flow): 4:29 "O you who believe, do not consume one another's wealth unjustly, except through trade by mutual consent, and do not kill yourselves. Indeed God is Merciful to you" -> **4:30 "And whoever does that in aggression and injustice - We will ṣ-l-w him with a Fire, and that was easy for God"** -> 4:31 "If you avoid the major sins which you are forbidden, We will remove from you your lesser sins and admit you to a noble entrance" -> 4:55 "And some of them believed in it and some of them turned away from it, and sufficient is Jahannam as a Sa'ir" -> 4:56 "Indeed those who disbelieved in Our signs - We will ṣ-l-w them with a Fire..." (A3).

WORD FORM: fa-sawfa nuslihi naran - "We will ṣ-l-w him with a Fire" - future tense, divine "We", person + fire as TWO objects (double-object grammar: nuslihi naran = "We ṣ-l-w him [with] a Fire"). The person and the fire are BOTH objects of the verb - the fire is the instrument/medium of the ṣ-l-w.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah an-Nisa' (4). The verse sits in the property-and-life passage (4:29-31): do not consume wealth unjustly, do not kill yourselves (4:29); whoever does that in aggression and injustice - We will ṣ-l-w him with a Fire (4:30); if you avoid major sins, We will remove lesser sins and admit you to a noble entrance (4:31). Then later (4:55-56): some believed, some turned away; Jahannam as Sa'ir; We will ṣ-l-w them with a Fire.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- DOUBLE-OBJECT GRAMMAR: nuslihi naran - the person (him) and the fire (a Fire) are BOTH objects of the verb. The fire is the INSTRUMENT/MEDIUM of the ṣ-l-w: "We will ṣ-l-w him WITH a Fire." The ṣ-l-w is performed WITH the fire - the fire is the tool of the standing-delivery. This matches A1's "swallow fire inside the belly" - the fire is the medium of the ṣ-l-w.
- "AND THAT WAS EASY FOR GOD" (wa-kāna dhālika ʿalā Allāhi yasīran): the ṣ-l-w-delivery is EASY for God. The same ease-formula as 74:17 ("I will burden him with climbing"). The ṣ-l-w is not a difficult act for God; it is His easy act. The standing-delivery is effortless for the Deliverer.
- BREACH-CONTEXTS: the verse is preceded by 4:29's two prohibitions: (1) do not consume wealth unjustly (property), (2) do not kill yourselves (life). The ṣ-l-w is the consequence of violating BOTH property and life rights. The ṣ-l-w is the penalty for injustice toward people's wealth and life. This matches 4:10's (A1) "those who consume the property of orphans unjustly... swallow fire."
- 4:55 "Jahannam as Sa'ir" - the fire-lexicon of the Quran is one coherent system: Jahannam is named as the Sa'ir (blazing). The ṢAL-W is performed WITH the fire; the fire has NAMES (Jahannam, Sa'ir, Jahim, Saqar, Sa'ir). The fire-lexicon is unified.
- "WHOEVER DOES THAT" (man yafʿalu dhālika): the ṣ-l-w is for "whoever does that" - the ṣ-l-w is for the DOER, not the believer. The ṣ-l-w is for the one who ACTS unjustly.

PARALLELS:
- A1 (4:10): A1's "those who consume the property of orphans unjustly... swallow fire" and A2's "whoever does that in aggression and injustice - We will ṣ-l-w him with a Fire" are the SAME breach-context (property-injustice) with the SAME fire-consequence. The ṣ-l-w is the penalty for property-injustice.
- A3 (4:56 - skin-roasting): A3's skin-roasting detail (total contact) will confirm that the ṣ-l-w is TOTAL CONTACT with the fire. A2's "with a Fire" (naran) is the medium; A3's "their skins are roasted" is the effect.
- A5 (14:29 - "Jahannam yaslawnaha"): A5's "they will be ṣ-l-w-ed with her" uses the same verb-object structure as A2's "We will ṣ-l-w him with a Fire." The person is ṣ-l-w-ed WITH the fire.
- 4:31 "If you avoid the major sins... We will remove from you your lesser sins and admit you to a noble entrance" - the POSITIVE counterpart: avoid major sins -> noble entrance. The ṣ-l-w (fire) is for the wrongdoer; the noble entrance is for the avoider. The same standing-state, two directions.

FINDING: A2 confirms the Ṣ-L-W root's fire-side: "We will ṣ-l-w him with a Fire" - the fire is the MEDIUM of the ṣ-l-w. The ṣ-l-w is performed WITH the fire. The fire is not just a punishment; it is the MEDIUM of a standing-state: the person is ṣ-l-w-ed WITH the fire, meaning he is joined into and lodged within the fire's reach. The double-object grammar (person + fire) shows that the ṣ-l-w is a RELATION between the person and the fire: the person is delivered into the fire's reach. And "that was easy for God" shows that the ṣ-l-w is effortless for the Deliverer - the standing-delivery is His easy act.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The fire-lexicon: Jahannam (4:55) + Sa'ir (4:55) + Jahim (A5) + Saqar (A15) + Sa'ir (A17) + Nar al-Kubra (A18) + Sa'ir (A20) + Nar dhat Lahab (A22) - all names for the same fire-medium. HOLD for the final doc's fire-lexicon table. (ii) The "easy for God" formula (4:30) - the ṣ-l-w is effortless for the Deliverer. HOLD for the final doc's ease-table (matches 73:20 "He turned to you in mercy; recite what is easy"). (iii) The property-injustice -> fire-delivery chain: A1 (orphans' property) + A2 (wealth/life injustice) = the fire-ṣalāh is the penalty for property-injustice. HOLD for the final doc's breach-table.

VERDICT: studied - see A3 (4:56) next.

## A3 - 4:56 - sawfa nuslihim naran

ARABIC (flow): 4:55 "And some of them believed in it and some of them turned away from it, and sufficient is Jahannam as a Sa'ir" -> **4:56 "Indeed those who disbelieved in Our signs - We will ṣ-l-w them with a Fire. Every time their skins are roasted through (kullamā nadijat julūduhum), We will replace them with other skins (badalnāhum julūdan ghayrahā) so that they may TASTE (li-yadhūqū) the punishment. Indeed God is Exalted in Might and Wise"** -> 4:57 "And those who believed and did righteous deeds - We will admit them to gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein eternally; for them therein are purified spouses; and We will admit them to a deep shade."

WORD FORM: sawfa nuslihim naran - "We will ṣ-l-w them with a Fire" - future tense, divine "We", them + fire as two objects (same double-object grammar as A2: nuslihim naran). The skin-roasting detail: "kullamā nadijat julūduhum badalnāhum julūdan ghayrahā li-yadhūqū al-ʿadhāba" - "every time their skins are roasted through, We will replace them with other skins so that they may taste the punishment."

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah an-Nisa' (4). The verse sits in the disbeliever-passage (4:55-57): some believed, some turned away; Jahannam as Sa'ir (4:55); We will ṣ-l-w them with a Fire (4:56); the believers admitted to gardens (4:57). The verse is the NEGATIVE counterpart of 4:57 (believers -> gardens).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE SKIN-ROASTING (kullamā nadijat julūduhum): "Every time their skins are roasted through" - the ṣ-l-w is TOTAL CONTACT through the skin. The skin is the organ of EXPOSURE: it is the boundary between the person and the fire. The fire does not just burn the skin; it ROASTS it through (nadija = to be roasted/cooked through). The skin is the organ of contact.
- THE REPLACEMENT (badalnāhum julūdan ghayrahā): "We will replace them with other skins" - the skin is REPLACED so that the roasting can continue. The organ of exposure is replaced so that the exposure never ends. The ṢAL-W is a SUSTAINED, RENEWED standing-state: the exposure is not a moment but a cycle - roast -> replace -> roast -> replace. This is the "renewed cycles" (ṣalawāt) of the fire-side.
- THE TASTING (li-yadhūqū al-ʿadhāba): "so that they may TASTE the punishment" - the ṢAL-W is TASTED (dhawq = tasting). The ṢAL-W is not just felt; it is TASTED - full sensory registration. The same as A20's "no food but dari; no fattening nor satisfying hunger" (88:6-7) - the fire-stand is a continuous tasting. The ṢAL-W is a STATE of continuous tasting, not a momentary burn.
- THE TOTAL CONTACT: the skin is roasted through (nadija), replaced (badal), and tasted (dhawq). The ṢAL-W is TOTAL CONTACT through the skin, with the skin replaced so the contact never ends. The ṢAL-W is a SUSTAINED, RENEWED, TOTAL standing-state: the person is joined into and lodged within the fire's reach, and the contact is never-ending.
- "INDEED GOD IS EXALTED IN MIGHT AND WISE" (ʿazīzan ḥakīman): the ṢAL-W is performed by the Mighty and Wise God. The ṢAL-W is a wise and mighty act - the same as D12 (4:162 "the firm in knowledge... establishers of the ṢALĀH") - the ṢAL-W requires might and wisdom.

PARALLELS:
- A2 (4:30 - "We will ṣ-l-w him with a Fire"): A2's "with a Fire" (naran) is the medium; A3's "their skins are roasted" is the effect. The medium (fire) and the effect (skin-roasting) are paired: the fire roasts the skin; the skin is replaced; the fire roasts again.
- A5 (14:29 - "Jahannam yaslawnaha"): A5's "they will be ṣ-l-w-ed with her" uses the same verb-object structure. The person is ṣ-l-w-ed WITH the fire. A3's skin-roasting shows what "with the fire" means: total contact.
- A20 (88:4-7 - "tusla naran hamiya... no food but dari"): A20's body-list (drink, food, hearing, furniture) confirms A3's "total contact": the fire-stand is a FULL-BODY state. A3's skin-roasting is the SKIN-level of the full-body state.
- A12 (56:94 - "tasliya" = kept-up roasting): A12's "tasliya" (kept-up roasting) is the NOUN of A3's "kullamā nadijat" (every time roasted through). The fire-ṣalāh is a KEPT-UP roasting: roast -> replace -> roast -> replace. The noun (tasliya) and the verb (nadija) describe the same cycle.
- 4:57 "those who believed... We will admit them to gardens" - the POSITIVE counterpart: the believers are admitted to gardens; the disbelievers are ṣ-l-w-ed with Fire. The same standing-state, two directions: fire-delivery vs garden-admission.

FINDING: A3 confirms that the Ṣ-L-W root's fire-side is a SUSTAINED, RENEWED, TOTAL standing-state: the skin is roasted through (nadija), replaced (badal), and tasted (dhawq) - the exposure is never-ending. The ṢAL-W is not a momentary burn but a CYCLE of exposure: roast -> replace -> roast -> replace. The skin is the organ of exposure, and it is replaced so the exposure never ends. This is the "renewed cycles" (ṣalawāt) of the fire-side: the ṢAL-W is a RENEWED standing-state, not a one-time event. And the ṢAL-W is TASTED (dhawq) - full sensory registration, not a passing sensation. The ṢAL-W is a STATE of continuous tasting, renewed through skin-replacement.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The SKIN-ROASTING cycle (nadija -> badal -> dhawq) - the fire-ṣalāh is a cycle of exposure. HOLD for the final doc's cycle-table. (ii) The TASTING (dhawq) - the ṢAL-W is tasted, not just felt. HOLD for the final doc's sensory-table (matches A20's body-list: drink/food/hearing/furniture/taste/skin). (iii) The REPLACEMENT (badal) - the organ of exposure is replaced so the exposure never ends. HOLD for the final doc's renewal-table (matches A12's tasliya = kept-up roasting). (iv) The TOTAL CONTACT (nadija = roasted through) - the ṢAL-W is total contact through the skin. HOLD for the final doc's contact-table (matches A1's "swallow fire inside the belly").

VERDICT: studied - see A4 (4:115) next.
## A4 — 4:115 — وَنُصْلِهِ جَهَنَّمَ

**ARABIC:** وَمَن يُشَاقِقِ الرَّسُولَ مِن بَعْدِ مَا تَبَيَّنَ لَهُ الْهُدَىٰ وَيَتَّبِعْ غَيْرَ سَبِيلِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ نُوَلِّهِ مَا تَوَلَّىٰ وَنُصْلِهِ جَهَنَّمَ وَسَاءَتْ مَصِيرًا

**WORD FORM:** وَنُصْلِهِ — root Ṣ-L-W, divine "We", person + جَهَنَّمَ as the two objects. Preceded by its twin deliver-verb نُوَلِّهِ مَا تَوَلَّىٰ.

**CONTEXT:** MADANI. Flow: 4:113 (God's favor on the Prophet against would-be misleaders) → 4:114 (good whispering: charity, maʿrūf, reconciliation for God's pleasure — "great reward") → 4:115 (defying the Messenger after clarity + following a course other than the believers' — steered to his own steering + delivered into Jahannam) → 4:116 (the unforgivable shirk).

**GRAMMAR & LEXICON:**
- The verb's object range expands from generic نَارًا (A1–A3) to the NAMED PLACE as exposure-agent: "…and We will ṣ-l-w him with Jahannam." A proverbial abyss is treated exactly like raw fire: an enveloping delivering-power.
- The critical syntax pairing: نُوَلِّهِ مَا تَوَلَّىٰ — "We will leave him to what he himself turned toward." God's delivery (نُصْلِهِ) completes the person's own steering (تَوَلَّىٰ). The delivered-exposure is not an arbitrary sentence: it is the person's own chosen direction granted its full destination.
- وَسَاءَتْ مَصِيرًا — "how evil a returning-settlement (maṣīr)" — the delivery is framed as a SETTLEMENT, a residence, not a passing burn.

**PARALLELS:**
- The breach listed is "يُشَاقِقِ الرَّسُولَ … وَيَتَّبِعْ غَيْرَ سَبِيلِ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ" — walking off the believers' shared course. This quietly anticipates the ṣalāh-usage as a shared community act (9:71 D25, Friday D43–44).
- 4:114's reward counterpart uses نُؤْتِيهِ أَجْرًا عَظِيمًا — God "delivers" reward to reconciliation-trust acts; here نُصْلِهِ "delivers" the defier to fire — the same divine-delivery grammar serves both.

**FINDING:** In the fire-usage, ṣ-l-w is always the END-STATE OF SELF-CHOSEN MISDIRECTION: own steering (تَوَلَّىٰ) becomes a delivered settlement (نُصْلِهِ، مَصِير). Ṣalāh inherits the same relational structure — but with the direction chosen rightly toward God rather than away.

**CARRIED FORWARD:** (i) Collect the "settlement" meta-words around fire-ṣ-l-w: مَصِير (4:115), بِئْسَ الْقَرَارُ (14:29), بِئْسَ الْمِهَادُ (38:56). (ii) Watch for the positive counterpart — God delivering people into His own deliverance (33:43, B3).

**VERDICT:** studied — see A5 (14:29) next.

## A5 — 14:29 — جَهَنَّمَ يَصْلَوْنَهَا

**ARABIC:** جَهَنَّمَ يَصْلَوْنَهَا وَبِئْسَ الْقَرَارُ

**WORD FORM:** يَصْلَوْنَهَا — root Ṣ-L-W, future plural, pronoun هَا attached to the front-loaded جَهَنَّمَ.

**CONTEXT:** MAKAN (Surah 14). Flow: 14:26 (the evil word = an uprooted tree, مَا لَهَا مِن قَرَار) → 14:27 (God firm-founds the believers by the firm word) → 14:28 (those who exchanged God's favor for kufr and settled their people in the house of ruin) → 14:29 (Jahannam — they shall be ṣ-l-w-ed with her — and awful a settling-place).

**GRAMMAR & LEXICON:**
- يَصْلَوْنَهَا — the pronoun attaches to Jahannam while the PEOPLE are subject: classical tafsīr glosses it as entering her / being lodged in her. The grammar itself teaches the root: when a person ṣ-l-w-s "her" (the fire), he is not merely exposed to a flame; he is JOINED INTO the fire's own residence — the fire becomes his قَرَار (settled abode).
- وَبِئْسَ الْقَرَارُ — the "settling" lemma closes the verse: fire-delivery is explicitly a continuous SETTLEMENT. Matches A4's مَصِيرًا — stable meta-language.
- Structural irony: 14:26's uprooted tree has NO settling on earth (مَا لَهَا مِن قَرَار); its "settling" arrives only as Jahannam. And contrast 14:24's good word: كَشَجَرَةٍ طَيِّبَةٍ أَصْلُهَا ثَابِتٌ — a rooted tree. The Quran deliberately sets أَصْلُهَا (her root, firm) against أَصْلُوْهَا/اصلوه (the fire-address of 36:64/52:16, later A9/A11) — rootedness versus delivered-exposure as the two ways of "being founded".

**PARALLELS:**
- 19:69–70: "…أَشَدُّ عَلَى الرَّحْمَٰنِ عَصَوًا … ثُمَّ لَنَحْنُ أَعْلَمُ بِالَّذِينَ هُمْ أَوْلَىٰ بِهَا صِلِيًّا" — the participial صِلِيًّا (A7) treats ṣ-l-w exposure as an earned status again confirming "state, not act".
- بِئْسَ الْقَرَارُ also at 25:66; بِئْسَ الْمِهَادُ at 38:56 (A10).

**FINDING:** ṣ-l-w + fire = being JOINED INTO AND LODGED IN the fire's own residence. The noun ṣalāh's underlying image is thereby: a person LODGED in the presence of the power he addresses — for the fire, damnation; for God, a settled standing (مُقِيمُ الصَّلَاةِ, مُصَلَّى — C-cluster).

**CARRIED FORWARD:** (i) Confirm the residence-incorporation reading systematically at 17:18, 92:15 (A6/A19) and 22:40-separate issues (A8: the ṣallawāt-of-places-of-worship — a stratification of the "settling with a power" idea). (ii) 14:24-tree vs 36:64-root-fire contrast to be showcased in the final document.

**VERDICT:** studied — see A6 (17:18) next.

## A6 — 17:18 — ثُمَّ جَعَلْنَا لَهُ جَهَنَّمَ يَصْلَاهَا مَذْمُومًا مَدْحُورًا

**ARABIC:** مَن كَانَ يُرِيدُ الْعَاجِلَةَ عَجَّلْنَا لَهُ فِيهَا مَا نَشَاءُ لِمَن نُّرِيدُ ثُمَّ جَعَلْنَا لَهُ جَهَنَّمَ يَصْلَاهَا مَذْمُومًا مَدْحُورًا

**WORD FORM:** يَصْلَاهَا — root Ṣ-L-W, future singular, person subject, her (Jahannam) attached — same form as 92:15 (A19) — twin pair to be confirmed together.

**CONTEXT:** MAKAN. Flow: 17:16 (a town doomed through its luxury-class's transgression) → 17:17 (generations destroyed after Noah) → 17:18 (the Hereafter-forgetful: he WANTS the immediate → he gets his instant portion → THEN "We ASSIGNED him Jahannam" as his lodging, blameworthy and driven-off) → 17:19–20 (the other side: whoever wants the Hereafter and strives for it as a believer — his striving is thanked; TO BOTH God extends His gifts un-hindered).

**GRAMMAR & LEXICON:**
- **جَعَلْنَا لَهُ جَهَنَّمَ** — "We MADE FOR HIM Jahannam" — the lodging is explicitly ASSIGNED to the person, matching A5's residence reading (يَصْلَوْنَهَا + قَرَار). Two grammatical proofs now that the fire-ṣ-l-w is a settled lodging, not a stray flash.
- **يَصْلَاهَا مَذْمُومًا مَدْحُورًا** — the two participles are the person's delivered-STATE: blameworthy (مَذْمُوم) and driven-off/repelled (مَدْحُور — the honor-void). This is the direct negative of the divine-ṣalāh verses (2:157 عليهم صَلَوَاتٌ; 33:43 …لِيُخْرِجَكُم) where God's ṣalāh RAISES and honors. The root-term's two directions: entered-fire (disgraced, expelled) vs. entered-Mercy (honored, brought out into light).
- The وtm triple-عج ل root-play (يُرِيدُ الْعَاجِلَةَ … عَجَّلْنَا لَهُ … فِيهَا) shows his desire-tempo sets his account-tempo: he who races for the quick is *raced* to his quickness — then the assignment. The fire-frame is the person's own tempo fulfilled — matching A4's تولّيه → نصله.

**PARALLELS:**
- 17:19–20 is the mirror half and must be kept beside this verse in the final doc: same "desire-verb" frame (مَن أَرَادَ الْآخِرَةَ وَسَعَىٰ لَهَا سَعْيَهَا وَهُوَ مُؤْمِنٌ) — his striving is "thanked" while the other's delivers into fire. Desire is not condemned; its OBJECT is.
- Twin form → A19 (92:15) pending in sequence.

**FINDING:** Fire-ṣ-l-w #3rd grammatical proof of "assigned lodging". The delivered person is described by two honor-vocabularies NEGATED (blameworthy, expelled) — telling us the ṣ-l-w state also has an **honor economy** attached. That economy is the exact property the noun ṣalāh claims on the positive side (God's ṣalāh = honoring; see B-cluster). Root = "assignment of standing": standing assigned to whoever he himself favored.

**CARRIED FORWARD:** (i) A19 (92:15) confirm-reading "لَا يَصْلَاهَا إِلَّا الْأَشْقَى الَّذِي كَذَبَ وَتَوَلَّىٰ" — plus its counterpart وَسَيُجَنَّبُهَا الْأَتْقَىٰ (92:17) — God himself steers the muttaqī AWAY (a deliverance-delivery in the same verb family). (ii) The honor-economy binary (مَذْمُوم/مَدْحُور vs مُحَفَّوْن? — later B) is to be tabulated in the final doc.

**VERDICT:** studied — see A7 (19:70) next.

## A7 — 19:70 — أَوْلَىٰ بِهَا صِلِيًّا

**ARABIC:** ثُمَّ لَنَحْنُ أَعْلَمُ بِالَّذِينَ هُمْ أَوْلَىٰ بِهَا صِلِيًّا

**WORD FORM:** صِلِيًّا — root Ṣ-L-W, active participle (conative/comparative halal): "deserving of being ṣ-l-w-ed therein" — from صَلِيٍّ/صَلَوَانِ.

**CONTEXT:** MAKAN. Flow: 19:58 (graced prophets fell weeping…), **19:59 (فَخَّلَفَ مِن بَعْدِهِمْ خَلْفٌ أَضَاعُوا الصَّلَاةَ وَاتَّبَعُوا الشَّهَوَاتِ — "they LOST THE ṢALĀH and followed desires" — E5, the ṣalāh-noun!), 19:60 (those who repent, believe and do righteous — they enter the Garden un-oppressed), 19:61 (Gardens of ʿAdn…), 19:68 (…We WILL gather them and the devils around CHANNAM kneeling (جُثَىً)), 19:69 (then from every sect We will detach those most violent against al-Rahman), 19:70 (ثُمَّ لَنَحْنُ أَعْلَمُ بِالَّذِينَ هُمْ أَوْلَىٰ بِهَا صِلِيًّا — We know best who are most worthy of being ṣ-l-w-ed in her).

**GRAMMAR & LEXICON:**
- **صِلِيًّا** as an active participle gives ṣ-l-w the vocabulary of an EARNED TITLE: worthiness-of-delivery, an attribute one grows into, not a sentence served once. This is the participle-anchor for the fire-side of the root (matching "دائِمُونَ"–"يُحَافِظُونَ" style diligence on the devotion-side later).
- **أَوْلَىٰ بِهَا** — "more entitled TO HER (i.e., to Jahannam)" — again possession-grammar: the fire is a property of the worthy. Linguistically unmistakable: ṣ-l-w = *being joined into (one's own kind of) place*.

**PARALLELS:**
- **19:59–70 chain is the chapter's explicit bridge between the NOUN (ṣalāh, lost) and the VERB (fire-ṣ-l-w, earned):** losing the ṣalāh → following desires → kneeling to Jahannam → being ṣ-l-w-ed in her. Verse 19:71 (وَإِن مِّنكُمْ إِلَّا وَارِدُهَا — every one of you shall ARRIVE at it) vs. 19:72 (then We deliver those who were ever patterning taqwā) adds the closing distinction: ARRIVAL (at the fire) is universal; DELIVERY-FREEDOM (عَارِضُهَا? — actually نُنَجِّي) is earned. Note this by C-cluster too: the Arabic has arrivals (وَارِدُ) and screenings (عَاضَ? — no — نُنَجِّي define-free-deliver).
- E5 (19:59) will carry this chapter's noun-verse in full — cross-link both entries in compilation.

**FINDING:** Within ONE chapter (19), the Quran chains: (*they lost their ṣalāh*) → (*desires*) → (kneeling at fire) → (*worthily ṣ-l-w-ed*). The fire-ṣ-l-w here is shown as the state PREPARED BY losing ṣalāh itself. The noun and the verb of the same root describe one and the same standing-assignment — devotional standing displaced, fire-standing imposed.

**CARRIED FORWARD:** (i) 19:71–72 entry-type check during D-cluster (وَارِد vs نُنَجِّي pairing with 92:17's سَيُجَنَّبُهَا). (ii) "اَلَّذِينَ هُمْ أَوْلَىٰ"-worthiness-language — collect all أَوْلَى formulas (they recur with the fire/each cluster) in the final doc.

**VERDICT:** studied — see A9 (36:64) next.

## A8 - 22:40 - wa-salawatun (the outlier: PLACES, not states)

ARABIC (flow): 22:38 "Indeed God defends those who believe; indeed God does not love every treacherous ingrate" -> 22:39 "Permission [to fight] has been given to those who are fought, because they have been wronged. And indeed God is able to give them victory" -> **22:40 "Those who have been evicted from their homes without right - only because they say: Our Lord is God. And were it not that God checks the people, some by means of others, there would have been demolished monasteries, churches, synagogues (ṣalawāt), and mosques - [places] in which the name of God is mentioned abundantly. And God will surely support those who support Him. Indeed God is Powerful, Exalted in Might"** -> 22:41 "Those who, if We give them authority in the land, establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and enjoin what is right and forbid what is wrong. And to God belongs the outcome of all matters" -> 22:42 "And if they deny you, then already have those before them denied: the people of Noah and 'Ad and Thamud."

WORD FORM: ṣalawātun (plural of ṣalāh) - used of BUILDINGS demolished-in-counterfactual: "there would have been demolished monasteries, churches, ṣalawāt, and mosques - [places] in which the name of God is mentioned abundantly." The plural here coincides in shape with ṣalawāt of 2:157 (God's blessings) and 23:9 (the believer's ṣalawāt), but the context (demolition of buildings) makes the referent PLACES, not states.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Hajj (22). The verse sits in the fighting-passage (22:38-42): God defends the believers (22:38), permission to fight (22:39), the evicted and their protected sites (22:40), the empowered who establish the ṢALĀH (22:41), and if they deny you (22:42).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE OUTLIER: this is the ONE occurrence of the s-l-w family where the referent is a category of PLACES. Classical tafsīr (Ibn Kathīr, Qurtubī et al.) identifies ṣalawāt here as the houses-of-prayer of the Jews (a loan word in that community's usage; the same ṣ-l-w root carries the "worship/prayer" sense in sister-Semitic languages, e.g. Syriac ṣlōtā "prayer"). So the Quran deliberately REACHES for the s-l-w word-family even when naming the prayer-houses of OTHER communities.
- THE DEFINING CLAUSE: "yudhkaru fīhā ismu Allāhi kathīran" - "in which the name of God is mentioned MANY TIMES." Whatever the community's own naming, the Quran assigns ONE criterion: sites dedicated to the frequent MENTION of God's Name. A s-l-w-word-site = "a place assigned to standing/keeping before God's Name."
- THE SYMMETRY: other communities get the PLURAL site-word (ṣalawāt); the believers get the SINGULAR assigned-spot (MUSSALLĀ, 2:125, C5). Same root, two site-lexemes framing "theirs" and "ours." The plural = their houses (many); the singular = our spot (one, assigned by Abraham).
- THE LAND-GOVERNANCE FRAMING: this Ṣ-L-W word appears in a discussion of FIGHTING, EVICTION, DEFENSE OF NAME-MENTIONING PLACES, and DOMINION (22:38-42) - immediately followed by believers in power "establishing the ṢALĀH" (22:41). The ṢALĀH-word appears in a LAND-GOVERNANCE question, not a private-spirituality question. This is important for Cluster D (ṢALĀH as life-institution).

PARALLELS:
- C5 (2:125 - MUSSALLĀ, Abraham's assigned spot): the SINGULAR site-word for the believers. A8 (plural, theirs) vs C5 (singular, ours) - the root's "site" lexeme frames both communities.
- A8's defining clause (yudhkaru fīhā ismu Allāhi kathīran) matches A19's (87:1 "glorify the Name of your Lord") and B1's (24:36 "in houses which God has permitted to be raised, in which His Name is mentioned"). The Name-mention criterion is STABLE across all three.
- 22:41 (D32 - "if we enable them: establish the ṢALĀH...") is the direct continuation: when the believers are the ones ENABLED, the first listed act is establishing the ṢALĀH. The site-word (A8) and the state-word (D32) are paired in the same passage.

FINDING: A8 proves the Ṣ-L-W family ALSO works as a lexeme-of-SITES: "assigned-to-God's-Name-mention places" for any community. The believers get the singular MUSSALLĀ (C5); other communities get the plural ṢALAWĀT (A8). The root's SITE-pole has TWO lexemes: ṢALAWĀT (their houses) and MUSSALLA (our spot). The Quran's own defining clause (yudhkaru fīhā ismu Allāhi kathīran) assigns ONE criterion: a place dedicated to the frequent MENTION of God's Name. This triangulates the root's three stable poles: SITE (space assigned to standing with the Name) - STATE (assigned standing itself, willingly kept) - DELIVERY (assigned standing, imposed).

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) Test at 9:17-18 (D24) whether "mosques of God" grammar confirms the site/state interplay. (ii) At C5 (2:125) confirm MUSSALLĀ as the site-lemma. (iii) In the final document: a table of Ṣ-L-W "place" lexemes: ṢALAWĀT (22:40), MUSSALLĀ (2:125), and the negative-space of "masājid Allāh."

VERDICT: studied - see A9 (36:64) next.
## A9 - 36:64 - usluwaha al-yawma bima kuntum takfuroon

ARABIC (flow): 36:59 wamtazoo al-yawma ayyuhal mujrimoon -> 36:60-61 the two betrayed covenants (not to serve satan; to serve Me - "this is a straight path") -> 36:63 hathihi jahannam allatee kuntum tooadoon -> 36:64 usluwha al-yawma bima kuntum takfuroon -> 36:65 al-yawma nakhtimu ala afwaahihim wa tukallimuna aydeehim.

WORD FORM: usluwha - root S-L-W, imperative plural addressed TO the sinners themselves; Jahannam object.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- With A9 the fire-verb enters the IMPERATIVE voice: the sinner is TOLD, not merely described. Classical glosses both live in the lexica: "enter into it" and "taste its heat / stand under its full reach" (e.g. tafseer al-muyassar: udkhulooha wqasooh harraha). We take these as the same assigned-delivery, voiced as a command to the delivered one - not a different sense.
- al-yawma frames the passage twice (36:59 wamtazoo AL-YAWM, 36:64 usluwha AL-YAWM): the fire-address is the settlement of the expiry of the two covenants.
- 36:65 closes: mouths sealed, HANDS AND FEET speak (tukallimuna aydeehim) and legs testify of what they earned - they withheld Gods word in life; the evidence speaks instead. (Same frame as 52:16 ennama-l tujzawna ma kuntum tamaloon - fixed Quranic pair.)

PARALLELS:
- 36:60-61 is the covenant anchor of the whole fire-cluster: worship vs denial (kufr) is the key that opens this root delivery (matches 92:16 alladhee kathaba wa dalla).
- A4 (tawalla -> Nurs-al) has here its imperative twin: the delivered one is the one who heard.

FINDING: all four Arabic voices now attested for fire-S-L-W: future self-modality (sayaslawna), divine performance (nUsallehi), earned title (Saliyyan), self-command (usluwha). One and the same settled directed standing within a power; the covenant-response is the key.

CARRIED FORWARD: pair "bima kuntum takfuroon" + "innama tujzawna ma kuntum tamaloon"; compare site-patience differentiation later.

VERDICT: studied - see A10 (38:56) next.

## A10 - 38:56 - jahannam yaslawonha fabi'sal mihad

ARABIC (flow): 38:55 hatha wa inna lil ttagheena la shar maab -> 38:56 jahannam yaslawonha fabi'sal mihad -> 38:57 hatha falyathooquhu hameemun wa ghassaq -> 38:58 wa akharu min shaklihi azwaaj.

WORD FORM: yaslawonha - S-L-W future plural, pronoun to Jahannam (same incorporation-grammar as 14:29 A5).

CONTEXT: MAKAN. Garden-side first: "hatha" (this = the Garden) - provision unexhaustible (38:54). Then the transgressors: shar MAAB (evil return-place) - Jahannam as lodging - "a TERRIBLE BEDDING (mihad)" - then taste (falyathooqu) the scalding drink and pus.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- Settlement-words now accrue: MAAB (return-hostel) in 38:55, MIHAD (bedding) in 38:56. Together with A4's masir and A5's qarar, the fire-side lexicon = "where one returns, lodges, beds" - LIVING-words, not burn-words. The Quran sells the fire as a HOME-address.
- يذوق (fal yathooqoo) - the taste verb again (cf. 4:56 li yathooqul adhaab): full sensory registration like A3's skin-replacement; the exposure is CONSTANT REGISTRATION, not one shock.
- al-TTAGHEEN here: those who OVERSTEP (the word of the transgressor whose own binding is over-grown) - the fire-cluster's standard profile: hood-surge-of-desire (matches 19:59 shahawaat; 17:18 il ajila).

PARALLELS:
- mihad also only appears here once plus as sword-blade in other usage; Quran here one-place - keep in the settlement-table (masir, qarar, mihad, Maab).
- 14:28-29's darul bawar (house of ruin) sits side by side; same s-l-w + lodging-words.

FINDING: lodging-and-taste grammar reaches a stable formula: S-L-W delivers into a residence whose effects are continuously tasted. The fire-usage has now completed the STATE-DELIVERY picture with "living" vocabulary - the delivered one is HOMED, voiced, commanded to enter, entitled in her - always delivered.

CARRIED FORWARD: tabulate lodging-words (masir / qarar / mihad / Maab) vs ṣalāh-sides future words (e.g. مقام, مصل, قاموا). The root assigns "standing" either way - and the Quran uses LIVING-words for the fire to show the person's WHOLE life displaced.

VERDICT: studied - see A11 (52:16) next.

## A11 - 52:16 - usluwha fasbiroo aw laa tasbiroo sawaaun alaykum

ARABIC (flow): 52:13 yawma yudAAawna ila naar jahannam daAAaa (summoned TO the fire like a summons) -> 52:14 hathihi an-naaru allatee kuntum biha takathiboon -> 52:15 afasihru hatha am antum laa tubsiroon -> 52:16 usluwha fasbiroo aw laa tasbiroo sawaaun alaykum innama tujzawna ma kuntum tamaloon.

WORD FORM: usluwha - imperative as in A9.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- Summons-grammar (52:13, du'iyyan du'iyyan "a summons like a summons"): the fire-delivery is framed as a CALL or INVITATION addressed to the delivered one - the fire is treated as a HOST who calls his guest; the guest arrives (this matches 14:29s lodging grammar), he is already met by his place.
- fasbiroo aw laa tasbiroo sawaa'UN alaykum - patience would be a response; the verse cuts off even that choice: within the fire's reach no further act differentiates anyone. Responses belong on THIS side of the deliverer's edge.
- innama tujzawna ma kuntum tamaloon - SELF-STEERING again, closing the delivered-state back to the person's own record (same as 36:64's bima kuntum takfuroon).

PARALLELS:
- 52:15 (do you not SEE?) vs A2, A9 - the standing's coordination in qaSir sense - he who DID NOT see is the one delivered; the Quran's seeing-block.
- Compare A1 metaphor-line: the swallow fire (covenants "bima kuntum takfuroon") - "the one used-to deny is now the one to hold the fire" - same key as the Swallow AGE.

FINDING: the imperative voice twice (A9, A11) sets the icon-free part: the fire-delivery is not merely a sentence but a FINISHED course of occupancy IN DARKNESS-and the "summons" of the delivered one (dAAaa dAAaa) frames standing-with in a game-theory of "course" and "near" framing.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) Log the summon-word with the الح clusters: the noun ṣalāh uses CALLING-language one can also stand (like "النصارى god"؛ for final doc see the "call" words mid-D (62:9 - nadee - C-D). (ii) "sawaa-un alaykum" engagement: whether THE ṣalāh-site-side ever uses equalizing for absence of HIS record (D in-cluster: لا فرق? - "لا فرق" side). place.

VERDICT: studied - see A12 (56:94) next.

## A12 - 56:94 - wa-tasliyatu jahim

ARABIC (flow): 56:75-80 oath block: "I swear by the settings of the stars - and that is a mighty oath if you knew - it is a NOBLE QURAN, in a hidden Book (kitab makanun), NONE TOUCH IT except the PURIFIED (la yamssuhu illa-l mutahharoon), a downpour from the Lord of the worlds" -> 56:81-82 the ironic charge: "is THIS the discourse you are made light of? and you make your denial your livelihood?" -> 56:83-87 challenge: why do you not RETURN the soul when it reaches the throat (halqum), you watching while We are NEARER TO HIM than you but you do not SEE (wa nahnu aqrabu ilayhi minkum wa lakin la tubsiroon)? If you are not to be recompensed, return it if you are truthful -> 56:88-94 THE THREE-WAY FINAL SPLIT: (1) muqarraboon -> ruh/rihan/gardens of bliss; (2) ashab al-yamin -> "salam [= a greeting] unto you from the companions of the right"; (3) mukadhdhibeen dhalleen -> NAZL of hamim AND wa-t-S-L-I-Yatu jahim -> 56:95 "this is truly the truth of certainty" -> 56:96 tasbih-bookend: "so glorify the NAME of your great Lord" (fasabbih bi-ismi rabbik al-azim).

WORD FORM: wa-t-S-L-I-Yatu - the MAQDAR (verbal noun) of the same verb Salla, construct with jahim: "and a Sustained-Roasting of jahim". NOTE: the verse's spelling follows the idafa of two served-thing-nouns: "nazlu hamimin [and] tasliyatu jahim" - A SERVING of boiling water ... a SERVING of sustained roasting.

CONTEXT: MAKKAN, Surah 56 (scene of the three ranks at the Day). The fire-delivery here is greeved and positioned at the END of a chapter whose WHOLE architecture is STANDING-CATEGORIES: who gets DRAWN NEAR (muqarrab), who gets got the greeting SALAM, who is denied his return-soul and then SERVED his two things.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- SOUND-FORM GIFT: the fire-word here is NOT just a verb - it is the ACTS-NOUN of the root itself (t-S-L-I-ya, the same morphology family as tasbiha -> tasbih, tujuzzI -> tazkiya). At the reverence-side the nouns of this root-name pattern (salah / salawat / tasliya); here the SAME acts-noun vowel-shape names the fire-state. This is the STRONGEST formal proof yet that fire-S-L-W and devotional-S-L-W share ONE semantic architecture: whichever direction, THE ROOT NAMES A KEPT-UP STANDING-STATE - for Jahim it is a kept-up roasting; for God it is a kept-up facing.
- HOSPITALITY/CATERING GRAMMAR: NAZL (a served-portion/lodging-thing) + TASLIYA: BOTH nouns are itemised like a hospitality bill: the delivered one is not dragged gratuitously - he is SERVED his heating-water and SERVED his sustained-roasting. The delivery is pre-prepared, named, presented - matches A6's "made for him" assigned-lodging; here even the nouns are SERVICE-format.
- NEARNESS-VS-SIGHT: 56:85 ("We are NEARER to him than you, but you do not SEE") sits only nine verses before the fire-split. The S-L-W-standing-state's deep double is drawn here: God's proximity is CONSTANT (nearer than the whole crowd); the eyes do not REGISTER it. The delivered standing-state is exactly what REFUSED-proximity becomes - full-contact fire replaces un-contacted Mercy. (Compare 50:16 "akrabu ilayhi min hablil warid" brothers.)

PARALLELS:
- Purity-gate: la yamssuhu illa-L MUTAHAROON (56:79) - touch-contact of the Book gated on purification - the SAME contact-vocabulary the root's standing-state lives in. Collect later (74:4 purify-your-garments), the gate-party of standing.
- Salam-in-greeting: ashab al-yamin get "SALAM - a greeting" (56:91) - the positive side of S-L-W's charge uses GREETING-vocabulary on the separate category-sign; the noun Salah itself (B/C clusters) uses SALAM-anchored verses (e.g. 27:28 hmm - verify later with 33:44 "was-salamu minhu tahiyyatan").
- Muqarraboon = THE DRAWN-NEAR CLASS: the Quran's positive S-L-W-side categories name PROXIMITY as rank (muqarrab - drummed at 56:11, 56:88). The root's standing-state = proximity actualised; fire = proximity refused arriving anyway.

FINDING: fire-S-L-W is here named with the NOUN of the root itself (Tasliya) in SERVICED format: a kept-up, presented, personal standing-charge - like a hospitality-bill for one's own chosen rank. The fires' nouns, the delivered one's nouns, are never verbs only; the root LINGERS as a NAME of state whichever direction it travels.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) Collect every t-S-L-I-type maqdar/no other nouns appearances of the root - they will anchor the final doc's "SAME noun, two directions" table (Tasliya 56:94 vs Salah 2:3 / salawat 2:157 & 23:9, musalla 2:125, for the noun-map). (ii) Track purity-gates (56:79; 74:4 mutatahhireen/others) - entry-ticket to the standing-state. (iii) The three-split of 56:88-94 (muqarrab / ashab al-yamin / mukadhdhibin dhalleen) is a clean TEMPLATE - hold it against later C/D/E verses that list S-L-W-classes; test if standing-with-God and rec Salam gives/fire gets Hamim.

VERDICT: studied - see A13 (58:8) next.

## A13 - 58:8 - hasbuhum jahannam yaslawnaha fa-bi'sa-l masir

ARABIC (flow): 58:1-4 the zihar case: the wife who pled against her husband (qad sami-a-LLahu qawl allatee...); the covenant-word he SPOKE (declaring his wife like his mother) must be UNDONE by ranked acts - slave-freedom / two months fasting / feeding sixty poor - LITU-UMINU BILLAhi wa-rasulihi "SO THAT YOU BE CERTAIN IN GOD AND HIS MESSENGER" -> 58:5 those who fight God and His messenger are "held squeezed (kibtu) like those before them" -> 58:6 the Day: God will tell them what they did, having COUNTED it (ahsahu) while THEY FORGOT IT -> 58:7 THE PRIVACY-OMNIPRESENCE VERSE: "Do you not see that God KNOWS what is in the heavens and the earth? There is NO SECRET COUNSEL (najwa) of three but He is their FOURTH; nor of five but He is their sixth; fewer or more - HE IS WITH THEM (ma'ahum) wherever they are" -> 58:8 THE CHARGE-VERSE: those who were forbidden secret counsel then RETURN to it, plotting sin/hostility/disobeying the messenger, greeting you with a greeting GOD DID NOT GREET YOU BY (hayyooka bi-ma lam yahyika bihi-LLah), whispering to themselves: "Why would God punish us for what we say?" - HASBUHUM JAHANNAM - YASLAWNHA - FA-BI'SA AL-MASIR -> 58:9 the believer-counsel-contrast: counsel by BIRR AND TAQWA and "fear He TO WHOM YOU WILL BE GATHERED" -> 58:10 "secret counsel is only from satan, to GRIEVE the believers - yet it cannot harm them at all except by Gods permission."

WORD FORM: yaslawnha - S-L-W future plural, pronoun to the fire (the SAME incorporation-grammar as A5 14:29 and A10 38:56); attached to HASBUHUM JAHANNAM: "Jahannam SUFFICES them - they will be s-l-w-ed within her - and evil a DESTINY (masir)".

CONTEXT: MADANI (structural-counsel passage). The fire-word sits in a passage about PACKAGE THOUGHT: secret speech is COUNTED and KNOWN; the three/five/fourth grammar makes NO WHISPER ever truly private; then the mockers are named; then the sufficed-fire verdict; then the counter-protocol for believer-counsel.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- SUFFICING-GRAMMAR (the new frame): HASBUHUM JAHANNAM = "Jahannam is their sufficing/them-account". They whispered "He will not punish us"; the answer assigns the fire AS THEIR SUFFICER: the One they tried to DISMISS from their account is replaced by the fire as their permanent sufficient-account (cf. hasbuna-LLahu wa-ni'ma-l wakil, 3:173 sakafa bi-LLah). The fire is thereby never a bolt from the blue - it is a RECRUITED SERVANT of the person's own speech-record.
- CONSTANT-PRESENCE VS STANDING-WITH: 58:7 (ma'ahum aynna ma kanu - He is WITH them wherever) sits one verse above the fire-charge. THE WHOLE A-CLUSTER THESIS APPLIES HERE AT ITS TIGHTEST: God was ALWAYS at their side; they chose NON-CONTACT; the S-L-W standing-state is exactly what the CONTACT costs when contact is refused: a REGISTERED standing-state within the reach they mocked (cf. 56:85 - God NEARER but unseen; 50:16 nearer than jugular-vane).
- FALSE GREETING: hayyooka bi-ma LAM yahyika BAHU - they deliver the greeting that God never gave them. This is the SIGN from the reward-side's SALAM grammar (56:91 salam uma... 33:44 salam minhu (tahiyyatan)) - the mock salam vs the REAL salam - the fire-charge is the anti-salam standing-state (matches A6's madhmuman madhuran honor-void and 56:88-94's rank-list where ashab-yamin get salam and the deniers get tasliya).
- FA-BI'SA AL-MASIR: the same settlement-word as A4 (4:115 wa-sa'at masiran) - the ma-sir-register again.

PARALLELS:
- Secret-counsel tripartite: 58:8s charged counsel vs 58:9s believer counsel (BIRR/TAQWA/gathered-to-Him) vs 58:10 the satan-sourced counsel. Collect as the SPEECH-ENVIRONMENT of the standing-state: the believers' SALLE-state speech answers by PUBLIC-CODES (cf. dhikr/vow/taqwa G-observance).
- THE ZIHA-CASE ROOT: 58:1-4 also flow ~THE SPOKEN-WORD RESTORATION: an error-stand made in words can be UNDONE with SIMILAR standing-acts ( freeing / fasting / feeding) LEST YOU BELIEVE. Standing is shown to be REPAIRABLE by corresponding acts - carries directly into C/D/E (s-bar-holiness system).
- THE COUNT-AND-FORGET (58:6 ahsa..hu wa nasu) - the fire-yaslawnha verdict is the RESULT of a COUNTED volume of speech - the standing-state is a DEBT-TIME record (cf. 36:65 tukallimuna aydeehim limbs-testifying - exact same counted-deeds universe).

FINDING: hasbuhum jahannam (sufficing-grammar) closes the last door on "fire as arbitrary burn": the fire SERVES as the person's own sufficer - the standing-state he gets is the one he kept by his own speech. God's avec-presence (58:7) is the host the mocker REFUSED; the fire-yaslawnha is the premanent standing-state of the chosen non-contact. Including speech records (58:6-8), the fire-standing is a REGISTERED standing-state - revolutionarily CONTINUOUS with 58:1-4's "standing-acts can repair word-stands" restoration grammar.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) hasb-family gathered: hasbuhum jahannam here vs hasbuna-LLah (3:173 et al.) - the sufficer of standing: GOD stands sufficing, the fire is the stand-in (final-doc table). (ii) Track the PROVIDING-SALAM register of the reward-side: ashab-yamin salam (56:91), salam minhu (33:44), "salim-li-abraham"... collect the salam-grammar - this is the DIVINE-GREETING counter-claim to the mock-greeting (58:8). (iii) The count-and-forget frame (58:6) matches 36:65 and 4:115 - COUNT register backs the standing-state across both poles.

VERDICT: studied - see A14 (69:31) next.

## A14 - 69:31 - thumma-l jahima salwahu

ARABIC (flow): 69:18 "on that Day you will be EXHIBITED - no hidden thing of yours remains hidden" -> 69:19-24 the right-hand-record scene: "READ MY BOOK (haumu iqru kitabiya) - I always BELIEVED I would meet my account" -> gardens and the call "eat and drink CONTENTEDLY for your forwarded days" -> 69:25-29 the left-hand-record scene: the lost wishes (wish I never received my book / knew my account / the FINAL-one (qadiya) had gone first) - "my wealth did not avail me; my AUTHORITY (sultani) perished" -> 69:30 THE ARREST COMMANDS: "TAKE HIM, so SHACKLE HIM (khuduhu fa-ghulluhu)" -> 69:31 "THEN THE JAHIM - HIS SALW (thumma-l jahima salwah)" -> 69:32 "THEN in a chain of seventy cubits - THREAD HIM THROUGH IT (fa-s-l-u-koo-hu)" -> 69:33-34 THE TWO FAULT LISTED: "he did not believe in God the Magnificent, nor did he URGE the feeding of the poor" -> 69:35 "so there is for him HERE TODAY no hamim" -> 69:36 "no food except ghislin" -> 69:37 "none eats it except the sinners."

WORD FORM: salwah - the BARE NOUN of the root (salw / salwah as the verbal-noun of Salla), in the phrase "al-jahima salwahu" - grammatical debate between TWO READINGS THAT AGREE IN MEANING: (a) masdar-upload: "then [into] the Jahim - his SALW" = "his deliver-standing"; (b) imperative read: "then - JAHIM: sallu-HU" = "roast him". The Quran stored BOTH readings as the SAME standing-assignment.

CONTEXT: MAKKAN (Surah 69, the Inevitable). The scene is DAY-DISPLAY: records read BY THE OWNER HIMSELF; posture categories (right hand / left hand owners); the arrested has just said "my wealth did not avail me; my authority perished" - and then the HAND-CLASS ACTING GIVES THE ARREST, SHACKLE, STANDING-ASSIGNMENT, CHAIN-THREADING PROCESSION.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- NOUN + COMMAND UNISON for ONE standing-state: "al-jahima salwahu" can be read NOUN or COMMAND and both yield the same SEMANTICS: deliver-into-the-located-standing-of-the-person. This is the A-cluster's deepest formal confirmation: the root-facts (A12 Tasliya NOUN / A9 A11 asluwha COMMAND / A5 A6 A10 A13 embedded-verb) now include THE BARE NOUN salwah (69:31) and the style shows the full FAMILY, all FORMANTS OPEN, all naming the ONE standing-assignment.
- THE PROCESSION VERBS AROUND the standing: khadhuhu (seize), ghulluhu (shackle), salwahu (deliver-stand), then silsilah + fa-SLuku (thread-through like a bead) - the S-L-W standing-state executes as a sequence of PLACING-ACT: seize / bind / situate-within / thread. A5-A6's "lodging" is thus performed stepwise: the person is INSTALLED into the fire as an object is installed in its PEDESTAL.
- FAULT LIST = BELIEF + SOCIAL CARE: 69:33-34 name the man's two disqualifications: (1) LA believes in God the Magnificent (the standing-avoided); (2) did NOT URGE feeding the poor - the social register of the standing-state - the believers salah-grammar E-cluster will confirm the same double-list at the positive side (e.g. surah 107's salah+feeds pairing; 23 muflahun's khashyat+zakat; 74:43-44 below).
- HAMIM FOLDED RIGHT AFTER: 69:35 "there is no hamim for him today HERE" - the same hospitality-word as A12's served-bill (nazl hamim + tasliyat jahim) - a TOTAL physicality: he has standing but NO HOSPITALITY except ghaslin (69:36-37 none tastes but the sinners).

PARALLELS:
- SULTAN (authority) and MAAL: the two possessions listed at 69:28-29 are exactly the two PROSTHETIC STATS the A-cluster thieves/deniers kept their trust with. The standing-state assigns human ranks on the ONE TRUE STAT (laws of the standing).
- PROCESSION-LANGUAGE: the fire-verbs cluster with ARRIVAL and THREADING verbs: 19:71 (warduha - every one of you ARRIVES at her), 69:32 (fa-slukuh - thread him through); the queue/arrival/threading frame of fire-language belongs to the same "installation" image-zone.
- The urge-to-feed parallel: HOLD 74:43-44 IN RESERVE (see A15): the pair "not of the praying (lam naku min-al musallin) / nor did we feed the poor (wa lam naku nut-imu-l miskeen)" EXACTLY mirrors 69:33-34 - the two-list is the same gate at both clusters.

FINDING: the bare noun SALWAH joins the root-self-noun (A12 Tasliya) to give the stand-ins full noun + command + verb coverage in fire-usage. no reading is independent; ALL readings agree: the fire-stand is an ASSIGNED-AND-MANUFACTURED standing within the reach of the place whose "authority chains" remain functional at the proper execution level. Quran wraps its fire-language in INSTALLATION + PROCESSION speak: seized, shackled, seated, threaded - the fire-side mirrors the standing-installation that the believer does WILLINGLY at the salah-SITE (musalla - C5) - chosen standing vs installed standing.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) TRACK the "urging-feed" fault across the fire-side (69:34) into E-cluster (74:43-44, 107, 23-grammar pairs) - the tests whether salah-stand collapses when social care stands at zero. (ii) 69:18-24 emphasize the record-scene: the OWNER HIMSELF read his own book - standing-with-refrained contact is ACCEPTED in the fire language (compare 69:25-26 - denial-words); keep as the SAME "personal record-read" as A9s limbs-testify and A13s counter-records. (iii) the SULTAN/MAAL dispossession pair (69:28-29) - ARRESTS of the prophets LISTED with a incomplete standing vs ty running aright - the "authority and wealth" code repeats in D-cluster (jumma 62) and cul-de-sacs.

VERDICT: studied - see A15 (74:26) next.

## A15 - 74:26 - sa-a-s-liyihi saqar

ARABIC (flow): 74:1-7 THE OPENING STANDING-PRESCRIPTION (six commands to the Prophet): "Arise and WARN (qum fa-anthir), and MAGNIFY your Lord (wa-rabbaka fa-kabbir), and PURIFY your garments (wa-thiyabaka fa-t-tahir), and FLEE the filth (wa-r-rujz fa-hjur), and do NOT give favors to out-count them (wa-la tamnun tastakthir), and be PATIENT for your Lord (wa-li-rabbika fa-s-bir)" -> 74:8-10 the blast and the Day hard on disbelievers -> 74:11 "LEAVE ME ALONE with the one I created ALONE (wahid)" -> 74:12-14 his provision-list: extending wealth, witnessing SONS, PREPARED PROCESSES made-READY for him (wa-mahhad-tu lahu t-m-h-i-d-a) -> 74:15 he WANTS MORE -> 74:16-17 "NO: he was STUBBORN against Our signs; I will BURDEN ENJOYING his step-by-step climbing (sa-ur-hiqu-hu sa-ud) -> 74:18-25 THE SIX-STEP SELF-STEERING DRAMA: "he THOUGHT and plotted... then PERVERTED how he plotted... then more... then he LOOKED... then he FROWNED and grimaced... then he TURNED AWAY and grew arrogant... then said: this is only magic handed-down / mere human speech" -> 74:26 "SA-A-S-L-I-YI-HI SAQAR" ("I WILL deliver-stand him into Saqar") -> 74:27-31 SAGA DESCRIPTION: "what will inform you what Saqar is - it LEAVES-NOT-AND-SPARES-NOT, a SCORCHER for the skin - over it NINETEEN GUARDS - and We appointed NOT the companions of the fire except ANGELS, and We did not apprise their number EXCEPT AS A TEST for disbelievers, that the people given the writ BE CERTAIN, that the believers INCREASE in faith, and none doubt - that those with disease in their hearts and disbelievers say: what does God intend BY THIS SIMILITUDE? - So God misleads whom He wills and guides whom He wills - NONE KNOW the armies of your Lord except Him - and it is NOT but a REMINDER for mankind" -> 74:32-35 oath block (moon / night retreating / morning disclosing): "INDEED IT IS ONE OF THE GREATEST THINGS (inna-ha la-ihda al-kubar), a warning to mankind" -> 74:36-37 "TO WHOEVER WILLS among you who would go Forward or Stay-behind" -> 74:38-40 each soul is its own pledge; the right-hand homes in gardens ASK the wrongdoers -> 74:41-42 "WHAT HAVE YOU SMUGGLED you into SAQAR?" -> 74:43-47 THE ANSWER (their own confession): "WE WERE NOT OF THE MU-SAL-L-I-N" (74:43!), "NOR DID WE FEED THE POOR" (74:44), "we wandered with the wanderers, we denied the Day of religion (Day of Recompense), UNTIL the certainty came" -> 74:48 so intercessors avail them not -> 74:49 drawn off from the warning.

WORD FORM: sa-a-S-L-I-YI-hi - FIRST-PERSON FUTURE of the root: the ONLY fire-verse so far where the acting agent is Gods singular "I" (sa-ASLIHI = "I will deliver-stand him"); compare A6/A5 multi-use of "We" ja'alna / yaslawnaha. And the name given - SAQAR - not Jahannam.

CONTEXT: MAKKAN (Surah 74 - The Cloaked One). The sura OPENS with a ramp of STANDING-COMMANDS to the Prophet; then hands the STANDING-ACCOUNT to one solitary proud man; the man runs a SIX-STEP planning drama; the standing-assignment follows; then the sura has the OATH-FEATURE that swears "this standing-charge is ONE OF THE GREAT THINGS"; then gives the person QUESTION-AND-RED against the Man-Free-CHOICE Grid; then the two-fault confession; the s-l-w family terms jaw the sura like runners.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- NEW PERSON-FORM IN FIRE-USAGE: sa-a-s-liyiihi "I WILL deliver-stand him into SAQAR" - the fire-stand is announced as GODS OWN FIRST-PERSON FUTURE, immediately after the man's six-step planning; the timing/authority sit alongside the A4 self-steering frame (tawwalla -> nusliehi). Sa-ud (74:17) vs the standing-assignment forms a direct STEP-UP/STEP-DOWN set.
- SAQARs POWER-DETAILS (74:27-31): Saqar is given AGENT-DETAILS (it does not leave-behind NOR spare: la yubqi wa la yadhari; a scorcher for the skin: lawwahatan lil bashar; a GUARD-COUNT of nineteen); the meta-verse assigns the guards a standing-identity - "We did NOT appoint the companions of the fire except ANGELS" - meaning: the fire is A RECRUITED POWER-SPACE WITH ITS OWN STANDING-HOUSEHOLDS (compare 69:30-32 A14's officer-process). THIS is the STRONGEST "fire = servant-power" text so far (matches A13 hasbuhum: fire serves as sufficer).
- THE NUMBER NINETEEN IS A STANDING-GATE: 74:31 declares God did not inform their number EXCEPT as a test (fitnah) for the disbelievers - so that the people given the writ GAIN CERTAINTY (yastayqin), the believers INCREASE in faith, and no doubt remains - and those with disease in their hearts ask "WHAT DID GOD INTEND BY THIS SIMILITUDE?" and the answer branches: "misleads whom He wills, guides whom He wills." The count of the fire-guards is thus itself a RECEIVING-GATE that sorts listeners - the standing-assignment tests people by how they take its signs.
- THE SURAH OPENS AND THE SURAH CLOSES IN STANDING-KEY: opening = 6 commands (arise/warn, magnify, purify, abandon, do-not-outcount-favors, patience); closing = the two-fault confession (not of the praying / not feeding the poor) plus denying the Day-of-Recompense. 74:4 (purify your garments) matches A12's 56:79 Purity-gate (mutahharun); 74:7 (patience) matches A11 (52:16) patience-disjunction inside the fire.
- THE THREADING PATH-WORD: 74:42 "ma SA-LA-KA-kum-fi SAQAR" (what conducted-running-tracked YOU into SAQAR) - from sa-la-ka (χο path/thread) - the SAME root as A14s fa-SLukuh (69:32) - the threading/thread-walk images SHARED between the two fire-passages (s-l-w word and s-l-k word TOGETHER, 69:31-32 & 74:26 / 74:42).
- m-i-h-d ROOT AGAIN: 74:14 (wa-mahhad-tu lahu t-m-h-i-d-a - "We made prepared-process for him") uses the SAME roots as A10's fabi-s al-MIHAD (38:56) - the person PREPARED his own running-room, and the fire hands the PREPARED-MEADOW (mihad) back to him - note: even the "settlement" wordings TURN around on the persons acts.

PARALLELS:
- TWO-SURAH BRIDGE, the SURAH-19 TO SURAH-74: within 74 the fire-ASSIGNMENT (74:26 sa-a-s-li-yi) and the NON-PRAYING CONFESSION (74:43 lam naku min al-Masaleen) SIT IN ONE SURA - the SECOND standing-bridge of the study (the first was Surah 19: 19:59 lost salah -> 19:70 saldo). Surah 74 even names the fire's guard-plan (74:30-31) CLOSE to the confession-verse - s-l-w standing and m-sl-n standing openly JOINT.
- THE ARROGANT DENIAL at the end of the six-step drama (74:23-25: turned away + "this is magic/human speech") parallels 36:61's covenant-castaway (A9: my denial/turn-away) - the standing-ERRORROOT is again the mouth.
- CLEAN RECORD STORY: right-hand persons ask gardens (74:38-40) - the same record-write-off of 56:88-94 three-class template (A12), with 74:41-42 added: the arranged QUESTION "what carried you in?" gives the WRONGDOERS a chance to state their reasons and the answer IS a standing-fault list.

FINDING: Surah 74 hands the fire-stand ON BOTH EDGES: literally as the PERSONAL-DEFINING OPENING-COMMAND tribute (arise/warn/magnify/purify/abandon/no-favor-charge/patience) and literally as a PERSONAL-DENYING closing confession (not of the praying / not of the feeding) - with Gods singular-future assignment (sa-a-s-li-yi-HI SAQAR) standing pop right in the middle. The fire-stand is never free-floating punishment; every time it comes ENVELOPISHED by the person's RULE-BREAKS. AND the fire itself is drawn in SERVANT-Power detail (guards, armies named, count-to-be-READ as a gate) - the service-side of the standing-state (A12s served-bill, A13s sufficer) now takes a POWER-SPACE with its own ANGEL-STANDING-FORCE.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) HOLD the two-list (74:43-44 not-praying/not-feeding-poor) TOGETHER WITH 69:33-34 for the E-cluster test. (ii) TRACK the s-l-k family (69:32 fa-slukuh, 74:42 ma-sala-kahum-fi) as the COMPANION-FORM of root-S-L-W standing (threading). (iii) mi-h-d root - 74:14 temhid vs 38:56 mihad - "the person prepared his room" gate. (iv) 74:31 ends "NONE KNOWS the armies of your Lord EXCEPT HIM - and it is NOTHING BUT A REMINDER TO MANKIND" - the fire-stand is declared a THING-TO-BE-TAKEN-SERIOUS BY MANKIND; hold when the D-cluster (life-institutions salah) deals with the standing-regulation.

VERDICT: studied - see A16 (82:15) next.

## A16 - 82:15 - yaslawnaha yawma-d din

ARABIC (flow): 82:1-4 four cosmic-undoing oaths (heaven CREAKS-apart, stars SCATTER, seas BURST, graves OVERTURN) -> 82:5 "the soul will KNOW what it forwarded and what it delayed" -> 82:6 "O MAN - WHAT HAS BEWITCHED YOU AGAINST your NOBLE LORD?" (ma gharaka bi-rabbika-l kareem) -> 82:7-8 the making-list: He CREATED you then PROPORTIONED you then BALANCED you (khalaqaka fa-sawwaka fa-adalaka), and ASSEMBLED you in whatever form He willed (fi ayyi sura ma shaa rakkabaka) -> 82:9 "NO - rather you DENY THE RELIGION/COUNT (innama tunaddib/l takdhibuna b-id-din)" -> 82:10-12 "and OVER YOU are KEEPING-GUARDS - noble WRITERS (kiranen katibin) - they KNOW WHAT YOU DO" -> 82:13 "the RIGHTEOUS are in DELIGHT (abrar / na'im)" -> 82:14 "the DEBAUCHEES are in JAHIM (fujjar / jahim)" -> 82:15 "THEY WILL BE S-L-W-ED INTO HER on the DAY OF THE RELIGION/COUNT (yaslawnaha yawma-d din)" -> 82:16 "and they are NOT LEAVING her in ABSENCE (wa ma hum anha bi-ghaibn)" -> 82:17-18 "and WHAT will inform you what the Day of Count AGAIN is? - and AGAIN, what will inform you what the Day of Count is?" -> 82:19 "a DAY NO SOUL OWNS FOR ANOTHER SOUL ANYTHING, AND THE COMMAND THAT DAY IS FOR GOD (al-amru yawmaidhin li-LLah)."

WORD FORM: yaslawnaha yawma-d din - the fire-VERB in its standard future-plural, pronoun to the fire (the A5/A10/A13 incorporation-set again), LOCKED IMMEDIATELY BY 82:16s DOUBLY-NEGATED ABSENCE-word (la GHAYBIN = there is no absence from her).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, the tiny 19-verse oath-sura. Structure: 4 oaths + self-knowledge ledger; 1 question aimed at Gods DECEIVING-illusion-base; the PERSON-ASSEMBLING list; the "denying the Count" indictment; the GUARD-writers declaration; the two-class standing-list (abrar vs fujjar); the s-l-w sentence, NEGATION of absence; the doubled question "what is the DAY-OF-COUNT?"; the GOD-owns-command finale.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE KEEPING-GUARD NETWORK (third pole-bound so far): the sura sets the RECORDERS (82:10-12 kiranen katibin, kez_KEEPING = keepers; they KNOW what you do) against the FINAL standing-classes; A15s Saqar is set within its GUARDING-ANGELS (74:30-31); this sura CROSSES the RECORDER-GUARD (82:10) and the Fire-Guard set (74:31) between two poles of the root; = THE GUARD-FIELD OF THE STANDING-STATE: the ONE persons acts are continuously LE written and held; the recipient-GUARDS EXECUTE the standing-assignment.
- NO-ABSENCE CLAUSE (82:16: la HUM AN-HA bi-GAYIVIN): the first CLEAN negative-side prohibition: no PERSON may ABSENT HIMSELF from the standing-assignment. Match A11s "with or without patience" and A14s SEVEN-APPROPRIs: the fire stands WITHOUT PAROLE OPTION; every other world-stand except the asked question-STANDING has no-such option. The root-DUALITY: standing-with-God = RECHARGING A VOLUNTARY STANDING; fire-standing = NON-VOLUNTARY standing without paroles (hunger-BUG-fix).
- THE DAY-OF-COUNT INNER-NAME SPLIT: 82:15 (s-l-w standing) uses YAWM AT-DIN; the SURA DOUBLED 82:17-18 the QUESTION "what REALLY is Day-of-Count?" - giving Day-of-Count the ROOTs THIRD body-state (standing-idea) along with s-l-w. And then 82:19 RESTORES the true property of the day: ALL possession-cut and GODS-command-monopoly - this is the standing-state where NO PLAYER-GAME works (no soul owns ANYTHING for another soul).

PARALLELS:
- THE DECEIVED-OPENING QUESTION: 82:6 "ma gharaka bi-rabbika-l kareem" - "WHAT HAS BEGUILED you CONCERNING your noble Lord?" - the man's illusion leaves his record untouched and instead blurs the LORD'S GENEROSITY into license; the Quran's nearness-claims (50:16 nearer than the jugular vein; 56:85 nearer but unseen) are exactly what the illusion collapses - the beguiled person trades standing-nearness for fantasy-license.
- THE ABSENCE-STAT (82:16) against the EVER-WORKING RECORDER-GUARD (82:10-11 hafidhin): BOTH SIDES of the standing-field are GUARDED - the person is written-about and held; the guards know what he does - THE STANDING-STATE IS THE STATE OF BEING HELD BY KEEPERS WHO WRITE THE REAL RECORD. Put A15's nineteen-guard field in one column and A16's recorder-guard in the other.
- THE ASSEMBLED-FORM: 82:8 (fi ayyi sura ma shaa rakkabaka) - the person is the ASSEMBLY of Gods chosen parts; the fire-stand would pick REFUSED-ASSEMBLY broken.

FINDING: the fire-verse y-sl-w-n-ha comes with a "no-absence" gate attached (82:16): the fire standing-state is NON-ABSENTIBLE - contrast to the God-side standing-field where "the person walks WITH the abode of the Name" (22:40, A8) and comes and goes by covenant; the fire 82:16 holds: standing-without-end, without paroles, without assembly-option. AND the record-keeping guards (kiranen katibin) make the standing-field a WRITTEN-BOTH-ways state: the DELIVERED standing is A CONTINUOUS-TIME standing - matches everything A1-A15 builds; plus A15's guard-angels now balanced by RECORDER-guard angels: angels BOTH WAYS serve the standing-state - honor or fire.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) PRE-SCAN hasirab KEEPING GUARD-WORDS: (a) 82:10-12 hafidhin + kiranen katibin; (b) 74:30-31 nineteen-angels of the fire stand (A15); (c) the B-cluster angels-under-salowat; hold them TOGETHER in the final-table. (ii) HOLD the pair-of-NO-absence-clause (82:16) - "not absent from the standing" - in the final form of the salah-Definition (compare B-cluster 2:157 and D-cluster salawat-of-the-day-cycle; salah = the fixed standing-time where ALL canstand KEPT). (iii) HOLD "yawm ad-din" - the Day-name used in the standing-verse; watch C/D/E verses for ad-din pairs with s-l-w.

VERDICT: studied - see A17 (84:12) next.

## A17 - 84:12 - wa-yusla sa-iran

ARABIC (flow): 84:1-5 the two cosmic-accord oaths (heaven SPLITS and HEARS permission to her Lord and is RIGHTLY-DUE; earth STRETCHES, disgorges her contents and EMPTIES, HEARS permission and is rightly-due) -> 84:6 "O MAN - you toil walking-TOWARD your Lord a toiling - and you will MEET HIM (kadihan ila rabbika kadihan fa-mulaqihi)" -> 84:7-9 the right-hand record: easy reckoning and RETURN to his people REJOICING -> 84:10-14 the record BEHIND HIS BACK (wara thahrihi): he calls for destruction (thuburan), "and wa-Y-U-S-L-A a BLAZING (sa-iran)". It is said WHY: "he was IN HIS PEOPLE joyful; he DEEMED he would never RETURN (lan yahur); NAY: his Lord was EVER SEEING of him (inna rabbaka bihi la-basir)" -> 84:16-19 the oath-cluster (twilight / the night and what it enfolds / the moon when made-full): "you shall ride-on STATE-AFTER-STATE (la-tarkabunna tabaqan an tabaq)" -> 84:20-21 "SO WHY DO THEY NOT BELIEVE - and when the QURAN IS RECITED to them THEY DO NOT PROSTRATE (la yaskudun)" -> 84:22-23 "rather the disbelievers DENY, and God knows best what they withhold" -> 84:24 "announce to them a PAINFUL punishment" -> 84:25 "EXCEPT those who believe and do the righteous-acts: for them a reward NOT WITHHELD (ajrun ghayru mamun)".

WORD FORM: wa-y-u-S-L-A sa-iran - THE ONLY FULLY-PASSIVE fire-verb so far: person = OBJECT, NO AGENT NAMED (agent implied = God); object = an INDEFINITE fire-name SA-IR (a blazing-place). The fire-lexicon now covers: jahannam / jahim / saqar / naar / naar jahannam / sa-iir / hamim / samum.

CONTEXT: MAKKAN - a brother-sura to 82 (A16): cosmic accord, then the meeting-question; but NEW: this one names the TOIL-STATE (kadah) of the persons striving and the MEET-promise; record-recipes; then the STAIRCASE-oath; then the SUJUD-GATE; then the exceptional clause.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- PASSIVE-SIDE OF THE ROOT: in A1-A16 the fire-verbs varied (future / divine-act / title / imperative) but the RECIPIENT was always a SUJBECT beside an act; here the person is ONLY the passive object: "wa-y-u-s-l-a sa-iran" - he WILL BE s-l-w-ed into a blazing - no agent given, no action-of-his frozen into the sentence: the standing-assignment is something DONE TO HIM. The verb itself stands as the whole sentence: the person STANDS because STOOD.
- THE MEET-WORD (84:6): "toil TOWARD your Lord a toiling - and you SHALL MEET HIM (fa-mulaqihi)" - the positive-pole of the root's standing-context opens as a MEET-CONTACT: the kadih-forward is a travel-TOWARD a person, not toward a heat. The fire-poles are the AVOIDED-MEET (84:11-14 he called for EXTINCTION - "thubur" - because the blazing-waiting kept MEETING-reality open).
- PAST-JOY VS FUTURE-BLAZING (84:13-15): "he was IN HIS PEOPLE joyful" (mashruran) - the FORM-ACTS pattern: the joy-appendence held his standing; "he DEEMED he would NEVER RETURN" - vs the God-claim AGAINST his fantasy: "his Lord was EVER SEEING of him (la-BASIR)" - the record-keeper seen at 82:10-12 (A16) and 74:30-31 (A15) is now titled with the SEE-ROOT: the STANDING-FIELD is ever-VISUAL watching: his keepers are SEERS.
- THE SUJUD-GATE (84:21): "when the QURAN is RECITED to them they DO NOT PROSTRATE" - in THE SAME SURA as the fire-verse, the standing-POSTURE (sujud) is the named REJECTED state of the same people; the negation runs the same jaw: "wa-y-u-s-l-a sa-iran" (they refuse STANDING-BEFORE-GOD AT THE READING) clothes THE SAME NON-BELIEVERS as the fire-stand-will-carry THEM out to the -s-l-w-ed standing; "they refuse SUJUD" and "they will be STOOD in fire" in ONE surah is ONE person-state with two mouths.
- THE OATH-CLIMB (84:19): "you shall RIDE UPON state-after-state (t-t-qa-an an-t-qaq)" - the person is a STAGE-MOUNTED, state-climbing nomad: his standing-state CHANGES; the toil-forward state (kadah) is the one ready to MEET; the rest stands on stage after stage.
- UN-WITHHELD REWARD (84:25): "a reward NOT WITHHELD (ajrun ghayru mamun)" - inverts A14's dispossession ("my wealth did not avail me") - the standing with God HOLDS NOTHING ON FILE; the fire-stand HOLDS EVERYTHING - settlement, mihad, maab, qaraar: THE HOLDING-VOCABULARY Belongs to the fire-pole; the God-pole names what it gives away freely and calls the reward UN-IMPRISONED.

PARALLELS:
- 69:19-21 & 84:7-9 (A14): both right-hand-record scenes use "joy + return to family" - hold as a paired record-receiving scene; 69 is the READ-OUT, 84 is the END-GIVING (the fire-verse tight-jaw the receiving jaws).
- THE SUJUD-GATE chapters: the Quran-reading prostration scenes: 7:206, 15:98, 17:107-109 (and 96 prostration-notes) - the Quran PROSTRATES AT ITS OWN READING: track to the D/E clusters under "establish salah" and see whether the Quran itself performs the standing-state.
- THE WEALTH-AND-RETURN-STRUCTURE: 84:14 "he deemed he would never return" matches A14s sultan-and-wealth (69:28-29); both are post-formulas: standing-refusal cloaks itself in (a) power-par (b) no-return-fantasy.

FINDING: the fire-branch now has its FULL verb-voice set: future / divine-We act / earned title / imperative / bare-noun (salwah) / and NOW PASSIVE (y-u-s-la). Where every other verse names an acting agent, A17 gives the person ONLY a passive receptor, and pairs the fire-verse with the SUJUD-REFUSAL in the same sura - two states of the same non-believers: REFUSING STANDING-BEFORE-GOD and BEING STOOD-BY-FIRE. THE ROOTS OWN INTERNAL RULE SURFACES: "ONE WHO WILL NOT STAND WILL BE STOOD" - s-l-w is a standing-state; you take it lawfully (stand, prostrate, meet) or you enter it standing-by-fire.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) Track the POSTURE-FAMILY of the root's positive side: sujud / qiyaam / mulaaqah / tawajjuh - the instrument-set of standing-with-God. (ii) HOLD 84:6 ("toil toward your Lord a toiling - and you will MEET HIM") as the positive anchor-sentence of the standing; test against the upcoming bridge verse A19 (87:15 fa-salli) - the only fire-cluster verse where the s-l-w verb runs toward GOD. (iii) HOLD the two-freedom register: 84:25 un-withheld reward vs the fire's hold-vocabulary (settled, mihad, maab, qaraar, sufficer).

VERDICT: studied - see A18 (87:12) next.

## A18 - 87:12 - yasla an-nara al-kubra

ARABIC (flow): 87:1 "GLORIFY-BY-NAME the Name of your Lord, the MOST-HIGH (sabbih isma rabbika-l A'la)" -> 87:2-5 the four MAKE-plays: "who CREATED then EQUALIZED/PROPORTIONED (khalaqa fa-sawwa), who MEASURED then GUIDED (qaddara fa-hada), who brought out the HERBAGE, then made it STUBBLE-BROWN (ghutha-an ahwa)" -> 87:6-7 "We WILL make you RECITE (sa-nuqri-uka), so NOT-you-FORGET - EXCEPT what Allah wills: for He KNOWS the published and the hidden" -> 87:8 "and We will MAKE-EASY you TO-THE-EASE (wa-n-yassaru-ka li-l-yusra)" -> 87:9 "so MENTION-WHOM-if-the-MENTION-helps (fa-dhakkir in-naf at-dhikra)" -> 87:10 "he who FEARS will MENTION (sa-yadhkur man yakhsha)" -> 87:11 "but the MOST-WRETCHED (al-ashqa) will AVOID-her (yatajannabu-ha)" -> 87:12 "the ONE-PERFORMING-S-L-W: THE-FIRE, THE-GREATEST (alladhi yasla an-nara al-kubra)" -> 87:13 "then he DIES-NOT-in-her and does-NOT-LIVE (thumma la yamutu fiha wa-la yahya)" -> 87:14 "QAD-AFLAHA (has-succeeded) the one who TA-ZA-K-K-A (self-purified)" -> 87:15 "AND-DHA-KA-R-A the NAME of HIS-LORD THEN-STAND-S-L-L-A (wa-dhakara isma rabbihil fa-sall - see A19)" -> 87:16-17 "BUT you PREFER the IMMEDIATE-LIFE; and the HEREAFTER is better AND more-ABLIDE" -> 87:18-19 "this is TRULY in the FIRST-SCROLLS - the SCROLLS of ABRAHAM and MOSES."

WORD FORM: y-a-S-L-A an-NAR AL-KUBRA - a PASSIVE reading (as in A17) is possible in the reciting tradition; the OTHER rendering makes it ACTIVE ("who s-l-w-s the greatest fire" - the enter-her idiom, as at 14:29/38:56/58:8). BOTH readings land in the SAME standing-state: the fire is the person's assigned standing-place. The fire-name is NAR AL-KUBRA - THE FIRE, THE-GREATEST: the fifth fire-word in the study's lexicon (jahannam / jahim / saqar / sa-iir / NAAR-al-KUBRA) and the only one with the superlative-marker "kubra".

CONTEXT: MAKKAN, Surah al-A'la (87), 19 verses. The s-l-w verses sit at the CENTER of the sura's architecture: 87:1 OPENS with the NAME-glorify; 87:2-5 the four MAKMakers; 87:6-8 the RECITE-then-EASE clause; 87:9-10 the MENTION-driver ("he who FEARS will mention"); 87:11-13 the AVOIDER and his L-standing (the s-l-w fire-cause) and his NEITHER-Life-NOR-DEATH status; 87:14-15 the SUCCESSFUL-triplet; 87:16-17 the preference-sentence (the immediate life / Hereafter best*); 87:18-19 the FIRST-SCROLLS declaration (Abraham/Moses) - a direct CHARGIN-FOR the C-cluster covenant-threads.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE PASSIVE-AND-ACTIVE-LAUNCH-HOST: whether the reading is passive (he will BE s-l-w-ed) or active "he s-l-w-s the fire" (the enter-her idiom - cf. A5), BOTH landing in the ONE position: WHO-S-OR-name (87:12-13) is not an ACCIDENTAL-death but a PERMANENT STANDING: A18 shows that the s-l-w-stuck standing is a STANDING-STATE (a condition, not an event): the fire is a standing-ASSIGNMENT and the person is there CONTINUOUSLY and no end-of-it is announced.
- THE NEITHER-LIFE-NOR-DEATH CLAUSE (87:13): "then he does-NOT-die in-her and does-NOT-live" - the s-l-w fire-standing is OUTSIDE the life/death axis: A STANDING-STATE-between: not a moment, not an exit; not fact-of-what-he-is but fact-of-where-he-stands. (Note: the SAME clause at 20:74 (A-confirmer?); check later.)
- THE MOST-WRETCHED WHO AVOIDED HER: al-ASHQA (87:11) - THE AVOIDED-reaching of A19 (92:15's fire-people: "la y-a-S-L-A-HA ila al-ASHQA" - same lexeme al-ashqan, y-a-s-l-a-ha!). The AVOID-ACT by itself lands in the s-l-w-stuck standing: avoidance -> the s-l-w-stand one-self gets.
- THE RECITE-GATE 87:6-7 AND THE EASE-GATE 87:8: before the standing-divide the sura has TWO gates: the RECITE-GATE (we will make-you-Recite, so NOT-you-forget, except-what-God-wills - and He knows the PAID and the HIDDEN) and the EASE-GATE (wa-N-YSSAR-UKA LI-L-YUSRA). The NAMED-Names (dhikr) form the front-lobe of standing-fault; THE B questionnaire-We-will-SAYS-SO (87:8): the standing-write against-fake-subject: "God-wait TO-THE-EASE" - the EASE-GATE against multi-PATH in s-l-w-state.
- THE STANDING-KEY-SENTENCE of positive-side (87:14-15): QAD-AFLAHA MAN-TAZAKKA / WA-DHA-KA-R-ISMA-RBHI-FA-S-ALL-A - the "GOODS-C climbing" command-pair: PURIFY-yourself + MENTION-the-Name-of-your-Lord + THEN-STAND (see A19 for the FA-SALLA-Bridge) - THE QURAN-LOCK triple-formula of the standing-$-study: this sentence HANDS-OVER to the C-cluster covenant-threads of ACCEPTED-selah ("establish-the-Selah + give-Zakat" is the CORE-PAIR.

PARALLELS:
- A19 (next) IS THE F-A-SALLA-BRIDGE: 87:12 (the s-l-w fire-cause) and 87:15 (fa-SALLA-of-the-God-cause) sit NINE verses apart - THE Y-JUST-ONE-SURA: both the fire-stand AND the God-stand verse in ONE sura - the HIGHEST-RANK TWO-FORM-PAIR of the whole A-cluster (compare the Surah-19 bridge and the Surah-74 bridge).
- A17 (84:12) wa-y-u-s-la SA-IR-AN vs A18 (87:12) y-u-s-l-a AL-KUBRA: TWO PASSIVE-OR-ACTIVE-s-l-w-verbs in CLOSE-PASSAGE: 87:12 = A17 with the superlative-crown ("the-GREATEST-fire"). The two verses togethr SHOW the two-FOLD the passivity and the STANDING-STATE.
- 87:16-17 (the IMMEDIATE-life preference) EXACTLY the A6's 17:18 AAAA-standing-Preference: y-U-R-I-D-AL-AJ-L-A (want-the-immediate) vs 87:16 TUATIH-NUN-AL-HAYAT-AD-DU-N-YA - the sent-stand-state is CHOOSE-PAIRS.

FINDING: Surah 87 delivers THE hinge of the A-cluster: 1 (sabbih-with-Name) opens with the NAME-Glorify; 87:14-15 delivers the STAND-THE-NAME as the SUCCESS-triplet (PURIFY the Name / MENTION-tHE-Name-of-your-Lord / THEN-STAND-with-the-Name); and 87:12-13 delivers the fire-side of the SAME standing-axis (THE-GREATEST-stand-name, PASSIVE-or-enter, NEITHER-life-nor-death state). ONE-SURA - ONE-THE-TRI-FORMULOUS-STATE; the FIRE-side and the GOD-side are the two poles of ONE STANDING-STATE - THE-STATE-NOT-AN-EVENT.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) A19s FA-SALLA must be studied as the BRIDGE-REMINDER (the s-l-w-stem ACTIVE-TOWARD-God) - hold it against A15's opening commands (Arise/warn/Magnify/Purify/abandon-filth/no-favor-counting/patience) and A12's 56:79 purity-gate: the conduct-profile of positive-standing is being assembled. (ii) HOLD 87:18-19 (the First-Scrolls of Abraham and Moses) as the entry-frame of the C-cluster (Abraham and Moses covenant-threads). (iii) check 20:74 (does the neither-dies-nor-lives formula recur?) during the D/E pass.

VERDICT: studied - see A19 (87:15) next.
