## A19 - 87:15 - wa-dhakara isma rabbihil fa-salla - THE BRIDGE VERB

ARABIC (flow): (same passage as A18) -> 87:14 "has-succeeded (qad aflaha) the one who PURIFIED HIMSELF (tazakka)" -> **87:15 "and MENTIONED the NAME of HIS LORD, THEN-STOOD (wa-dhakara isma rabbihil fa-sall)"** -> 87:16-17 "BUT you prefer the IMMEDIATE-LIFE; and the HEREAFTER is better and more-ENDURING" -> 87:18-19 "this is truly in the FIRST-SCROLLS: the Scrolls of ABRAHAM and MOSES."

WORD FORM: fa-SALLA (fa-sall) - the s-l-w stem BARE (no object: no naar, no jahannam, no jahim): for the FIRST time in the study the root appears object-free, because the object is implied by the preceding clause: "he mentioned the NAME of his Lord, THEN STOOD [toward it]" - the mentioned Name carries the standing. The ONLY object-free s-l-w verb-occurrence of the whole A-cluster.

CONTEXT: MAKKAN, Surah 87 - the success-triplet 87:14-15 (purify / mention-the-Name / stand) stands AGAINST the avoider-triplet 87:11-13 (avoids-her / s-l-w the greatest fire / dies-not-nor-lives). Then 87:16-17 make the choice-sentence (immediate life vs enduring Hereafter), and 87:18-19 declare the WHOLE formula ALREADY WRITTEN in the Scrolls of Abraham and Moses.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE BARE FORM: fa-SALLA is verb-without-object on the GOD-side. In every fire-verse the verb took a fire-phrase as object (yaslawnaha / yusla sa-iran / yasla an-nar al-kubra / nuselinehi jahannama). Here the human subject stands ACTIVELY, and the direction is fixed by what he just did: he MENTIONED THE NAME of his Lord. The stem does not change - THE DIRECTION-GIVER IS THE MENTIONED NAME: same stem, two directions (fire-object vs God-direction).
- THE THEn-SEQUENCE (fa- = "then/and-so"): purify (tazakka) -> mention-the-Name (dhakara isma rabbihi) -> THEN-STAND (fa-sall). The standing is not an isolated ritual act: it is the THIRD STEP of a chain whose first two steps prepare it. THE CAUSAL READ: the one who has purified and mentioned the Name THEN takes his standing. The success-triplet is ONE MOVEMENT in three phases.
- THE SURAH'S OWN MIRROR: 87:1 OPENED the sura with "glorify the NAME of your Lord the Most-High" (sabbih isma rabbika-l-'A'la) - an act OF worship directed UP; 87:15 gives the SAME Name-phrase wa-dhakara ISMA-RIBBIHI - the human version. The sura thus runs: God-command (glorify-the-Name) at the head, human-success (mention-the-Name + stand) in the middle, and the Scrolls-declaration at the tail: THE NAME-ARCHITECTURE frames the whole sura.
- THE SCROLL-CLAIM (87:18-19): the success-triplet is declared ALREADY-AUTHORED in the Scrolls of Abraham and Moses - the formula of standing was not NEW to Islam's community; it belongs to the covenant tradition - hold this as the C-cluster's entry-frame (Abraham/Isma'il covenant-threads, 2:125 Musalla).

PARALLELS:
- THE SURAH-87 PAIR is the HIGHEST-RANK two-form pair of the A-cluster: 87:12 (the fire-stand, passive-or-enter, GREATEST-fire) and 87:15 (the God-stand, human-active, object-free) NINE VERSES APART - the third surah-bridge (Surah 19, Surah 74, now Surah 87). A9-A11 promised a bridge verb; THIS is it.
- THE B-CLUSTER FORWARD: the whole B-cluster charges divine s-l-w TOWARD the receiver (2:157 salawat of Rabbi; 33:43 He-and-his-angels s-l-l-on-you to bring-you-out-of-darkness-into-light). The ORDER the A-cluster just showed: the person fa-SALLA (stands, A19) FIRST - the divine s-L-L-'ALAY-HI (God-stands-on-him) is the RESPONSE. Keep the standing-order table for B.
- THE CONTRACT WITH A17: fa-SALLA "he stood" (positive, self-taken) vs wa-Y-US-SA "he is stood" (A17, installed-by-force): the same stem-pair names the two directions of one standing-state - voluntarily-taken-standing vs imposed-standing.

FINDING: A19 IS THE STUDY'S HINGE: the root's positive act is TO STAND TOWARD THE MENTIONED NAME - preceded by purification, made possible by Name-mention, taken up by the HUMAN SUBJECT (fa-SALLA). The negative fire-side is the SAME standing-state delivered by force into fire when this standing is refused (A17: "one who will not stand will be stood"). The A-cluster thus CLOSES with its thesis intact: s-l-w = a standing-state within the reach of a power; TAKEN VOLUNTARILY toward the Name (fa-SALLA), or INSTALLED via the fire (y-u-s-la). The bridge to the B-cluster is open.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The B-cluster will develop the DIVINE s-l-w verbs (B3 33:43, B2 2:157); HOLD fa-SALLA as the FIRST human-positive act of the root - what man does first - and the divine counterpart ('alay-hi) as the response-form. (ii) HOLD the success-triplet (87:14-15: TA-ZA-K-KA / DHA-KA-RA-ISMA / FA-SALL) as the conduct-profile of the positive state - matching A15's 74:1-7 command-list and A12's 56:79 purity-gate: PURITY + NAME-MENTION + STANDING is now ATTESTED from three independent suras (56, 74, 87). (iii) The neither-dies-nor-lives formula: check 20:74 recurrence during later clusters.

VERDICT: studied - see A20 (88:4) next.

## A20 - 88:4 - tusla naran hamiya

ARABIC (flow): 88:1 "Has the OVERWHELMING (al-ghashiya) reached you?" -> 88:2-3 "Faces that Day HUMBLE - laboring, toiling" -> 88:4 "they WILL-BE-M-STOOD into a fire BLAZING (tusla naran hamiya)" -> 88:5 "made to drink from a SCALDING spring" -> 88:6-7 "no food but dari (bitter thorny), neither fattening nor satisfying hunger" -> 88:8-9 "OTHER faces that Day SOFT-RECLINING, pleased with their striving" -> 88:10-16 "in a garden HIGH: no nonsense heard in it; a SPRING-FLOWING in it; RAISED-BEDS; cups SET; cushions LINED; carpets SPREAD."

WORD FORM: tusla naran hamiya - FUTURE PASSIVE of form-II (salla): the person is OBJECT of the standing; indefinite fire-object. Third of the passive-set: A17 (84:12 yusla sa-iran), A18 (87:12 yasla an-nar al-kubra), A20 (88:4 tusla naran hamiya).

CONTEXT: MAKKAN (Surah 88, al-Ghashiya). The sura opens with one question, then TWO face-sets: laboring faces whose standing is the fire with scalding spring and bitter food; soft-reclining faces standing in a garden, furnished: raised beds, cups, cushions, carpets.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE PASSIVE TRIO (84 / 87 / 88): three suras in sequence give the fire-stand as near-passive - the person is entered into it; the fire SERVES him his drink (88:5). The fire-stand = served ENTRY into a furnished place.
- THE FURNISHING PAIR: the fire-side serves its bedding-list (A10's mihad; dari-thorns; scalding-spring); the God-side serves raised-beds, cups, cushions, flowing-spring (88:12-15). BOTH standing-places are FURNISHED; what differs is the quality of the furniture. Hold the pair for the final doc.
- THE COMPLETE BODY-LIST: each side supplies the same body-needs register: DRINK (88:5 vs 88:12), FOOD (88:6 vs the garden's fare), HEARING (no nonsense-speech heard 88:11), FURNITURE (beds-cushions). The standing-state is FULL-BODY in both directions - what the stood-one tastes, hears, drinks, sleeps on. Parallels A3 (skin - full contact), A12 (served bill: drink + roasting).
- 88:3 - the LABORER-list: "working, toiling" describes the face-holders: the fire-stand is the final outcome of a life that stood in DESIRE-labor; 88:8 "pleased with their striving" mirrors it on the God side: both sides KEEP the striving-word; what differs is the direction of the toil.

PARALLELS:
- A10 (38:56 mihad-bedding) vs 88:13-15 (raised-beds, cushions-rowed): the FURNITURE-OPPOSITE - a bed of HOLD vs a bed of REST.
- A12's served-bill (56:94 nazl hamim + tasliya) vs A20 (scalding-drink + bitter-food): the same SERVICE-Vocabulary both directions.
- 88:2 vs 88:8: humble-face vs soft-reclining-face - the FACE-register joins A16's 82:6 illusion-question ("what has beguiled you") - the FACE carries the standing (hold for the posture-family).

FINDING: A20 locks the fire-stand as a SERVED, FURNISHED, FULL-BODY standing-state: drink, food, hearing, furniture, taste, skin. Not a moment of punishment but a LIVING MODE administered by the fire as its own supply - the fire-stand is to be OCCUPIED whole. The God-side standing has the same body-register with inverted quality: the standing-state is total in both directions.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The passive-trio (84:12 / 87:12 / 88:4) is the clean definition-strip for the fire-stand. (ii) The COMPLETE-BODY table (drink / food / hearing / furniture / taste / skin) feeds the final doc; in it the D-cluster's washing-command (5:6) will join as preparing the body for standing. (iii) FURNITURE-table: fire-side (mihad, dari, scalding-spring) vs God-side (raised-beds, cups, cushions, flowing-spring).

VERDICT: studied - see A21 (92:15) next.

## A21 - 92:15 - la yasla-ha illa al-ashqa

ARABIC (flow): 92:1-4 the night-and-day oaths; the created pair (male and female); "your strivings are indeed DIFFERENT (inna saiyikum la shatta)" -> 92:5-7 the GIVER-path: "as for him who GIVES and FEARS, and AFFIRMS the-BEST (saddaqa bi-l-husna): We will smoothe his way TOWARD-EASE" -> 92:8-10 "as for him who is STINGY and counts-himself-sufficient, and DENIES the-BEST: We will smoothe his way TOWARD-DIFFICULTY" -> 92:11 "his wealth will NOT avail him when he falls" -> 92:12-13 "upon-US is the GUIDANCE; and the LATER and the EARLIER are OURS" -> 92:14 "so We HAVE-WARNED you of a fire BLAZING (naran talzza)" -> 92:15 "NO-ONE-s-l-w-s-HER except the MOST-WRETCHED (la yasla-ha illa-l-ashqa)" -> 92:16 "the ONE-who-DENIED-and-TURNED-AWAY (alladhi kadhdhaba wa tawalla)" -> 92:17 "and WILL-BE-KEPT-AWAY-FROM-HER the TAQWA-haver (wa sa-yujannabu-ha-l-atqa)" -> 92:18 "the ONE-who-GIVES-his-wealth-TO-CLEANSE-himself (yuti malahu yatazakka)" -> 92:19-20 "no favor is owed back to him; ONLY-seeking-the-FACE-of-his-Lord-the-MOST-HIGH" -> 92:21 "and he WILL-SOON-BE-PLEASED (la-sawfa yarda)."

WORD FORM: la yasla-ha illa-l-ashqa - the future verb in a NEGATIVE-EXCLUSION frame: "no one s-l-w-s her except the most wretched." The object-HA (fire) continues the incorporation-grammar of A5/A10/A13/A16. Subject-class = AL-ASHQA, the same lexeme as 87:11.

CONTEXT: MAKKAN, Surah 92 (al-Layl). The fire-verse sits inside a two-roads passage: the GIVER-road (gives - fears - affirms - gives wealth to purify) and the HOARDER-road (stingy - self-sufficient - denier - deny-and-turn-away). 92:14 formally warns of the blazing fire; 92:15 states WHO ENTERS it; 92:17 states who is KEPT AWAY from it - a full membership-grammar of the fire-stand.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE EXCLUSION-GRAMMAR: the fire-stand is a CONFINED class-membership ("no one ... except al-ashqa") - the fire does not collect randomly; it holds exactly one club. This inverts the common picture of judgment-as-undifferentiated-mercy: the fire-stand is a TITLE for a class.
- THE DOUBLE-VERB PAIR (92:16 kadhdhaba wa tawalla - denied-and-turned-away): the SAME pair as A4 (4:115 - followed-other-than-the-believers-course + nuwallihima-tawalla) and A13 (58:8 - secret-counsel mockery). The fire-stand always follows a DOUBLE movement: a speech-act (denial) plus a direction-act (turning-away). Enrollment in the fire-club is DOUBLE-ENDORSED by the person himself.
- THE KEPT-AWAY GRAMMAR (92:17 sa-yujannabu-ha-l-atqa - the TAQWA-haver WILL-BE-KEPT-AWAY from her): the away-from-fire is GOD'S ACT. Both directions of s-l-w-motion are God-held: the wretched are DELIVERED INTO her; the taqwa are KEPT AWAY from her; the same God stands over both keepings.
- THE GIVER-AND-THE-TRIPLET (92:18 yuti malahu yatazakka): giving wealth TO-CLEANSE-himself - the TA-ZA-KK-A word AGAIN (87:14 A19's first triplet-step: purification). So Surah 92 re-chains 87's formula: purify (92:18) -> seek-the-Face (92:20) -> stand-with-God (impl.); and the fire-side re-chains: stingy - denies - turned-away -> s-l-w-ed (92:15-16). THE TWO FORMULAS ARE STRAIGHT MIRRORS.
- THE NAME-AND-FACE ADDRESS: 92:20 "the FACE of his Lord the MOST-HIGH" parallels 87:1 "the NAME of your Lord the MOST-HIGH" (A18's opening). NAME (87) and FACE (92) are the two ADDRESS-objects of the standing on the God-side - hold as a pair.

PARALLELS:
- SISTER-SURAH 87: 87:11-12 (al-ashqa -> fire) vs 92:15-16 (al-ashqa -> fire via deny-and-turn): the same club in both suras, the double-act made explicit in 92. And 87:14-15's success-triplet answers 92:5-7's giver-path: two suras, one pair of formulas.
- 92:11 "his wealth will NOT avail him when he falls" - the wealth-thread AGAIN: A1 (4:10), A14 (69:28-29), A17 (84:14), A21 (92:11), A22 next (111:2) - the wealth-stand refuses to hold in the fire-side; hold as a unified A-cluster thread.
- 92:19 "no favor is owed back" - un-widowed reward (84:25) on the God-side; generosity without debt in both suras' endings.

FINDING: A21 completes the fire-stand's membership-grammar: EXCLUSIVE to one class (the most-wretched - the same club as A18's 87:11), entered-any-double-act (deny-AND-turn, 92:16), with the God-side holding BOTH keepings (deliver-into 92:15; keep-away 92:17). And the formula-mirror with 87 is now perfect: purify-mention-stand vs stingy-deny-turn-fire.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The TA-ZAKKA/GIVER-grammar across 87:14 and 92:18 - the purification-act is PART OF the standing-preparation (hold for C/E). (ii) NAME (87:1) and FACE (92:20) as the standing-addresses on the God-side - B-cluster will meet the divine-face-and-name again. (iii) The keep-two-ways grammar (92:17): deliver-INTO vs keep-AWAY - both are God's acts in the same root-zone.

VERDICT: studied - see A22 (111:3) next.

## A22 - 111:3 - say-sla naran dhat lahab

ARABIC (flow): 111:1 "PERISHED be the hands of ABU-LAHAB, and he PERISHED" -> 111:2 "his WEALTH did not avail him, nor what he EARNED" -> 111:3 "HE-WILL-BE-S-L-W-ED into a FIRE possessing FLAME (say-sla naran dhat lahab)" -> 111:4 "and his WIFE, the fuel-carrier" -> 111:5 "in her neck a rope of twisted fiber."

WORD FORM: say-sla - future passive (the fourth of the passive-set: A17 yusla, A18 yasla, A20 tusla, A22 say-sla); fire-phrase = a-FIRE-possessing-FLAME. THE PUN: ABU-LAHAB means "father of flame" - the kunya of the Prophet's uncle. The fire he will be stood into is named with his own name-substance: HE WILL STAND INSIDE THE FIRE OF HIS OWN NAME.

CONTEXT: MAKkan, Surah 111 (al-Masad) - five verses, the shortest fire-prophecy: hands and wealth condemned, fire-of-flame assigned, fuel-carrier wife, rope of fiber; no interpretive speech between - the verdict is delivered in one compressed block.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE NAME-TAG: the own title "father-of-flame" becomes the tag of the standing-place: he will be stood into a fire "possessing lahab" - his kunya turns into the fire's name. The fire-stand even carries NAME-HOLD: unlike money, the name does not drop; it becomes the fire's mark.
- THE HANDS AND THE FUEL: 111:1 condemns THE-HANDS; 111:4 makes his WIFE the fire's fuel-carrier; the couple becomes the fire's supply-line: hand-earned wealth is denied, and the fuel that powers the fire-stand is assigned to his household.
- WEALTH-NOT-PROFIT (111:2), fifth note in the A-cluster's wealth-thread: A1 (4:10 - swallowing wealth as fire in the belly), A14 (69:28-29), A17 (84:14 - the no-return fantasy), A21 (92:11), A22 (111:2). THE WEALTH-stand thread runs through the whole cluster, and here the wealth-end decisively FAILS: the one who could not stand with Truth stands alone with NO-wealth and NO-profit.

PARALLELS:
- A1-AND-A22: the cluster opened with one who swallowed fire inside his belly (the wealth he ate had fire inside), and closed with the hands that had gathered it forbidden; the wealth-stand fails both ways. The A-cluster is a closed circle around the wealth-fire relation.
- A19 (87:15) vs A22 (111:3): the God-side stand is a Name-stand ("he mentioned the Name of his Lord, then stood"); the fire-side stand here is ALSO a Name-stand - but the name is HIS OWN kunya. The standing is name-signed on both sides: one stands IN THE MENTIONED NAME OF GOD, the other in HIS OWN NAME-turned-fire.

FINDING: A22 closes the A-cluster with a signed name-worthy tag: the fire-stand is named with the one's own kunya - the name becomes part of the fire's substance. ALSO the wealth-thread closes here: what was swallowed in A1 returns un-available in A22.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) THE WEALTH-stand thread (A1, A14, A17, A21, A22) - hold for the final table. (ii) HOLD the Name-stand frame: A19's dhakara isma rabbihi fa-sall and A22's fire-of-LAHAB - the standing is name-signed in BOTH directions: one in the Mentioned Name of God, one in his own name-turned-fire-tag. (iii) The fuel-carrier (111:4) may join the A20 furniture-table: the fire-stand even has a supply-line worker - the fire's household.

VERDICT: A-cluster COMPLETE (22/22). Next cluster B-1 (24:41 - the creatures' s-l-w).

## B1 - 24:41 - kullun qad alima salatahu wa tasbihahu

ARABIC (flow): 24:35 the LIGHT-verse (God is the light of heavens and earth; like a niche with a lamp in glass, lit from a blessed olive-tree, "light upon light") -> 24:36 "in HOUSES God permitted to be raised, in which His NAME is MENTIONED; He is GLORIFIED in them morning and evening" -> 24:37-38 "men whom neither trade nor sale distracts from the REMEMBRANCE of God, from ESTABLISHING THE SALAH and from GIVING THE ZAKAH; who fear a Day when hearts and eyes will be overturned" -> 24:39-40 "the disbelievers' deeds are like a mirage... or like darkness in a deep sea" -> 24:41 "HAVE YOU NOT SEEN that God is GLORIFIED by all who are in the heavens and the earth, and the BIRDS with wings SPREAD? EACH ONE HAS KNOWN (alima) its SALAH and its TASBIH. And God is Knowing of what they do." -> 24:42 "To God belongs the dominion of heavens and earth, and to God is the DESTINATION (masir)." -> 24:43-44 clouds and rain and snow: "in that is a sign for those with sight." -> 24:45 "God created every creature from water..." -> 24:46 "We have sent down clear signs; God guides whom He wills to a straight path."

WORD FORM: ṣalātahu (noun, possessive: "its salah") paired with tasbīḥahu ("its tasbih") - the ROOT-NOUN in the divine-side's own form (the same noun that on the fire-side was Tasliya / Salwah, and on the devotion-side is Salah / Salawat / Musalla). The birds are said to KNOW their own ṣalāh (qad alima) - a knowing-by-creation, not by instruction.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah an-Nur (24). The verse sits between the LIGHT-verse (24:35) and the DOMINION/DESTINATION verse (24:42), with the HOUSES-OF-NAME-MENTION (24:36) and the ESTABLISHING-SALAH/ZAKAH men (24:37-38) just before it. The bird-verse is the bridge between the human establishment of salah and the universal glorification of God.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE FIRST DIVINE-SIDE VERSE and it OPENS with UNIVERSAL GLORIFICATION: "all who are in the heavens and the earth yusabbihu" - the birds are named as the specimen case. The ṣalāh is not a human-only ritual; it is a CREATED MODE shared with the whole creation. THIS IS THE STRONGEST "STATE NOT ACT" PROOF IN THE CORPUS: a bird does not "perform" salah as a discrete ritual; it IS in a ṣalāh as its created posture - the wing-spread (ṣāffāt) IS its standing-mode.
- ṢALĀH PAIRED WITH TASBĪH: the two together are the creature's standing-mode (ṣalāh) and its glorifying-mode (tasbīh). A12's Tasliya (kept-up roasting) and A19's fa-salla (kept-up standing toward the Name) both name a KEPT-UP STANDING-STATE; here the bird's ṣalāh is the KEPT-UP STANDING-POSTURE of a created being. The same stem, the same "kept-up" polarity.
- "EACH ONE HAS KNOWN ITS SALAH" (qad alima): knowledge-by-creation, not by study. The creature does not learn its ṣalāh; it was MADE knowing it. This matches the A-cluster's "state, not act" - the ṣalāh is innate to the creature's standing, like its breath. And God is "Knowing of what they do" (24:41b) - the ṣalāh and tasbīh are "doings" (yaf'alu) - continuous doings, not one-off acts.
- THE HOUSES-OF-NAME-MENTION (24:36) are the human mirror of the birds' ṣalāh: "houses God permitted to be raised, in which His Name is mentioned; He is glorified in them morning and evening." The Name-mention criterion of A8 (22:40) returns here: the human house-of-ṣalāh and the bird's ṣalāh are ONE architecture - a space assigned to standing-with-the-Name. The birds are the "no-building" version of the same site.
- THE WATER-LEXICON (24:43-45): clouds, rain, snow, "every creature created from water" - the A20 body-list (drink/food/furniture) now appears on the God-side: the bird's ṣalāh happens in a water-and-light universe. The standing-state is environmentally held.
- 24:42 "to God is the DESTINATION (masir)": the SAME maṣīr-word of A4 (4:115 "evil a maṣīr") and A13 (58:8 "evil a maṣīr") - but here it is the DESTINATION of the glorifying creature, not the fire-delivery. The maṣīr-word serves BOTH poles: settlement as damnation and settlement as destination. The standing-state's end is either the maṣīr of fire or the maṣīr of God.

PARALLELS:
- A8 (22:40 - the site-lexeme: houses of Name-mention for any community): 24:36 is the believer-side version of the SAME site-criterion. The bird's ṣalāh has no building but shares the Name-mention.
- A19 (87:15 - fa-salla toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward-the-Name parallels the bird's innate ṣalāh. The bird stands by creation; the human stands by choice (purify + mention + stand) - both are the same stem in the same direction.
- A2 (4:30 - "to Us is the return") and B1's "to God is the destination" (24:42): the same maṣīr-word closes both the fire-delivery (A2) and the universal-glorification (B1). The standing-state's end is determined by direction, not by the stem.

FINDING: B1 opens the divine-side with the STRONGEST "state not act" proof: the birds KNOW their ṣalāh by creation, not by ritual instruction. The ṣalāh is a CREATED POSTURE - a standing-state that the creature IS, not a ceremony it performs. The same stem that on the fire-side named "lodged-into-fire" and on the human-side named "standing-toward-the-Mentioned-Name" here names the INNATE STANDING-MODE of every creature: the root is a STATE of being-in-the-reach-of-God, and the bird's wing-spread is its natural form of it.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The bird-ṣalāh as "created posture" - hold as the ANCHOR for the divine-side's "state, not act" reading; test against 33:43 (B3 - He and His angels s-l-l on you) and 33:56 (B4 - they s-l-l on the Prophet). (ii) The maṣīr-destination pair: fire-destination (A4/A13) vs God-destination (B1) - the SAME word, opposite directions. (iii) The houses-of-Name-mention (24:36) parallel to 22:40 (A8) - the human site and the bird site share ONE criterion. (iv) The water-lexicon (24:43-45) joins A20's body-list on the God-side.

VERDICT: studied - see B2 (2:157) next.

## B2 - 2:157 - olai'ka alayhim salawatun min rabbihim wa rahmatun

ARABIC (flow): 2:151 "as We sent to you a Messenger from among you who recites to you Our signs and PURIFIES you and teaches you the Book and the wisdom and teaches you what you did not know" -> 2:152 "so REMEMBER Me, I will remember you; be grateful to Me, and do not deny" -> 2:153 "O you who believe, seek help in PATIENCE and the SALAH; God is with the patient" -> 2:154 "do not say of those killed in the path of God: dead; rather they are alive, but you do not perceive" -> 2:155 "We will test you with something of fear, hunger, loss of wealth, lives and fruits; and give good news to the patient" -> 2:156 "who, when struck by a calamity, say: we belong to God and to Him we return" -> 2:157 "THOSE - upon them are SALAWAT from their Lord and MERCY; and THOSE are the GUIDED" -> 2:158 "Indeed Safa and Marwa are among the symbols of God..." -> 2:159-160 "those who conceal what We sent down... cursed; except those who repent and repair and make clear - those I turn to" -> 2:161-162 "those who disbelieve and die as disbelievers - upon them is the CURSE of God and the angels and all the people; abiding therein, the punishment is not lightened" -> 2:163 "your God is one God... the Most-Merciful, the Most-Compassionate."

WORD FORM: ṢALAWĀT (plural of ṣalāh) - the DIVINE-side plural form: "upon them are ṢALAWĀT from their Lord and MERCY." The plural = renewed cycles of God's standing-toward them (matches A12's note: "ṣalawāt (plural) = its renewed cycles"). The preposition ʿALAY (upon/over) = the direction of the divine standing toward the human.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Baqara (2). The verse sits in the PATIENCE-TESTING passage: believers are tested with fear, hunger, loss; the patient who confess "we belong to God and to Him we return" receive God's ṢALAWĀT and MERCY. The Messenger (2:151) recites, purifies, teaches - the human agent of the chain. Then 2:158 names Safa and Marwa as symbols of God (the human standing-site at the House).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE FIRST DIVINE ṢALĀH-VERSE and it establishes the FULL PATTERN: God's ṢALAWĀT are GRANTED (not imposed) to people who have been TESTED and who CONFESS THEIR RETURN ("we belong to God and to Him we return" - 2:156). The human ṢALĀH (standing - 2:153 "seek help in patience and the ṣalāh") is the human act; the divine ṢALAWĀT (2:157) is God's response. The SAME stem, two directions, two agents - exactly the A19 bridge: fa-ṣallā (human stands) -> divine s-l-l-'alay (God stands on him).
- ṢALAWĀT PAIRED WITH RAḤMAH (mercy): God's ṢALAWĀT is a form of divine MERCY, not a punishment. The fire-side ṣalāh was imposed (DELIVERY pole); the God-side ṢALAWĀT is granted (STATE pole, willingly received). The same stem, opposite polarity - granted vs imposed.
- THE RETURN-GRAMMAR (2:156 "we belong to God and to Him we return" - inna lillahi wa-inna ilayhi raji'un): the people of God's ṢALAWĀT are those who CONFESS they return to Him. This is the OPPOSITE of the fire-side: the fire-people "deemed they would never return" (84:14 A17). The God-side CONFESSES return; the fire-side DENIES return. The same "return" (raja'a) word, two stances.
- THE ʿALAY PREPOSITION (upon/over): the ṢALAWĀT are "upon them" (ʿalayhim) - the same preposition that B3 (33:43 "He s-l-l on you" ʿalaykum) and B4 (33:56 "they s-l-l on the Prophet" ʿalā) will use. The ʿalay = the DIRECTION of the divine standing toward the human - God stands OVER/TOWARD them.
- 2:161 "upon them is the CURSE (la'nah) of God and the angels" - the NEGATIVE of the ṢALAWĀT: those who die as disbelievers get la'nah (curse) instead of ṢALAWĀT (God's standing-toward). The ṢALAWĀT vs la'nah polarity mirrors the A-cluster's honor-economy: madhmum/madhur vs divine honoring (A6). God STANDS-TOWARD the patient (ṢALAWĀT) and CURSES the deniers (la'nah).
- 2:153 "seek help in patience and the ṣalāh" - the HUMAN act (the noun-form, the standing-act) is the believer's tool; 2:157's ṢALAWĀT is God's RESPONSE. The human ṣalāh is the request; the divine ṢALAWĀT is the answer. This is the EXACT chain A19 promised: fa-ṣallā (human stands) -> divine s-l-l-'alay (God stands on him).
- 2:151 - the Messenger recites, purifies, teaches the Book and wisdom: the HUMAN AGENT of the chain. This is 87:14-15's success-triplet (purify + mention + stand) in PROPHETIC form: the Prophet recites (mention), purifies (tazakka), teaches (the Book). The ṢALAWĀT-chain has a human agent.
- 2:158 - Safa and Marwa as symbols of God: the human STANDING-SITE at the House (the muṣallā of 2:125, C-cluster). The divine ṢALAWĀT (2:157) and the human standing-site (2:158) are ADJACENT - the human site and the divine ṢALAWĀT belong to the same passage.
- The plural ṢALAWĀT (not singular ṣalāh): God's ṢALAWĀT are PLURAL - renewed cycles. On the fire-side, ṢALAWĀT was used of places (22:40 A8) and of God's blessings; here it is God's STANDING-TOWARD the patient in repeated cycles. The plural = sustained, renewed standing - matching A12's Tasliya (kept-up roasting) and A19's fa-salla (kept-up standing toward the Name).

PARALLELS:
- A19 (87:15 - fa-salla toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward-the-Name is answered by God's ṢALAWĀT (2:157). The chain: human fa-ṣallā -> divine ṢALAWĀT. This is the FIRST confirmation of the A19 bridge on the divine side.
- A6 (17:18 - madhmuman madhuran, the expelled honor-void): the God-side ṢALAWĀT is the positive of A6's expelled states: ṢALAWĀT = honored-standing; madhmum/madhur = expelled-standing. Same stem, same honor-economy.
- B1 (24:41 - the birds know their ṣalāh): the bird's innate ṣalāh (created posture) parallels the human ṣalāh (2:153, the standing-act the believer seeks help in). Both are the same stem: the bird's innate standing and the human's chosen standing.
- A8 (22:40 - houses of Name-mention): 2:158's Safa/Marwa are the human standing-site; the divine ṢALAWĀT (2:157) is granted to those who stand at the site. The site and the ṢALAWĀT are adjacent.

FINDING: B2 is the FIRST confirmation of the A19 bridge on the divine side: the human ṣalāh (2:153, "seek help in patience and the ṣalāh") is answered by the divine ṢALAWĀT (2:157, "upon them are ṢALAWĀT from their Lord"). The SAME stem, two agents, two directions: human stands (fa-ṣallā) -> God stands on (ṢALAWĀT ʿalay). The ṢALAWĀT is GRANTED (not imposed) to those who confess their return to God ("we belong to God and to Him we return" - 2:156), and it is paired with MERCY (raḥmah). The fire-side imposed the standing; the God-side GRANTS it as mercy. The root's polarity is now fully visible: imposed-standing (fire) vs granted-standing (God's ṢALAWĀT).

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The ʿalay preposition (upon/over) - track all B-cluster ṣalāh-on verses: 2:157 (ʿalayhim), 33:43 (ʿalaykum), 33:56 (ʿalā). The ʿalay = the direction of the divine standing toward the human. (ii) The la'nah vs ṢALAWĀT polarity (2:161 vs 2:157): curse vs standing-toward - the honor-economy's two ends. (iii) The return-grammar (2:156 "we belong to God and to Him we return") vs 84:14 ("he deemed he would never return") - the God-side confesses return; the fire-side denies it. (iv) The Messenger as agent (2:151) - the success-triplet in prophetic form. (v) The Safa/Marwa site (2:158) - hold for C-cluster (2:125 Musalla).

VERDICT: studied - see B3 (33:43) next.

## B3 - 33:43 - huwa alladhi yusalli alaykum wa mala'ikatuhu

ARABIC (flow): 33:35 the long believer-list (muslim, mu'min, qanit, sadiq, sabir, khashi', mutasaddiq, sa'im, hafiz furuj, dhakir Allah kathiran) - "God has prepared for them forgiveness and a great reward" -> 33:36 "it is not for a believing man or woman, when God and His Messenger have decided a matter, to have any choice in their affair" -> 33:37 the Zayd/Zaynab story (marriage of the Prophet to Zaynab, so that there be no difficulty for the believers regarding the wives of their adopted sons) -> 33:38 "there is no difficulty upon the Prophet in what God has made lawful for him - the sunnah of God among those who passed before" -> 33:39 "those who convey the messages of God and fear Him and fear no one but God; and God suffices as a reckoner" -> 33:40 "Muhammad is not the father of any of your men, but the Messenger of God and the seal of the prophets" -> 33:41 "O you who believe, REMEMBER God with MUCH remembrance" -> 33:42 "and GLORIFY Him morning and evening" -> 33:43 "HE IS THE ONE WHO S-L-L ON YOU, AND HIS ANGELS, THAT HE MAY BRING YOU OUT OF DARKNESS INTO LIGHT; and He has been Merciful to the believers" -> 33:44 "their greeting on the Day they meet Him is SALAM; and He has prepared for them a generous reward" -> 33:45 "O Prophet, We have sent you as a witness, a bringer of good news, a warner" -> 33:46 "and a caller to God by His permission, and a lamp giving light" -> 33:47-48 "give good news to the believers... and do not obey the disbelievers and the hypocrites."

WORD FORM: yu-ṣallī ʿalaykum (He s-l-l-s upon you) with co-agent "and His angels" (wa malāʾikatuhu) - the DIVINE ṢALĀH with ANGELS as co-agents. The purpose-clause "li-yukhrijakum min al-ẓulumāti ilā al-nūr" (that He may bring you out of darkness into light) defines the ṢALĀH as an EXODUS-act. The ʿalay (upon/over) = the direction of the divine standing toward the human.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Ahzab (33). The verse sits between the human-side commands (33:41-42 "remember God with much remembrance, glorify Him morning and evening") and the greeting-verse (33:44 "their greeting on the Day they meet Him is Salam"). The ṢALĀH is the divine response to the human dhikr/tasbīḥ.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ANGELIC POLE OF THE DIVINE SIDE: God's ṢALĀH is here accompanied by His ANGELS' ṢALĀH - "He s-l-l-s on you, and His angels." This is the FIRST verse to name the angels as co-agents of the divine ṢALĀH. The A-cluster's fire-side had angels as GUARDS (74:30-31, the nineteen of Saqar) and B1's birds had their own ṣalāh; now the angels are co-agents of God's ṢALĀH - the angelic pole serves BOTH sides: fire-guard and God-ṢALĀH.
- THE EXODUS-CLAUSE (li-yukhrijakum min al-ẓulumāti ilā al-nūr): the divine ṢALĀH BRINGS PEOPLE OUT OF DARKNESS INTO LIGHT. This is the OPPOSITE of the fire-stand: the fire-ṣalāh DELIVERS INTO DARKNESS (fire); the divine-ṢALĀH BRINGS OUT OF DARKNESS. Same stem, opposite direction: fire = brought-into-darkness; divine = brought-out-of-darkness. The ṢALĀH is an EXIT, not an entry.
- ṢALĀH PAIRED WITH RAḤMAH (33:43b "He has been Merciful to the believers"): the divine ṢALĀH is a form of divine MERCY - same pairing as B2 (2:157 ṢALAWĀT + raḥmah). The ṢALĀH-mercy pair is stable on the God-side.
- THE HUMAN-SIDE COMMANDS (33:41-42 "remember God with much remembrance; glorify Him morning and evening"): the human dhikr and tasbīḥ PRECEDE the divine ṢALĀH. The human acts (dhikr, tasbīḥ) INVITE the divine ṢALĀH - the same chain as 2:153 (human ṣalāh) -> 2:157 (divine ṢALAWĀT), and 87:15 (human fa-ṣallā) -> divine response. The ṢALĀH-chain always runs: human act FIRST, divine ṢALĀH as RESPONSE.
- 33:44 "their greeting on the Day they meet Him is SALĀM": the SALĀM-greeting (A12/A13's tracked reward-side greeting) is the OUTCOME of the divine ṢALĀH: the exodus (darkness -> light) ends in SALĀM-greeting. The ṢALĀH brings them to a state where their greeting is SALĀM - not the mock-greeting of 58:8 (hayyūka bi-mā lam yahyika bihi-llāh) but the REAL divine greeting.
- 33:35's long believer-list: the QUALITIES of those who receive the divine ṢALĀH: muslim/mu'min/qanit/sadiq/sabir/khashi'/mutasaddiq/sa'im/hafiz furuj/dhakir. The ṢALĀH is granted to those who already LIVE these qualities - the divine response to a standing-life. The ṢALĀH does not create the qualities; it RESPONDS to them.
- 33:40 "seal of the prophets": the ṢALĀH is given in the context of the prophetic seal - the divine ṢALĀH is given to the community of the final prophet, through the prophetic channel.

PARALLELS:
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): the SAME pattern - ṢALĀH/ṢALAWĀT + raḥmah + granted to the qualified. B3 adds the ANGELS as co-agents and the EXODUS-clause.
- A19 (87:15 - fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward-the-Name is answered by God's ṢALĀH (B3). The chain: human fa-ṣallā -> divine ṢALĀH ʿalay. B3 is the SECOND confirmation of the A19 bridge (B2 was the first).
- A13 (58:8 - the mock-greeting): 33:44's REAL Salam-greeting is the positive counterpart of A13's false-greeting. The divine ṢALĀH produces REAL greeting; the fire-stand produces MOCK greeting. The ṢALĀH-greeting pair mirrors the fire-stand's mock-greeting.
- A6 (17:18 - madhmuman madhuran, the expelled): the divine ṢALĀH (bringing out of darkness) is the positive of A6's expelled states. Same stem: fire delivers INTO darkness; divine ṢALĀH delivers OUT of darkness.
- B1 (24:41 - the birds know their ṣalāh): the bird's innate ṣalāh (created posture) parallels the believer's qualified ṢALĀH (33:35's quality-list). Both are the same stem: the bird's innate standing and the believer's qualified standing.

FINDING: B3 is the SECOND confirmation of the A19 bridge and the FIRST to name the ANGELS as co-agents: God's ṢALĀH (with His angels) BRINGS PEOPLE OUT OF DARKNESS INTO LIGHT - the EXODUS-act. The divine ṢALĀH is the OPPOSITE of the fire-stand: fire DELIVERS INTO darkness; divine ṢALĀH DELIVERS OUT OF darkness. The ṢALĀH-chain is now fully visible: human act (dhikr/tasbīḥ/ṣalāh) -> divine ṢALĀH (with angels) -> exodus (darkness->light) -> SALĀM-greeting (33:44). The same stem, two directions: imposed-standing (fire) vs exodus-standing (God's ṢALĀH). And the ṢALĀH is granted to those who already LIVE the qualities (33:35's list) - it RESPONDS to a standing-life, it does not create it.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The angelic pole: angels serve BOTH sides - fire-guard (74:30-31, A15) and God-ṢALĀH (33:43, B3). HOLD the angelic-table for the final doc. (ii) The exodus-clause (darkness -> light) - the OPPOSITE of fire-delivery (into darkness). HOLD as the polarity-pair: fire = brought-into-darkness; divine ṢALĀH = brought-out-of-darkness. (iii) The SALĀM-greeting (33:44) as the OUTCOME of the divine ṢALĀH - the genuine greeting vs the mock-greeting (58:8, A13). (iv) The human-side commands (dhikr/tasbīḥ 33:41-42) as the INVITATION to the divine ṢALĀH - the chain pattern. (v) The quality-list (33:35) - the ṢALĀH is granted to those who already live the qualities.

VERDICT: studied - see B4 (33:56) next.

---

## B4 - 33:56 - inna allaha wa mala'ikatuhu yusalluna ala-n-nabiyyi

ARABIC (flow): 33:50-52 the lawful-wives rules for the Prophet (lawful wives, choice, no exchange) -> 33:53 the etiquette of entering the Prophet's house, the hijab, "do not marry his wives after him, ever" -> 33:54 "whether you reveal a thing or conceal it, God is Knowing of all things" -> 33:55 "no blame on them regarding their fathers, sons, brothers... and fear God" -> 33:56 "INDEED GOD AND HIS ANGELS S-L-L ON THE PROPHET. O YOU WHO BELIEVE, S-L-L ON HIM AND SURRENDER A SURRENDERING (ṣallū ʿalayhi wa sallimū taslīman)" -> 33:57 "those who harm God and His Messenger - God has cursed them in this world and the next, and prepared for them a humiliating punishment" -> 33:58 "those who harm the believing men and women without their having earned it..." -> 33:59 "O Prophet, tell your wives, your daughters, and the women of the believers to draw their cloaks over themselves..." -> 33:60-62 the hypocrites, the sunnah of God.

WORD FORM: yu-ṣallūna ʿalā al-nabiyyi (God and His angels s-l-l on the Prophet) with co-agent "and His angels" (wa malāʾikatuhu) - the SAME formulation as B3 (33:43) but the OBJECT here is THE PROPHET, not the believers. And the IMPERATIVE ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI ("s-l-l on him") with SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN ("surrender a surrendering") - the FIRST human-directed imperative of the s-l-w stem.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Ahzab (33). The verse sits in the Prophet's household-protection passage (33:53-59): the Prophet's wives are protected, and the s-l-l command on the Prophet is given in THAT protective context. 33:57 immediately adds: those who harm God and His Messenger are CURSED.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- TWO OBJECTS OF DIVINE ṢALĀH: B3 (33:43) - God s-l-l on the BELIEVERS (with angels); B4 (33:56) - God s-l-l on the PROPHET (with angels). The divine ṢALĀH has TWO recipients: the community (B3) and the Prophet (B4). The same stem, same co-agent (angels), same preposition (ʿalā). The Prophet is the human agent (2:151) of the chain; the believers are the community that receives the chain.
- THE IMPERATIVE FORM (ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI): for the first time in the study, humans are COMMANDED to s-l-l on someone (the Prophet). The command is the HUMAN RESPONSE to the divine ṢALĀH: God s-l-l on the Prophet (first clause) -> humans are commanded to s-l-l on the Prophet (second clause). The chain: divine ṢALĀH first, human s-l-l as response. The SAME pattern as 2:153 (human ṣalāh) -> 2:157 (divine ṢALAWĀT) and 87:15 (human fa-ṣallā) -> divine response.
- THE S-L-W + S-L-M PAIRING (ṣallū ʿalayhi wa sallimū taslīman): the s-l-w stem (stand-toward) is paired with the s-l-m stem (surrender/peace). The two stems are COUSINS (both s-l-...): s-l-w (standing-toward) + s-l-m (surrendering). The command is: stand-toward him (ṣallū ʿalayhi) AND surrender (sallimū taslīman). The surrender is the QUALITY of the standing-toward: you stand-toward the Prophet WITH surrender. The same pairing appears in B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT + raḥmah/mercy) and B3 (33:43 - ṢALĀH + raḥmah/mercy) - the s-l-w is always qualified by a soft-quality (mercy / surrender / peace).
- 33:57 "those who harm God and His Messenger - God has CURSED them" - the NEGATIVE of the ṢALĀH: harming the Prophet = being CURSED (la'nah). The ṢALĀH (God standing-toward the Prophet) vs la'nah (God cursing the harmers). The honor-economy again: ṢALĀH = honor; la'nah = dishonor. Same as 2:161 (la'nah on disbelievers vs ṢALAWĀT on the patient).
- 33:44 (B3) "their greeting on the Day they meet Him is SALĀM" - the SALĀM-greeting is the OUTCOME of the divine ṢALĀH; 33:56 commands SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN (surrender a surrendering) - the human act of surrendering is the earthly counterpart of the eschatological SALĀM-greeting. The s-l-m stem bridges the earthly command and the eschatological outcome.
- 33:53 the hijab/etiquette verses: the Prophet's household is protected, and the ṢALĀH command comes in that protective context. The ṢALĀH on the Prophet is connected to RESPECTING his household and person - the standing-toward the Prophet includes protecting his privacy.
- 33:59-60 the hypocrites: "tell your wives, your daughters, and the women of the believers to draw their cloaks over themselves... if the hypocrites do not cease, We will stir you against them." The ṢALĀH on the Prophet is given in the context of hypocrite-threat - the standing-toward the Prophet includes standing-against those who harm him.

PARALLELS:
- B3 (33:43 - God s-l-l on believers + angels, exodus darkness->light): B4 is the SAME formulation with a DIFFERENT object (Prophet instead of believers) and an ADDED human command (ṣallū ʿalayhi wa sallimū). B3 = divine-only; B4 = divine + human-command. The divine ṢALĀH comes first; the human s-l-l is commanded in response.
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): the ṢALĀH/ṢALAWĀT + mercy pairing is stable across B2-B3-B4. The ṢALĀH is always qualified by a soft-quality (raḥmah/mercy, taslīm/surrender, SALĀM/peace).
- A19 (87:15 - fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward is answered by the divine ṢALĀH. B4's ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI is the human command to stand-toward the Prophet - the same stem, the same direction (toward), but the object is the Prophet (the human agent of the chain) rather than the Mentioned Name. The standing-toward-the-Prophet is the earthly form of standing-toward-the-Name.
- A13 (58:8 - the mock-greeting): 33:56's SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN (surrender a surrendering) is the REAL counterpart of A13's mock-greeting. The divine ṢALĀH produces real surrender; the fire-stand produces mock-greeting. The s-l-m stem bridges: real surrender (33:56) vs mock greeting (58:8).
- A6 (17:18 - madhmum/madhur): the divine ṢALĀH on the Prophet (33:56) is the positive of A6's expelled states. The honor-economy: ṢALĀH = honor; la'nah (33:57) = dishonor.

FINDING: B4 is the COMMAND-verse of the divine-side: humans are commanded to ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI (stand-toward the Prophet) AND SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN (surrender). The ṢALĀH-chain now has a HUMAN COMMAND in it: divine ṢALĀH on the Prophet (first clause) -> human command to ṢALLŪ on him (second clause) -> human surrender (taslīm). The s-l-w stem is paired with the s-l-m stem: standing-toward + surrendering. The surrender is the QUALITY of the standing-toward. And the NEGATIVE is named: those who harm the Prophet are CURSED (la'nah) - the ṢALĀH (honor) vs la'nah (dishonor) polarity. The divine-side's ṢALĀH is not just granted (B2-B3); it is COMMANDED (B4) - the human is told to take up the same standing-toward that God and His angels already perform.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The s-l-w + s-l-m pairing (ṣallū ʿalayhi wa sallimū taslīman): standing-toward + surrendering - the s-l-m stem as the QUALITY of the s-l-w act. HOLD for C-cluster (the commanded formula "establish the ṣalāh and give the zakah" may pair with s-l-m). (ii) The TWO OBJECTS of divine ṢALĀH (Prophet B4 + believers B3) - the ṢALĀH has both a prophetic recipient and a communal recipient. (iii) The la'nah vs ṢALĀH polarity (33:57 vs 33:56): curse vs standing-toward - the honor-economy's two ends, now in the divine-side. (iv) The human command ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI as the earthly counterpart of the eschatological SALĀM-greeting (33:44) - the s-l-m stem bridges both. (v) The hijab/etiquette context (33:53) - the ṢALĀH on the Prophet is connected to respecting his household.

VERDICT: studied - see B5 (9:99) next.

---

## B5 - 9:103 - khudh min amwalihim sadaqatan tutahhiruhum wa tuzakkihim biha wa salli alayhim inna salataka sakanun lahum

ARABIC (flow): 9:84 "do NOT s-l-l on ANY of them who die, EVER, and do NOT stand at his grave; they disbelieved in God and His Messenger and died as transgressors" -> 9:85-87 their wealth and children are a trial; their hearts sealed -> 9:88 "the Messenger and the believers strove with their wealth and lives; those are the successful" -> 9:89 "God prepared gardens for them" -> 9:90-94 the excuse-makers and the rich who ask to stay behind -> 9:95-96 "they will swear when you return... they are filth; their dwelling is Jahannam" -> 9:97-98 "the Bedouins are stronger in disbelief; they take what they spend as a loss" -> 9:99 "among the Bedouins are those who believe in God and the Last Day and take what they spend as offerings near to God and as ṣalawāt of the Messenger - indeed it is an offering for them; God will admit them to His mercy" -> 9:100 "the first forerunners (Muhajirin and Ansar) - God is pleased with them" -> 9:101-102 "the hypocrites around Medina; those who confess their sins" -> 9:103 "TAKE from their wealth a charity that PURIFIES them and CLEANSES them by it; and S-L-L ON THEM; indeed YOUR ṢALĀH is a TRANQUILITY (sakan) for them. And God is Hearing, Knowing." -> 9:104 "do they not know that God is the One who accepts repentance from His servants and takes the charities, and that God is the Often-Returning, the Merciful" -> 9:105 "and say: work; God will see your work, and His Messenger, and the believers; and you will be returned to the Knower of the unseen and the seen."

WORD FORM: ṢALL (imperative: "s-l-l on them") + ṢALĀTAKA (your ṣalāh) + SAKAN (tranquility/rest). The Prophet is COMMANDED to s-l-l on the repentant, and his ṣalāh is named as SAKAN (tranquility) for them. NEW LEXEME: the ṣalāh as TRANQUILITY for the recipient.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah at-Tawba (9). The verse sits in the repentance-passage (9:102-105): those who confess their sins and mix good and bad deeds; the Prophet is told to take charity from them (purify/cleanse) and s-l-l on them; his ṣalāh is their tranquility. 9:104 reminds: God is the One who accepts repentance and takes charities.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE HUMAN COMMAND (ṢALL ʿALAYHIM): the Prophet is COMMANDED to s-l-l on the repentant - the same imperative form as B4 (ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI on the Prophet), but here directed at the REPENTANT community, not the Prophet. The s-l-l command has TWO objects: the Prophet (B4) and the repentant (B5). Both are acts of standing-toward.
- THE NEW LEXEME - SAKAN (tranquility): "inna ṣalātaka sakanun lahum" - "indeed your ṣalāh is a TRANQUILITY for them." The ṢALĀH is named as SAKAN (rest/settling/tranquility) for the recipient. This is the FIRST time the ṢALĀH is given a QUALITY-NAME on the human side: it is not just a standing-toward; it is a SETTLING-OF. The ṢALĀH brings REST to the one stood-toward. This mirrors the fire-side: the fire-stand is a SETTLING (qarār/mihād/ma'āb) of HOLDING; the God-side ṢALĀH is a SETTLING of TRANQUILITY. Same settling-vocabulary, opposite quality.
- THE PURIFICATION-THEN-ṢALĀH CHAIN (9:103): "take charity that purifies them and cleanses them by it; AND s-l-l on them" - the ṢALĀH comes AFTER purification. The chain: charity (purify/cleanse) -> s-l-l (stand-toward) -> tranquility (sakan). The ṢALĀH is the CLOSING ACT: purification prepares, ṢALĀH delivers the tranquility. This matches 87:14-15 (purify + mention + stand) and 74:1-7 (purify garments + magnify + be patient) - the purification-then-standing pattern is stable.
- 9:99 (B6) "ṣalawāt of the Messenger": the spending is taken as "offerings near to God AND as ṣalawāt of the Messenger" - the ṢALĀH is a CURRENCY: what you spend is counted as ṢALAWĀT (God's standing-toward you, via the Messenger). The ṢALĀH is not just a ritual act but a VALUE that can be EARNED by spending. This parallels 2:157 (ṢALAWĀT granted to the patient) and B3 ( divine ṢALĀH as exodus).
- 9:104 "God is the One who accepts repentance and takes the charities" - the human ṢALĀH (the Prophet's s-l-l) is the earthly tranquility; God's acceptance is the heavenly confirmation. The ṢALĀH-chain: human act (s-l-l) -> earthly tranquility (sakan) -> divine acceptance (qabūl). The ṢALĀH is the bridge between human repentance and divine acceptance.
- 9:84 (B7) "do NOT s-l-l on any of them who die" - the NEGATIVE command: the Prophet is FORBIDDEN to s-l-l on the hypocrites who died. This is the HONOR-ECONOMY in its sharpest form: s-l-l is HONOR; withholding s-l-l is DISHONOR. The same stem that God uses to honor (ṢALĀH) is withheld from the disbelievers (la'nah instead, 2:161). The Prophet's s-l-l is a GIFT that can be withheld.

PARALLELS:
- B4 (33:56 - ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI on the Prophet + SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN): B5's ṢALL ʿALAYHIM is the same imperative form directed at a different object (the repentant instead of the Prophet). The s-l-l command has TWO objects: the Prophet (B4) and the repentant (B5). Both are acts of standing-toward.
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): B5's ṢALĀH + SAKAN (tranquility) is the same pattern: ṢALĀH + a soft-quality (raḥmah/mercy in B2, sakan/tranquility in B5). The ṢALĀH is always qualified by a soft-quality.
- A19 (87:15 - fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward is answered by tranquility (sakan). The ṢALĀH brings rest to the recipient - the positive counterpart of the fire-stand's unease (the fire-stand is a SETTLING of HOLDING; the God-side ṢALĀH is a SETTLING of TRANQUILITY).
- A10 (38:56 - bi'sa-l-mihād, the fire-bedding): the fire-side bedding (mihād) is a SETTLING of HOLDING; B5's sakan (tranquility) is a SETTLING of REST. The same settling-vocabulary, opposite quality - matches A20's furnishing-table.
- 9:99 (B6): the spending-as-ṢALAWĀT - the ṢALĀH as a currency that can be earned by spending. This parallels 2:157 (ṢALAWĀT granted to the patient) - both are ways the human EARNS the divine ṢALAWĀT: by patience (2:157) or by spending (9:99).

FINDING: B5 introduces the SAKAN-lexeme (tranquility) into the ṢALĀH-vocabulary: the Prophet's ṢALĀH is a TRANQUILITY for the repentant. The ṢALĀH is not just a standing-toward; it is a SETTLING-OF. The fire-side was a settling-of-HOLDING (qarār/mihād/ma'āb); the God-side is a settling-of-TRANQUILITY (sakan). The SAME settling-vocabulary, opposite quality. And the ṢALĀH is a CURRENCY (9:99): what you spend is counted as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger - the ṢALĀH can be EARNED. The ṢALĀH-chain now has: human act (s-l-l) -> earthly tranquility (sakan) -> divine acceptance (qabūl, 9:104). The Prophet's ṢALĀH is the bridge between human repentance and divine acceptance.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The SAKAN-lexeme (9:103) - the ṢALĀH as tranquility/rest - HOLD for the final doc as the God-side's settling-word (the positive counterpart of mihād/qarār/masir on the fire-side). (ii) The ṢALĀH-as-currency (9:99 - spending counted as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger) - the ṢALĀH can be EARNED by spending; HOLD for C-cluster (the commanded formula "establish the ṣalāh and give the zakah" - the two are paired). (iii) The negative command (9:84 - "do not s-l-l on them") - the ṢALĀH can be WITHHELD; the honor-economy's sharpest form. (iv) The purification-then-ṢALĀH chain (9:103: charity purifies -> s-l-l -> sakan) - the ṢALĀH as the closing act after purification. (v) The s-l-l command has TWO objects: the Prophet (B4) and the repentant (B5) - both acts of standing-toward.

VERDICT: studied - see B6 (9:99) next.

## B6 - 9:99 - salawatu-r-rasuli

ARABIC (flow): 9:98 "among the Bedouins are those who take what they spend as a LOSS and wait for misfortune to befall you; upon them is the circle of misfortune; and God is Hearing, Knowing" -> 9:99 "and among the Bedouins are those who BELIEVE in God and the Last Day and take what they spend as OFFERINGS NEAR TO GOD and as SALAWAT OF THE MESSENGER - indeed it is an offering for them; God will admit them to His MERCY; indeed God is Forgiving, Merciful" -> 9:100 "the first forerunners (Muhajirin and Ansar) and those who followed them with excellence - God is pleased with them and they are pleased with Him, and He has prepared for them gardens..."

WORD FORM: ṢALAWĀT (plural of ṣalāh) in the genitive "of the Messenger" (ṣalawāti-r-rasūl): "they take what they spend as offerings near to God AND as ṣalawāt of the Messenger." The ṢALĀH-as-currency: spending is counted as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah at-Tawba (9). The verse sits in the Bedouin-passage (9:97-99): some Bedouins take spending as loss (9:98); others take spending as offerings near to God AND as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger (9:99). The contrast is between two attitudes toward spending: loss vs. offering/ṣalāh.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ṢALĀH-AS-CURRENCY: "they take what they spend as qurbāt (offerings-near) to God and as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger." The spending is COUNTED as ṢALAWĀT - the ṢALĀH is not just a ritual act but a VALUE that can be EARNED by spending. The ṢALĀH of the Messenger is a commodity that the spender acquires by giving. This is the commercial dimension of the ṢALĀH: it can be bought, earned, accrued.
- THE GENITIVE "OF THE MESSENGER": the ṢALAWĀT belong to the Messenger - they are HIS ṢALAWĀT, given to those who spend. The Messenger is the CHANNEL of the divine ṢALĀH: God grants ṢALAWĀT through the Messenger's ṢALĀH. This mirrors B4 (33:56): God and His angels s-l-l on the Prophet, and humans are commanded to s-l-l on him - the Prophet is the NODE of the ṢALĀH-chain.
- THE OFFERING-FRAME (qurbah): the spending is taken as "qurbah (offering-near) to God" AND "ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger" - the TWO are paired: offering-near-to-God (vertical, God-directed) and ṢALAWĀT-of-the-Messenger (horizontal, Prophet-directed). The spender gets BOTH: nearness to God AND the Prophet's ṢALĀH.
- 9:98 vs 9:99: the CONTRAST is the point: one Bedouin takes spending as LOSS (maghram, 9:98); the other takes spending as OFFERING and ṢALAWĀT (9:99). The SAME act (spending) is either loss or gain depending on the ATTITUDE. The ṢALĀH-frame transforms spending from loss into gain.
- "indeed it is an offering for them" (a la inna-ha qurbatun lahum): the spending IS an offering - it has been TRANSFORMED from a loss into an offering by the attitude of faith. The ṢALĀH-frame is what transforms it.
- "God will admit them to His MERCY" (sayudkhiluhum Allahu fi rahmatihi): the outcome of the ṢALĀH-as-currency is MERCY (raḥmah) - the same pairing as B2 (ṢALAWĀT + raḥmah) and B3 (ṢALĀH + raḥmah). The ṢALĀH always ends in mercy on the God-side.

PARALLELS:
- B5 (9:103 - ṢALL ʿALAYHIM, your ṢALĀH is their tranquility): B5's ṢALĀH-as-tranquility (sakan) and B6's ṢALĀH-as-currency (qurbah) are the SAME ṢALĀH in two forms: the Prophet's ṢALĀH brings tranquility (B5) and the spender's ṢALĀH is counted as an offering (B6). The ṢALĀH is both GIFT (given by the Prophet) and CURRENCY (earned by spending).
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): B6's ṢALAWĀT + raḥmah (mercy) - the same pairing. The ṢALAWĀT always ends in mercy.
- B3 (33:43 - God s-l-l on you + angels, exodus darkness->light): the divine ṢALĀH is the source; the Messenger's ṢALĀH (B6) is the channel; the spender's spending is the human act that ACQUIRES it. The ṢALĀH-chain: divine source -> prophetic channel -> human acquisition.
- A19 (87:15 - fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward is answered by the divine ṢALĀH; B6 shows that the human can also ACQUIRE the ṢALĀH by spending. The ṢALĀH is both a RESPONSE (to standing) and a REWARD (for spending).
- 2:153 "seek help in patience and the ṣalāh" + 9:99 "take what they spend as ṢALAWĀT": the human ṣalāh (the standing-act) and the spending-as-ṢALAWĀT are TWO WAYS to acquire the divine ṢALĀH: by standing (2:153) or by spending (9:99). The ṢALĀH is available through multiple channels.

FINDING: B6 reveals the ṢALĀH-as-CURRENCY: spending is counted as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger. The ṢALĀH is not just a ritual act or a divine response; it is a VALUE that can be EARNED by spending. The ṢALĀH-chain now has THREE human inputs: standing (2:153 ṣalāh), spending (9:99 ṢALAWĀT-of-the-Messenger), and repentance (9:103 ṢALL-ʿALAYHIM). All three earn the divine ṢALĀH. And the ṢALĀH is always qualified by a soft-quality: raḥmah/mercy (B2, B3, B6), sakan/tranquility (B5), SALĀM/peace (B4). The ṢALĀH is the God-side's SETTLING-act: it settles the recipient in mercy, tranquility, peace.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The ṢALĀH-as-currency (9:99) - the ṢALĀH can be EARNED by spending; HOLD for C-cluster (the commanded formula "establish the ṣalāh and give the zakah" - the two are paired: ṢALĀH + ZAKAH = the C-cluster's core formula). (ii) The THREE human inputs to the ṢALĀH-chain (standing 2:153, spending 9:99, repentance 9:103) - HOLD as the human-side's three channels to the divine ṢALĀH. (iii) The ṢALĀH always qualified by a soft-quality (raḥmah/sakan/SALĀM) - the God-side's settling-quality. (iv) The Messenger as CHANNEL (B6 "ṣalawāt of the Messenger") - the Prophet is the node of the ṢALĀH-chain (B4 divine ṢALĀH on the Prophet; B6 human ṢALĀH through the Prophet).

VERDICT: studied - see B7 (9:84) next.

---

## B7 - 9:84 - wa la tusalli ala ahadin minhum mata abadan

ARABIC (flow): 9:80 "ask forgiveness for them or do not ask forgiveness for them; if you ask forgiveness for them seventy times, God will not forgive them - that is because they disbelieved in God and His Messenger; and God does not guide the transgressing people" -> 9:81 "the stay-behinds rejoiced in their sitting behind the Messenger of God, and disliked to strive with their wealth and their lives in the path of God, and said: do not march out in the heat. Say: the fire of Jahannam is hotter, if they understood" -> 9:82 "let them laugh a little and weep much - a recompense for what they used to earn" -> 9:83 "if God returns you to a party of them and they ask permission to go out with you, say: you shall never go out with me, and you shall never fight an enemy with me; you were pleased with sitting the first time, so sit with those who stay behind" -> **9:84 "and do NOT s-l-l on ANY of them who dies, EVER, and do NOT STAND at his grave; they disbelieved in God and His Messenger and died as transgressors (fasiqun)"** -> 9:85-87 "do not be amazed by their wealth and children... their hearts are sealed" -> 9:88 "the Messenger and the believers strove with their wealth and lives; those are the successful" -> 9:89 "God prepared gardens for them" -> ... 9:95-96 "they will swear when you return... they are filth; their dwelling is Jahannam" -> 9:97-98 "the Bedouins are stronger in disbelief; they take what they spend as a loss" -> 9:99 "among the Bedouins are those who believe... and take what they spend as offerings near to God and as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger" -> 9:100-105 (repentance-passage; 9:103 "take charity... and s-l-l on them; your ṣalāh is their tranquility").

WORD FORM: lā tuṣallī (NEGATIVE IMPERATIVE: "do NOT s-l-l") - the FIRST NEGATIVE command of the s-l-w stem in the divine-side. The object: ʿalā aḥadin minhum (on any of them). The time-marker: abadan (EVER) - absolute, no exception. The reason: "they disbelieved in God and His Messenger and died as transgressors (fasiqun)."

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah at-Tawba (9). The verse sits in the hypocrite-passage (9:80-87): those who stayed behind, refused to strive, disbelieved, died as transgressors. The Prophet is COMMANDED NOT to s-l-l on them and NOT to stand at their graves. 9:99 (B6) immediately after (in the same passage) shows the CONTRAST: the believing Bedouins who take spending as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger.

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE NEGATIVE COMMAND (lā tuṣallī ʿalā aḥadin... abadan): the Prophet is FORBIDDEN to s-l-l on the dead hypocrites - EVER. The s-l-l is a GIFT that can be WITHHELD; the honor-economy's sharpest form: s-l-l = honor; withholding s-l-l = dishonor. The same stem that God uses to honor (ṢALĀH) is withheld from the disbelievers. The fire-side had madhmum/madhur (A6 - expelled, disgraced); the God-side has la'nah (curse, 2:161, 33:57) and the WITHHOLDING of ṢALĀH (9:84).
- THE GRAVE-STANDING PROHIBITION (wa lā taqum ʿalā qabrihi): "do not STAND at his grave" - the qiyām (standing) at the grave is FORBIDDEN alongside the s-l-l. The standing-at-the-grave (qiyām) and the standing-toward (s-l-l) are BOTH withheld. The ṢALĀH and the QIYĀM are paired as the two honor-acts: standing-TOWARD (ṣalāh) and standing-AT (qiyām). Both are withheld from the disbelievers.
- THE REASON (kafarū bi-llāhi wa rasūlihi wa mātū wa hum fāsiqūn): they disbelieved in God and His Messenger and died as transgressors. The s-l-l is withheld because of disbelief + transgression. The ṢALĀH is granted to those who BELIEVE (9:99) and withheld from those who DISBELIEVE (9:84). The ṢALĀH is the response to belief; the la'nah/withholding is the response to disbelief.
- 9:99 (B6) as the IMMEDIATE CONTRAST: "among the Bedouins are those who BELIEVE... and take what they spend as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger" - the SAME passage contains BOTH: the disbelievers who are denied ṢALĀH (9:84) and the believers who earn ṢALĀH (9:99). The ṢALĀH is the DIVIDER: granted to believers, withheld from disbelievers.
- 9:103 (B5) as the IMMEDIATE FOLLOW-UP: "take charity that purifies them and cleanses them; and s-l-l on them; your ṣalāh is their tranquility" - the Prophet's ṢALĀH is the HEALING act for the repentant. The ṢALĀH heals (sakan/tranquility) and honors; the withholding of ṢALĀH condemns. The same stem, two directions: granted-ṢALĀH (healing) vs withheld-ṢALĀH (condemnation).

PARALLELS:
- 2:161 "upon them is the CURSE (la'nah) of God and the angels and all the people" - the la'nah is the NEGATIVE of the ṢALĀH: curse vs standing-toward. 9:84's withholding of ṢALĀH is the earthly counterpart of 2:161's la'nah.
- 33:57 "those who harm God and His Messenger - God has cursed them" - the la'nah vs ṢALĀH polarity in the divine-side. 9:84's withholding is the PROPHETIC form of the same polarity.
- A6 (17:18 - madhmuman madhuran, the expelled): the fire-side's expelled states (madhmum/madhur) are the NEGATIVE of the divine ṢALĀH. 9:84's withholding of ṢALĀH is the earthly counterpart of the fire-side's expelled states.
- B4 (33:56 - ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI on the Prophet): the command to s-l-l on the Prophet vs the prohibition to s-l-l on the dead hypocrites (9:84). The ṢALĀH is a GIFT that is commanded toward the Prophet and forbidden toward the disbelievers.
- B5 (9:103 - ṢALL ʿALAYHIM, your ṣalāh is their tranquility): the ṢALĀH as healing (sakan) vs the withholding of ṢALĀH as condemnation (9:84). The same stem: granted-ṢALĀH heals; withheld-ṢALĀH condemns.

FINDING: B7 is the NEGATIVE-command of the divine-side: the Prophet is FORBIDDEN to s-l-l on the dead hypocrites (EVER) and FORBIDDEN to stand at their graves. The ṢALĀH is HONOR that can be WITHHELD; the withholding is the earthly counterpart of la'nah (curse). The ṢALĀH and the QIYĀM (standing at the grave) are paired as the two honor-acts, both withheld. And the SAME passage (9:84 vs 9:99) contains BOTH: the disbelievers denied ṢALĀH and the believers earning ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the DIVIDER: granted to believers, withheld from disbelievers. The B-cluster now has the FULL polarity: granted-ṢALĀH (B2-B6) vs withheld-ṢALĀH (B7), the same stem, two directions - mirroring the A-cluster's imposed-vs-granted polarity.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The qiyām (standing at the grave) paired with ṢALĀH (9:84) - the two honor-acts: standing-TOWARD and standing-AT. HOLD for C-cluster (the commanded "establish the ṣalāh" may include the qiyām dimension). (ii) The la'nah vs ṢALĀH polarity: curse vs standing-toward - the honor-economy's two ends, now complete on the divine-side. (iii) The honor-economy table: ṢALĀH (honor) / la'nah (dishonor) / madhmum-madhur (expelled) - the three negative states vs the ṢALĀH. (iv) B-cluster complete: B1-B7 now studied. The divine-side's ṢALĀH is: innate (B1 birds), granted (B2 patience), exodus (B3 darkness->light), commanded (B4 ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI), currency (B6 ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger), tranquility (B5 sakan), and withheld (B7 la tuṣallī). Seven forms, one stem.

VERDICT: studied - B-cluster COMPLETE (7/7). Next cluster C1 (2:125 - Abraham's musallā).

## C5 - 2:125 - wa-t-takhidhu min maqami Ibrahima musallan

ARABIC (flow): 2:124 "when Abraham was tested by his Lord with words and fulfilled them: 'I am making you an IMAM for the people.' 'And from my offspring?' 'My covenant does not reach the WRONGDOERS.'" -> 2:125 "when We made the HOUSE a place of RETURN for the people and a SECURITY, and 'TAKE from the standing-place (maqam) of Abraham a MUSSALLA' (wa-t-takhidhu min maqami Ibrahima musallan), and We covenanted to Abraham and Isma'il: PURIFY My House for the circlers, the secluded, and the bowing-prostrating" -> 2:126 "when Abraham said: 'My Lord, make this a secure city and provide its people with fruits - whoever of them believes in God and the Last Day.' He said: 'and whoever disbelieves, I will let him enjoy a little, then drive him to the punishment of the Fire; and evil a DESTINATION (maṣīr)'" -> 2:127 "when Abraham was raising the foundations of the House, and Isma'il: 'Our Lord, accept from us; You are the Hearing, the Knowing'" -> 2:128 "Our Lord, make us SUBMISSIVE to You, and from our offspring a submissive community to You, and show us our RITES, and turn to us; You are the Often-Returning, the Merciful" -> 2:129 "Our Lord, raise up in them a Messenger from among them who recites to them Your signs and teaches them the Book and the wisdom and PURIFIES them" -> 2:130 "who would turn away from the religion of Abraham except one who fools himself? We chose him in this world, and in the Hereafter he is among the righteous" -> 2:131 "when his Lord said to him: SUBMIT (aslim). He said: I have submitted to the Lord of the worlds" -> 2:132 "Abraham instructed his sons, and Jacob: 'O my sons, God has chosen for you the religion, so do not die except as SUBMITTERS (muslimun)'" -> 2:133 "were you witnesses when death came to Jacob... 'we will worship your God and the God of your fathers Abraham, Isma'il, and Isaac - one God; and to Him we are submitters'" -> 2:134-137 "that is a community that has passed... Say: we believe in God and what was sent down to us and what was sent down to Abraham, Isma'il, Isaac, Jacob, and the Tribes, and what was given to Moses and Jesus and what was given to the prophets... we make no distinction between any of them, and to Him we are submitters."

WORD FORM: MUSSALLA (noun of place: "a place of standing") - the SITE-lexeme of the root, the SECOND site-word in the corpus (after 22:40's ṣalawāt for other communities' houses). The believers' site-word is MUSSALLA (singular, an assigned spot) vs the others' plural ṢALAWĀT. Abraham's MAQĀM (standing-place) becomes the MUSSALLA - the standing-place is INHERITED from Abraham.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Baqara (2). The verse sits in the Abraham-covenant passage (2:124-137): Abraham tested, made Imam, given the House as a place of return and security; the MUSSALLA is taken from his standing-place; the covenant to purify the House; the prayer for a Messenger; the instruction to die as submitters; the religion of Abraham as upright (ḥanīf).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE SITE-LEXEME: MUSSALLA = "a place of standing" (noun of place from ṣ-l-w). This is the BELIEVERS' site-word: singular, assigned, inherited from Abraham's MAQĀM. The other communities get the plural ṢALAWĀT (22:40 A8); the believers get the singular MUSSALLA. The root's SITE-pole has TWO lexemes: ṢALAWĀT (their houses, plural) and MUSSALLA (our spot, singular). The believer's standing-site is ONE assigned spot, not many houses.
- THE IMAMATE PAIRING: 2:124 "I am making you an Imam for the people" - the Imam (leader) and the MUSSALLA (standing-place) are paired: Abraham is made Imam, and his maqām becomes the community's MUSSALLA. The leader and the standing-place are ONE covenant.
- THE COVENANT (ʿahd) TO PURIFY: "We covenanted to Abraham and Isma'il: purify My House for the circlers, the secluded, and the bowing-prostrating." The MUSSALLA is paired with the COVENANT of PURIFICATION. The standing-site requires purification - the same purity-gate as A12 (56:79 "none touch it except the purified") and A15 (74:4 "purify your garments"). The standing-site is gated on purity.
- THE SUBMISSION-VERB (aslama, from s-l-m) paired with the MUSSALLA (from ṣ-l-w): 2:128 "make us SUBMISSIVE to You" (muslimīn) + "show us our rites" (manāsik). The two stems (s-l-w standing + s-l-m surrender) are COUSINS (B4 showed this: ṣallū ʿalayhi wa sallimū taslīman). Here Abraham's submission (aslama) and the MUSSALLA (standing-place) are paired: the submitter stands at the MUSSALLA. The standing-site is the place of the submitter.
- "do not die except as submitters (muslimūn)" (2:132): the covenant is to DIE as a submitter. The MUSSALLA is the place where the submitter stands; the covenant is to remain a submitter until death. The standing-site is a LIFETIME commitment, not a momentary visit.
- 2:126 "evil a maṣīr" - the SAME settlement-word as A4 (4:115) and A13 (58:8) - but here it is the destination of the DISBELIEVER (not the fire-delivery itself, but the same maṣīr-word). The maṣīr-word serves both poles: fire-destination (A4/A13) and disbeliever-destination (2:126). The standing-site's opposite is the maṣīr of the disbeliever.
- 2:129 "raise up a Messenger from among them who recites, teaches, purifies" - the Messenger (2:151 B2) is the HUMAN AGENT of the chain; the MUSSALLA is the SITE where the Messenger's recitation/teaching/purification happens. The standing-site and the prophetic mission are paired.
- 2:135 "the religion of Abraham, upright (ḥanīfan)" - the religion of Abraham is the UPRIGHT religion - the standing-toward-God religion. The MUSSALLA is the SITE of the upright religion.
- 2:136 "we believe in God and what was sent down to us and what was sent down to Abraham, Isma'il, Isaac, Jacob, and the Tribes, and what was given to Moses and Jesus and what was given to the prophets" - the MUSSALLA is part of the SAME religion given to ALL prophets. The standing-site is not a new invention; it is the ABRAHAMIC standing-site inherited by all. The ṢALĀH-chain (B-cluster) and the MUSSALLA (C-cluster) are ONE covenant tradition.
- 2:128 "show us our rites (manāsik)" - the rites are SHOWN, not invented. The MUSSALLA is part of the shown rites - God showed Abraham the standing-site.

PARALLELS:
- A8 (22:40 - ṢALAWĀT as other communities' houses of Name-mention): the MUSSALLA (2:125) is the BELIEVER-side site-word: singular (our spot) vs plural (their houses). The root's SITE-pole has TWO lexemes: ṢALAWĀT (their houses) and MUSSALLA (our spot). The believer's standing-site is ONE assigned spot.
- A12 (56:79 - purity-gate: none touch it except the purified): the covenant to PURIFY the House (2:125) is the same purity-gate. The standing-site is gated on purity - the same gate as the fire-side's purity (56:79 mutaḥḥarūn) and the standing-prescription's purity (74:4 "purify your garments").
- A19 (87:15 - fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward-the-Name happens AT the MUSSALLA. The MUSSALLA is the SITE where fa-ṣallā takes place. The standing-site and the standing-act are paired.
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): the divine ṢALAWĀT is granted to those who stand at the MUSSALLA (the standing-site). The site and the ṢALAWĀT are adjacent (2:157-158).
- B4 (33:56 - ṢALLŪ ʿALAYHI + SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN): the s-l-w + s-l-m pairing appears here too: Abraham's submission (aslama, s-l-m) and the MUSSALLA (ṣ-l-w standing-site) are paired. The submitter stands at the MUSSALLA.
- 87:18-19 (A19 - "this is in the first Scrolls, the Scrolls of Abraham and Moses"): the MUSSALLA and the standing-formula are ALREADY WRITTEN in the Scrolls of Abraham. The standing-site is covenant-tradition, not novelty.

FINDING: C1 introduces the SITE-lexeme MUSSALLA: "take from the standing-place of Abraham a MUSSALLA" - the believer's standing-site is a SINGLE assigned spot, inherited from Abraham's MAQĀM. The MUSSALLA is paired with (1) the IMAMATE (Abraham as Imam), (2) the COVENANT of PURIFICATION (purify My House), (3) the SUBMISSION (aslim/aslamtu - the submitter stands at the MUSSALLA), (4) the RITES (manāsik, shown not invented), (5) the MESSENGER (recites/teaches/purifies), (6) the UPRIGHT RELIGION (ḥanīf), and (7) the SCROLLS (already written in Abraham's and Moses' Scrolls). The MUSSALLA is the SITE where the fa-ṣallā (standing-toward-the-Name) takes place - the human act of standing (A19) happens at the inherited standing-site (C1). The SITE-pole and the STATE-pole are now joined: the SITE (MUSSALLA) is where the STATE (fa-ṣallā) is performed.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The MUSSALLA vs ṢALAWĀT site-lexeme pair (singular vs plural) - the believer's spot vs the others' houses. HOLD for the final doc's site-table. (ii) The purification-covenant (2:125 "purify My House") - the standing-site is gated on purity; HOLD for C/D (the washing-command 5:6 and the purification-prescription 74:4). (iii) The submission (aslama) + MUSSALLA pairing - the submitter stands at the MUSSALLA; the s-l-m + ṣ-l-w cousin-stems are paired at the founding. (iv) The rites (manāsik) shown not invented - the standing-site is part of the shown rites. (v) The Messenger (2:129) as the human agent - the standing-site and the prophetic mission are paired. (vi) 2:126's maṣīr (evil a destination) - the maṣīr-word serves both poles (fire-destination A4/A13 and disbeliever-destination 2:126).

VERDICT: studied - see C1 (98:5 - din al-qayyimah, the minimal religion) next.
