## D28 - 13:22 - wa alladhina sabaru ibtigha'a wajhi rabbihim wa aqamu-s-salata wa anfiqu mimmā razaqnāhum sirran wa 'alāniyatan wa yadra'ūna bi-l-hasinati-s-sayyi'ata

ARABIC (flow): 13:20 "Those who fulfill the covenant of God and do not break the pact" -> 13:21 "And those who join what God has commanded to be joined and fear their Lord and fear the evil of the reckoning" -> **13:22 "AND THOSE WHO ARE PATIENT, SEEKING THE COUNTENANCE OF THEIR LORD, AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND SPEND FROM WHAT WE HAVE PROVIDED THEM, SECRETLY AND PUBLICLY, AND REPEL EVIL WITH GOOD - THOSE WILL HAVE THE GOOD OUTCOME OF THE HOME"** -> 13:23 "Gardens of Eden which they will enter, and those who were righteous among their fathers and their spouses and their offspring, and the angels will enter upon them from every gate" -> 13:24 "Peace be upon you for what you patiently endured. How excellent is the final home" -> 13:25 "And those who break the covenant of God after contracting it and sever what God has commanded to be joined and cause corruption in the land - those will have the curse, and they will have the worst home."

WORD FORM: ṣabarū (were patient) + ibtighāʾa (seeking) wajhi (the countenance/face of) rabbihim (their Lord) + aqāmū (established) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + anfaqū (spent) mimmā (from what) razaqnāhum (We provided them) sirran (secretly) wa ʿalāniyatan (and publicly) + yadraʾūna (repel) bi-l-ḥasanati (with good) al-sayyiʾata (the evil) + ulāʾika (those) lahum (for them) ʿuqbā (the good outcome) al-dāri (of the home).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah ar-Ra'd (13). The verse sits in the righteous-passage (13:20-25): those who fulfill the covenant (13:20), join what God commanded (13:21), are patient seeking God's face, establish the ṢALĀH, spend secretly and publicly, repel evil with good (13:22), enter gardens of Eden (13:23), receive peace (13:24), vs those who break the covenant (13:25).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE FIVE-ACT FORMULA: (1) PATIENCE (ṣabr) seeking God's face (ibtighāʾ wajhi rabbihim), (2) ESTABLISH THE SALAH, (3) SPEND from what God provided, secretly and publicly (sirran wa ʿalāniyatan), (4) REPEL evil with good (yadraʾūna bi-l-ḥasanati al-sayyiʾata). The ṢALĀH is act #2 of a five-act formula. The same as C19 (31:17: establish ṢALĀH + enjoin right + forbid wrong + patience) and D26 (9:71: enjoin right + forbid wrong + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey). The ṢALĀH is always in the MIDDLE of a multi-act formula.
- THE SEEKING-GOD'S-FACE (ibtighāʾ wajhi rabbihim): "seeking the COUNTENANCE of their Lord." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who SEEKS GOD'S FACE (wajh). The ṢALĀH is the God-facing act: the stander faces God. The same as A19 (fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name) - the ṢALĀH is the standing-toward-God's-Face. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who seeks God's face.
- THE SECRET-AND-PUBLIC SPENDING (sirran wa ʿalāniyatan): "spend from what We have provided them, SECRETLY AND PUBLICLY." The ṢALĀH is paired with spending that is both secret and public. The ṢALĀH is the God-ward act; spending is the people-ward act. The spending is both hidden (secret) and visible (public) - the ṢALĀH is the same: it is both a private standing (secret) and a public act (called, D15).
- THE REPEL-EVIL-WITH-GOOD (yadraʾūna bi-l-ḥasanati al-sayyiʾata): "repel evil with good." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who REPELS EVIL WITH GOOD. The ṢALĀH is the good that repels evil. The same as C17 (11:114 "the good deeds remove the evil deeds") - the ṢALĀH removes evil. The ṢALĀH is the anti-evil act.
- 13:24 "Peace be upon you for what you patiently endured. How excellent is the final home" - the ṢALĀH is rewarded with PEACE (salām) and a good home (ʿuqbā al-dār). The ṢALĀH is the act that earns peace and a good home. The same as B3 (33:43-44 - divine ṢALĀH -> SALĀM-greeting) and B5 (9:103 - "your ṣalāh is their tranquility").
- 13:20-21 "those who fulfill the covenant of God and do not break the pact; and those who join what God has commanded to be joined and fear their Lord" - the ṢALĀH is paired with COVENANT-KEEPING (fulfill the covenant) and JOINING (join what God commanded to be joined). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who keeps covenants and joins what should be joined. The same as C3 (2:83 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah; then you turned away") - the ṢALĀH is part of the covenant.

PARALLELS:
- D1 (2:3 - "believe in the unseen and establish the ṢALĀH and spend"): D1 gave the ṢALĀH + SPENDING pair. D28 adds PATIENCE + SEEKING GOD'S FACE + REPELLING EVIL WITH GOOD. The ṢALĀH is embedded in a five-act formula: patience + seek God's face + ṢALĀH + spending (secretly and publicly) + repel evil with good.
- C17 (11:114 - "the good deeds remove the evil deeds"): C17 gave the ṢALĀH's SIN-REMOVAL. D28 gives the ṢALĀH's EVIL-REPELLING (yadraʾūna bi-l-ḥasanati al-sayyiʾata). The ṢALĀH both removes evil (C17) and repels evil (D28). The ṢALĀH is the anti-evil act.
- C19 (31:17 - "establish the ṢALĀH, enjoin right, forbid wrong, be patient"): C19's four-command formula (ṢALĀH + enjoin + forbid + patience) and D28's five-act formula (patience + seek God's face + ṢALĀH + spending + repel evil) are the SAME structure: the ṢALĀH is in the MIDDLE, paired with patience and social acts.
- D3 (2:177 - birr: 10-item character list): D3's 10-item list and D28's 5-act formula are the SAME structure: the ṢALĀH is embedded in a comprehensive character. D3's list is longer; D28's is more focused.
- A19 (87:15 - fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward-the-Name is what the ṢALĀH (established) IS. D28's "seeking the countenance of their Lord" (ibtighāʾ wajhi rabbihim) is the same as A19's fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name. The ṢALĀH is the standing-toward-God's-Face.
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): D28's "Peace be upon you for what you patiently endured" (13:24) is the SALĀM-greeting (B3/B4). The ṢALĀH is rewarded with SALĀM. The same as B3 (33:43-44 - divine ṢALĀH -> SALĀM-greeting).

FINDING: D28 gives the ṢALĀH as the GOD-FACING act: "those who are patient, seeking the COUNTENANCE of their Lord, and ESTABLISH THE SALAH and spend from what We have provided them, secretly and publicly, and REPEL EVIL WITH GOOD." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who SEEKS GOD'S FACE (wajh). The ṢALĀH is the standing-toward-God's-Face. And the ṢALĀH is paired with: (1) patience (ṣabr), (2) seeking God's face (ibtighāʾ wajh), (3) spending (secretly and publicly), (4) repelling evil with good. The ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE act of a five-act formula. And the ṢALĀH is rewarded with PEACE (salām) and a good home (ʿuqbā al-dār). The ṢALĀH is the act that earns peace and a good home.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The GOD-FACING (ibtighāʾ wajhi rabbihim) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who seeks God's face. HOLD for the final doc's face-table. (ii) The REPEL-EVIL-WITH-GOOD (yadraʾūna bi-l-ḥasanati al-sayyiʾata) - the ṢALĀH is the good that repels evil. HOLD for the final doc's anti-evil table. (iii) The SECRET-AND-PUBLIC SPENDING (sirran wa ʿalāniyatan) - the ṢALĀH is paired with spending that is both secret and public. HOLD for the final doc's spending-table. (iv) The SALĀM-GREETING (13:24) - the ṢALĀH is rewarded with peace. HOLD for the final doc's greeting-table. (v) The COVENANT-KEEPING (13:20) - the ṢALĀH is paired with covenant-keeping. HOLD for C-cluster's covenant-tradition. (vi) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-THREE forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), and God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28). All twenty-three are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D29 (14:31 - ???) next.

## D29 - 14:31 - qul li-'ibadi alladhina amanu yuqimu-s-salata wa yunfiqu mimmā razaqnāhum sirran wa 'alāniyatan min qabli an ya'tiya yawmun la bay'a fihi wa la khilal

ARABIC (flow): 14:30 "And they have set up rivals to God to mislead [people] from His way. Say: Enjoy yourselves, for indeed your destination is the Fire" -> **14:31 "Say to My servants who have believed: ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND SPEND from what We have provided them, SECRETLY AND PUBLICLY, before a Day comes in which there is no bargaining nor friendship"** -> 14:32 "God is the One who created the heavens and the earth and sent down water from the sky and brought forth thereby fruits as provision for you, and subjected for you the ships to sail through the sea by His command, and subjected for you the rivers."

WORD FORM: qul (say) li-ʿibādī (to My servants) alladhīna (who) āmanū (have believed) yuqīmū (establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) wa-yunfiqū (and spend) mimmā (from what) razaqnāhum (We have provided them) sirran (secretly) wa-ʿalāniyatan (and publicly) min qabli (before) an yaʾtiya (comes) yawmun (a Day) lā bayʿa (no bargaining) fīhi (in it) wa lā khilāl (nor friendship).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah Ibrahim (14). The verse sits in the warning-passage (14:30-32): rivals to God mislead (14:30), establish the ṢALĀH and spend secretly and publicly before a Day with no bargaining nor friendship (14:31), God created the heavens and the earth and provided for you (14:32).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE TIME-DEADLINE (min qabli an yaʾtiya yawmun lā bayʿa fīhi wa lā khilāl): "before a Day comes in which there is no bargaining nor friendship." The ṢALĀH + spending must be done BEFORE the Day of Reckoning. After that Day, no bargaining (bayʿ) and no friendship (khilāl) will avail. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who acts BEFORE the Day. The ṢALĀH is a PRE-DEADLINE act: it must be done while there is still time for bargaining and friendship.
- THE SECRET-AND-PUBLIC (sirran wa ʿalāniyatan): "spend from what We have provided them, SECRETLY AND PUBLICLY." The ṢALĀH (and the spending) are done both secretly and publicly. The ṢALĀH is both a private act (secret) and a public act (called, D15). The same as D28's "spend from what We have provided them, secretly and publicly." The ṢALĀH is both hidden and visible.
- "MY SERVANTS" (ʿibādī): "Say to My servants who have believed." The ṢALĀH is established by God's SERVANTS (ʿibād). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is a servant of God. The same as C7 (14:40 "make me an establisher of the ṢALĀH") - Abraham prayed to be a servant-establisher. The ṢALĀH is the act of servanthood.
- THE ṢALĀH + SPENDING PAIR (AGAIN): "establish the ṢALĀH and spend from what We have provided them." The SAME two-act definition as D1 (2:3) and D21 (8:3). The ṢALĀH is the God-ward act; spending is the people-ward act. The two-act definition is STABLE across suras.
- 14:30 "your destination is the Fire" - the ṢALĀH is the act that avoids the Fire. The ṢALĀH is the alternative to the Fire. The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose destination is NOT the Fire.
- 14:32 "God is the One who created the heavens and the earth and sent down water from the sky and brought forth thereby fruits as provision for you" - the ṢALĀH is established with what God has provided (water, fruits, provision). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who acknowledges God's provision. The same as 2:3 ("spend from what We have provided them") and 8:3 ("spend from what We have provided them").

PARALLELS:
- D28 (13:22 - "those who are patient, seeking God's face, establish the ṢALĀH and spend, secretly and publicly, and repel evil with good"): D28 gave the ṢALĀH as the GOD-FACING act (seeking God's face). D29 gives the ṢALĀH as the PRE-DEADLINE act (before a Day with no bargaining). The ṢALĀH is both God-facing (D28) and time-bound (D29).
- D1 (2:3 - "those who believe in the unseen and establish the ṢALĀH and spend"): D1 gave the ṢALĀH + SPENDING pair. D29 repeats it with a TIME-DEADLINE. The ṢALĀH + SPENDING pair is the two-act definition; the time-deadline adds urgency.
- D17 (5:106 - "detain them after the ṢALĀH"): D17 gave the ṢALĀH as a LEGAL anchor. D29 gives the ṢALĀH as a PRE-DEADLINE act. The ṢALĀH is both a legal anchor (D17) and a time-bound act (D29).
- C10 (20:14 - "establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance"): C10 gave the ṢALĀH's PURPOSE (remembrance). D29 gives the ṢALĀH's URGENCY (before the Day). The ṢALĀH is both a remembrance-act (C10) and an urgent act (D29).
- 14:30 "your destination is the Fire" - the ṢALĀH is the act that avoids the Fire. The same as A-cluster (fire-delivery for ṢALĀH-loss). The ṢALĀH is the anti-Fire act.

FINDING: D29 gives the ṢALĀH's URGENCY-DIMENSION: "establish the ṢALĀH and spend from what We have provided them, SECRETLY AND PUBLICLY, before a Day comes in which there is no bargaining nor friendship." The ṢALĀH must be done BEFORE the Day of Reckoning - after that Day, no bargaining (bayʿ) and no friendship (khilāl) will avail. The ṢALĀH is a PRE-DEADLINE act: it must be done while there is still time. And the ṢALĀH is for God's SERVANTS (ʿibād) - the ṢALĀH is the act of servanthood. The ṢALĀH is both secret and public (sirran wa ʿalāniyatan) - the same as D28's spending. The ṢALĀH is both a private act and a public act.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The TIME-DEADLINE (14:31) - the ṢALĀH must be done before the Day of Reckoning. HOLD for the final doc's time-table. (ii) The SECRET-AND-PUBLIC (14:31) - the ṢALĀH is both secret and public. HOLD for the final doc's visibility-table. (iii) The SERVANTHOOD (ʿibād) - the ṢALĀH is the act of God's servants. HOLD for the final doc's servanthood-table. (iv) The NO-BARGAINING (lā bayʿa) - after the Day, no bargaining will avail. HOLD for the final doc's eschatology-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-FOUR forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), and urgent (must be done before the Day, D29). All twenty-four are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D30 (21:73 - ???) next.

## D30 - 21:73 - wa-ja'alnahum a'immatan yahduna bi-amrina wa awḥayna ilayhim fi'la al-khayrati wa iqama-s-salati wa ita'a-z-zakati

ARABIC (flow): 21:70 "And they intended for him a plan, but We made them the greatest losers" -> 21:71 "And We saved him and Lot to the land which We had blessed for the worlds" -> 21:72 "And We gave him Isaac and Jacob as a gift, and all [of them] We made righteous" -> **21:73 "AND WE MADE THEM LEADERS (aʾimmatan) GUIDING BY OUR COMMAND, AND WE INSPIRED TO THEM THE DOING OF GOOD DEEDS AND THE ESTABLISHMENT OF THE SALAH AND THE GIVING OF THE ZAKAH, AND THEY WERE WORSHIPPERS OF US"** -> 21:74 "And to Lot We gave judgment and knowledge, and We saved him from the city which used to work abominations. Indeed, they were a people, evil, defiantly disobedient" -> 21:75 "And We admitted him into Our mercy. Indeed, he was of the righteous."

WORD FORM: jaʿalnāhum (We made them) aʾimmatan (leaders) yahdūna (guiding) bi-amrinā (by Our command) + wa-awḥaynā (and We inspired/revealed to them) ilayhim (to them) fiʿla (the doing of) al-khayrāti (good deeds) wa-iqāma (and the establishment of) al-ṣalāti (the ṣalāh) wa-ītāʾa (and the giving of) al-zakāti (the zakah) + wa-kānū (and they were) lanā (for Us) ʿābidīn (worshippers).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah al-Anbiya' (21). The verse sits in the Abraham-passage (21:69-75): they intended a plan against Abraham (21:70), We saved him and Lot (21:71), We gave him Isaac and Jacob (21:72), WE MADE THEM LEADERS GUIDING BY OUR COMMAND, AND WE INSPIRED TO THEM THE DOING OF GOOD DEEDS AND THE ESTABLISHMENT OF THE SALAH AND THE GIVING OF THE ZAKAH, AND THEY WERE WORSHIPPERS OF US (21:73), We saved Lot (21:74-75).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE LEADERSHIP (aʾimmatan): "We made them LEADERS (aʾimmatan) GUIDING BY OUR COMMAND." The ṢALĀH-establishers are LEADERS (aʾimma) who GUIDE by God's command. The ṢALĀH is the act of a LEADER: the leader establishes the ṢALĀH and guides others. The same as C5 (2:124 "I am making you an Imam for the people" - Abraham as Imam). The ṢALĀH and the imamate are paired: the leader establishes the ṢALĀH.
- THE DIVINE INSPIRATION (awḥaynā ilayhim): "We INSPIRED to them the doing of good deeds and the establishment of the ṢALĀH and the giving of the zakah." The ṢALĀH is INSPIRED (wahy) by God. The ṢALĀH is not a human invention; it is a DIVINE INSPIRATION. The same as C1 (98:2 "a Messenger from God, reciting purified pages") and C10 (20:13 "listen to what is revealed"). The ṢALĀH is revealed.
- THE THREE INSPIRED ACTS: (1) doing good deeds (fiʿl al-khayrāt), (2) establishing the ṢALĀH (iqāma al-ṣalāh), (3) giving the zakah (ītāʾ al-zakāh). The ṢALĀH is one of THREE inspired acts. The ṢALĀH is paired with good deeds and zakah. The same as C1 (98:5 "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah") - the two-pillar formula now has a THIRD element (good deeds).
- "THEY WERE WORSHIPPERS OF US" (kānū lanā ʿābidīn): the ṢALĀH-establishers are WORSHIPPERS of God. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who worships God. The ṢALĀH is the worship-act. The same as 6:162 ("my ṢALĀH... is for God") - the ṢALĀH is FOR God.
- 21:72 "We gave him Isaac and Jacob as a gift, and all [of them] We made righteous" - the ṢALĀH is given to a RIGHTEOUS family. The ṢALĀH is the act of the righteous family.

PARALLELS:
- C5 (2:124 - "I am making you an Imam for the people"): C5 gave the IMAMATE (Abraham as Imam). D30 gives the LEADERSHIP (aʾimmatan) - the same imamate, now given to Abraham and his offspring. The ṢALĀH and the imamate are paired: the leader establishes the ṢALĀH.
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): C1's two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + zakah) and D30's three-pillar formula (good deeds + ṢALĀH + zakah) are the SAME structure with good deeds added. The ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE pillar.
- C10 (20:14 - "listen to what is revealed... establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance"): C10 showed the ṢALĀH as a REVELATION (wahy). D30 shows the ṢALĀH as an INSPIRATION (awḥaynā). The ṢALĀH is both revealed (C10) and inspired (D30). The revelation and the inspiration are the same: the ṢALĀH is God-given.
- 2:125 (C5 - "purify My House... and take from the standing-place of Abraham a MUSSALLA"): the MUSSALLA (standing-site) is where the leader establishes the ṢALĀH. The site (C5) and the leader (D30) are paired: the leader establishes the ṢALĀH at the site.
- D26 (9:71 - "believing men and women are allies of one another... establish the ṢALĀH"): D26 gave the ṢALĀH as the community-bond. D30 gives the ṢALĀH as the leadership-act. The ṢALĀH is both a community-bond (D26) and a leadership-act (D30).

FINDING: D30 gives the ṢALĀH as a LEADERSHIP-ACT: "We made them LEADERS guiding by Our command, and We inspired to them the doing of good deeds and the establishment of the ṢALĀH and the giving of the zakah." The ṢALĀH is INSPIRED (wahy) by God - the ṢALĀH is not a human invention; it is a DIVINE INSPIRATION. And the ṢALĀH-establishers are LEADERS (aʾimma) who GUIDE by God's command. The ṢALĀH is the act of a LEADER: the leader establishes the ṢALĀH and guides others. The ṢALĀH is one of THREE inspired acts (good deeds + ṢALĀH + zakah). And the ṢALĀH-establishers are WORSHIPPERS of God (ʿābidīn) - the ṢALĀH is the worship-act.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The LEADERSHIP (aʾimmatan) - the ṢALĀH-establishers are leaders who guide. HOLD for the final doc's leadership-table. (ii) The DIVINE INSPIRATION (awḥaynā) - the ṢALĀH is inspired by God. HOLD for the final doc's revelation-table. (iii) The THREE INSPIRED ACTS (good deeds + ṢALĀH + zakah) - the ṢALĀH is one of three inspired acts. HOLD for the final doc's act-table. (iv) The WORSHIP (ʿābidīn) - the ṢALĀH-establishers are worshippers. HOLD for the final doc's worship-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-SIX forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), and leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30). All twenty-six are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D31 (22:35 - those whose hearts tremble at the mention of God, and who establish salah) next.

## D31 - 22:35 - alladhina idha dhukira Allahu wajilat qulubuhum wa-s-sabirina 'ala ma asabahum wa-l-muqimi-s-salati wa mimmā razaqnāhum yunfiqun

ARABIC (flow): 22:34 "And for every community We have appointed a rite (manāsik) that they may mention the name of God over what He has provided for them of animal livestock. For your God is one God, so to Him submit; and give good tidings to the humble" -> **22:35 "THOSE WHO, WHEN GOD IS MENTIONED, THEIR HEARTS TREMBLE, AND THE PATIENT OVER WHAT AFFLICTS THEM, AND THE ESTABLISHERS OF THE SALAH, AND [THOSE WHO SPEND] FROM WHAT WE HAVE PROVIDED THEM"** -> 22:36 "And the camels and cattle - We have made them for you as among the symbols of God. In them is good for you. So mention the name of God over them when they are lined up; then when they fall on their sides, eat from them and feed the self-sufficient and the beggar. Thus We have subjected them to you, that you may be grateful."

WORD FORM: alladhīna (those who) idhā (when) dhukira (is mentioned) Allāhu (God) wajilat (tremble) qulūbuhum (their hearts) + wa-l-ṣābirīna (and the patient) ʿalā (over) mā (what) aṣābahum (afflicts them) + wa-l-muqīmī (and the establishers of) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + wa mimmā (and from what) razaqnāhum (We provided them) yunfiqūn (they spend).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Hajj (22). The verse sits in the sacrifice-passage (22:34-36): for every community We have appointed a rite (22:34), those who, when God is mentioned, their hearts tremble, and the patient, and the establishers of the ṢALĀH, and those who spend (22:35), and the camels and cattle are symbols of God (22:36).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE HEART-TREMBLING (wajilat qulūbuhum): "those who, when God is mentioned, their hearts TREMBLE." The ṢALĀH-establisher's heart TREMBLES when God is mentioned. The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose heart trembles at the mention of God. The same as 8:2 ("whose hearts tremble when God is mentioned") and 39:23 ("the skins of those who fear their Lord shiver"). The ṢALĀH is the act of the God-fearing: the heart trembles, faith increases, and reliance on God follows.
- THE PATIENCE (al-ṣābirīna ʿalā mā aṣābahum): "the patient over what afflicts them." The ṢALĀH-establisher is PATIENT over what afflicts him. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is patient in affliction. The same as 13:22 ("those who are patient, seeking the countenance of their Lord") and 2:153 ("seek help in patience and the ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH and patience are paired.
- THE ṢALĀH + SPENDING PAIR (AGAIN): "the establishers of the ṢALĀH and [those who spend] from what We have provided them." The SAME two-act definition as D1 (2:3), D21 (8:3), D29 (14:31). The ṢALĀH is the God-ward act; spending is the people-ward act. The two-act definition is STABLE across suras.
- 22:34 "for every community We have appointed a rite (manāsik) that they may mention the name of God" - the ṢALĀH is the rite (manāsik) of every community. The ṢALĀH is the universal rite: every community has its rite (manāsik), and the ṢALĀH is the rite of the believers. The same as C6 (14:37 "show us our rites (manāsik)") - the rites are SHOWN, not invented.
- 22:36 "the camels and cattle - We have made them for you as among the symbols of God... mention the name of God over them... eat from them and feed the self-sufficient and the beggar" - the ṢALĀH is paired with SACRIFICE (the camels/cattle are sacrificed with God's name mentioned). The ṢALĀH and the sacrifice are paired: the standing-toward-God (ṢALĀH) and the offering-to-God (sacrifice) are the two acts of worship. The same as 6:162 ("my ṢALĀH and my sacrifice... are for God") and C12's four-fold dedication.

PARALLELS:
- D21 (8:3 - "those who establish the ṢALĀH and spend from what We provided them"): D21 gave the ṢALĀH + SPENDING pair as the two-act definition of the TRUE BELIEVER. D31 repeats it with HEART-TREMBLING and PATIENCE added. The ṢALĀH + SPENDING pair is the CORE; heart-trembling and patience are the QUALITIES.
- D28 (13:22 - "those who are patient, seeking God's face, establish the ṢALĀH and spend, secretly and publicly, and repel evil with good"): D28 gave the ṢALĀH as the GOD-FACING act. D31 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of one whose HEART TREMBLES. The ṢALĀH is both God-facing (D28) and heart-trembling (D31).
- 8:2 (D21 - "whose hearts tremble when God is mentioned"): the same heart-trembling (wajilat) appears in both 8:2 and 22:35. The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose heart trembles. The same tremble-word (wajila) in both verses.
- C6 (14:37 - "show us our rites (manāsik)"): the rites are SHOWN. D31's "for every community We have appointed a rite" - the ṢALĀH is the universal rite. The ṢALĀH is the rite of every community.
- 6:162 (C12 - "my ṢALĀH and my sacrifice... are for God"): the ṢALĀH + sacrifice pair. D31's 22:36 (camels/cattle sacrificed with God's name mentioned) is the same pair. The ṢALĀH and the sacrifice are the two acts of worship.

FINDING: D31 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of one whose HEART TREMBLES when God is mentioned. The ṢALĀH is the act of the God-fearing: the heart trembles, faith increases, and reliance on God follows. The ṢALĀH is paired with PATIENCE (ṣabr) over affliction and SPENDING (from what God provided). The ṢALĀH + SPENDING pair is the CORE; heart-trembling and patience are the QUALITIES. And the ṢALĀH is the universal RITE (manāsik) of every community: "for every community We have appointed a rite." The ṢALĀH is the universal rite of the believers.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The HEART-TREMBLING (wajilat qulūbuhum) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one whose heart trembles when God is mentioned. HOLD for the final doc's heart-table. (ii) The UNIVERSAL RITE (manāsik) - the ṢALĀH is the rite of every community. HOLD for the final doc's universality-table. (iii) The SACRIFICE-PAIR (22:36) - the ṢALĀH is paired with sacrifice. HOLD for the final doc's sacrifice-table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-SEVEN forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), and heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31). All twenty-seven are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D32 (22:41 - if we enable them: establish salah + give zakah + enjoin right + forbid wrong) next.

## D32 - 22:41 - alladhina in makkanāhum fi-l-ardi aqamu-s-salata wa atu-z-zakata wa amaru bi-l-ma'rufi wa nahaw 'ani al-munkari

ARABIC (flow): 22:38 "Indeed God defends those who believe; indeed God does not love every treacherous ingrate" -> 22:39 "Permission [to fight] has been given to those who are fought, because they have been wronged. And indeed God is able to give them victory" -> 22:40 "Those who have been evicted from their homes without right - only because they say: Our Lord is God. And were it not that God checks the people, some by means of others, there would have been demolished monasteries, churches, synagogues (ṣalawāt), and mosques - [places] in which the name of God is mentioned abundantly. And God will surely support those who support Him. Indeed God is Powerful, Exalted in Might" -> **22:41 "THOSE WHO, IF WE GIVE THEM AUTHORITY IN THE LAND, ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND ENJOIN WHAT IS RIGHT AND FORBID WHAT IS WRONG. AND TO GOD BELONGS THE OUTCOME OF ALL MATTERS"** -> 22:42 "And if they deny you, then already have those before them denied: the people of Noah and 'Ad and Thamud."

WORD FORM: in (if) makkanāhum (We give them authority) fī al-arḍi (in the land) aqāmū (they establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) wa-ātū (and they give) al-zakāta (the zakah) wa-amarū (and they enjoin) bi-l-maʿrūfi (what is right) wa-nahaw (and they forbid) ʿani al-munkari (what is wrong) wa-li-llāhi (and to God belongs) ʿāqibatu (the outcome of) al-umūri (all matters).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Hajj (22). The verse sits in the fighting-passage (22:38-42): God defends the believers (22:38), permission to fight (22:39), the evicted and their protected sites (22:40), THOSE WHO, IF GIVEN AUTHORITY, ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND ENJOIN RIGHT AND FORBID WRONG (22:41), and if they deny you (22:42).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE FOUR-ACT FORMULA (WHEN EMPOWERED): (1) ESTABLISH THE SALAH, (2) GIVE THE ZAKAH, (3) ENJOIN WHAT IS RIGHT (amr bi-l-maʿrūf), (4) FORBID WHAT IS WRONG (nahy ʿan al-munkar). The ṢALĀH is act #1 - the FIRST act when given authority. The ṢALĀH is the FIRST act of the empowered: when you are given authority in the land, the first thing you do is establish the ṢALĀH. The same as C19 (31:17: establish ṢALĀH + enjoin right + forbid wrong + patience) and D26 (9:71: enjoin right + forbid wrong + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey). The ṢALĀH is always in the formula, and here it is FIRST.
- "IF WE GIVE THEM AUTHORITY IN THE LAND" (in makkanāhum fī al-arḍi): the ṢALĀH is established when given AUTHORITY. The ṢALĀH is the act of those who have been given authority in the land. The ṢALĀH is the first act of the empowered: when you are given authority, you establish the ṢALĀH first. The ṢALĀH is the priority-act of the empowered.
- "AND TO GOD BELONGS THE OUTCOME OF ALL MATTERS" (wa-li-llāhi ʿāqibatu al-umūri): the ṢALĀH is established, but the OUTCOME belongs to God. The ṢALĀH is the human act; the outcome is God's. The ṢALĀH is the standing-toward-God; the outcome is God's to decide. The same as D29 (14:31 "before a Day comes in which there is no bargaining") - the ṢALĀH is the pre-deadline act; the outcome is God's.
- 22:40 "the mosques of God are... [places] in which the name of God is mentioned abundantly" - the ṢALĀH-sites (ṣalawāt, 22:40) are protected by God's defense of the believers (22:38). The ṢALĀH-site (A8) and the ṢALĀH-act (D32) are paired: the site is protected; the act is performed.
- 22:38 "Indeed God defends those who believe" - the ṢALĀH is the act of those whom God defends. The ṢALĀH is the act of the defended.

PARALLELS:
- C19 (31:17 - "establish the ṢALĀH, enjoin right, forbid wrong, be patient"): C19's four-command formula (ṢALĀH + enjoin + forbid + patience) and D32's four-act formula (ṢALĀH + zakah + enjoin + forbid) are the SAME structure with zakah added. The ṢALĀH is the FIRST act in both.
- D26 (9:71 - "believing men and women are allies... enjoin right + forbid wrong + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey"): D26 gave the ṢALĀH as the MIDDLE act (act #3 of 5). D32 gives the ṢALĀH as the FIRST act (act #1 of 4). The ṢALĀH is both the middle act (D26) and the first act (D32). The ṢALĀH is both the core and the priority.
- D23 (9:11 - "if they repent and establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah, then they are your brothers in religion"): D23 gave the ṢALĀH as the brotherhood-condition. D32 gives the ṢALĀH as the first act of the empowered. The ṢALĀH is both the condition for brotherhood (D23) and the first act of authority (D32).
- C5 (2:125 - MUSSALLA, the standing-site): the SITE where the empowered establish the ṢALĀH. The MUSSALLA (standing-site) and the ṢALĀH (standing-act) are paired: the site is where the act is performed.
- A8 (22:40 - ṢALAWĀT as other communities' houses of Name-mention): the ṢALĀH-sites (ṣalawāt) are protected by God's defense. The site (A8) and the act (D32) are paired: the site is protected; the act is performed.

FINDING: D32 gives the ṢALĀH as the FIRST act of the EMPOWERED: "if We give them authority in the land, they ESTABLISH THE SALAH and give the zakah and enjoin right and forbid wrong." The ṢALĀH is the FIRST act when given authority: when you are given authority in the land, the first thing you do is establish the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the PRIORITY-act of the empowered. And the outcome belongs to God ("to God belongs the outcome of all matters") - the ṢALĀH is the human act; the outcome is God's.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The FIRST-ACT-OF-THE-EMPOWERED (22:41) - the ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority. HOLD for the final doc's power-table. (ii) The FOUR-ACT FORMULA (22:41) - ṢALĀH + zakah + enjoin right + forbid wrong. HOLD for the final doc's formula-table. (iii) The OUTCOME-BELONGS-TO-GOD (22:41) - the ṢALĀH is the human act; the outcome is God's. HOLD for the final doc's outcome-table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-NINE forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), and first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32). All twenty-nine are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D33 (22:78 - ???) next.

## D33 - 22:78 - wa-jahidu fi Allahi haqqa jihadihi... fa-aqimu-s-salata wa-atu-z-zakata wa-i'tasimu bi-llahi

ARABIC (flow): 22:77 "O you who believe, bow and prostrate and worship your Lord and do good, that you may be successful" -> **22:78 "AND STRIVE FOR GOD WITH THE STRIVING DUE TO HIM. He has chosen you and has NOT PLACED UPON YOU IN THE RELIGION ANY DIFFICULTY - the religion of your father, Abraham. He named you Muslims before and in this, that the Messenger may be a witness over you and you may be witnesses over the people. SO ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND HOLD FAST TO GOD. He is your protector; and excellent is the protector, and excellent is the helper."**

WORD FORM: wa-jāhidū (and strive) fī Allāhi (for God) ḥaqqa (with the) jihādihī (striving due to Him) + huwa (He) ajtabākum (has chosen you) + wa-mā (and has not) jaʿala (placed) ʿalaykum (upon you) fī al-dīni (in the religion) min ḥarajin (any difficulty) + millata (the religion of) abīkum (your father) Ibrāhīma (Abraham) + huwa (He) sammākum (named you) al-muslimīna (Muslims) min qabli (before) wa-fī (and in) hādhā (this) + li-yakūna (that) al-rasūlu (the Messenger) shahīdan (a witness) ʿalaykum (over you) wa (and) takūnū (you may be) shuhadāʾa (witnesses) ʿalā (over) al-nāsi (the people) + fa-aqīmū (so establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) wa-ātū (and give) al-zakāta (the zakah) wa-iʿtaṣimū (and hold fast) bi-llāhi (to God) + huwa (He is) mawlākum (your protector); fa-niʿma (and excellent is) al-mawlā (the protector), wa-niʿma (and excellent is) al-naṣīr (the helper).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Hajj (22). The verse sits in the striving-passage (22:77-78): bow, prostrate, worship, do good (22:77), strive for God, no difficulty in the religion of Abraham, named Muslims, establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and hold fast to God (22:78).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- "NO DIFFICULTY IN THE RELIGION" (mā jaʿala ʿalaykum fī al-dīni min ḥarajin): the ṢALĀH is part of a religion that has NO DIFFICULTY. The ṢALĀH is not a burden; it is an ease. The same as 73:20 ("He turned to you in mercy; recite what is easy") and 5:6 ("God does not intend to make difficulty for you, but He intends to purify you"). The ṢALĀH is EASY, not difficult. The ṢALĀH is a religion of ease.
- "THE RELIGION OF YOUR FATHER, ABRAHAM" (millata abīkum Ibrāhīma): the ṢALĀH is the religion of Abraham. The ṢALĀH is the ABRAHAMIC standing. The same as 2:135 ("the religion of Abraham, upright") and 6:161 ("the religion of Abraham, upright"). The ṢALĀH is the religion of Abraham.
- "HE NAMED YOU MUSLIMS" (sammākum al-muslimīna): the ṢALĀH is the act of those who are named "Muslims" (submitters). The ṢALĀH is the act of the Muslim. The same as C5 (2:128 "make us submitters") and C12 (6:163 "I am the first of the submitters"). The ṢALĀH is the act of the submitter.
- THE ṢALĀH + ZAKAH + HOLD-FAST (fa-aqīmū al-ṣalāta wa-ātū al-zakāta wa-iʿtaṣimū bi-llāhi): the ṢALĀH + ZAKAH pair (C1's two-pillar formula) now with a THIRD element: HOLD FAST TO GOD (iʿtaṣimū bi-llāhi). The ṢALĀH is paired with holding fast to God. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who holds fast to God. The same as D30 (21:73 "We made them leaders... and they were worshippers of Us") - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who worships God.
- 22:77 "bow and prostrate and worship your Lord and do good, that you may be successful" - the ṢALĀH is paired with bowing (rukūʿ), prostration (sujūd), worship (ʿibādah), and doing good. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who bows, prostrates, worships, and does good.

PARALLELS:
- C5 (2:125 - MUSSALLA, the standing-site): the MUSSALLA is the standing-site at the House. The ṢALĀH is the act performed at the MUSSALLA. The site and the act are paired.
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): the SAME two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + ZAKAH). D33 adds HOLD FAST TO GOD as the third element.
- C12 (6:162 - "my ṢALĀH... is for God"): the ṢALĀH is FOR God. D33's "hold fast to God" is the same: the ṢALĀH is the act of holding fast to God.
- D30 (21:73 - "We made them leaders... and they were worshippers of Us"): D30 gave the ṢALĀH as a LEADERSHIP-act. D33 gives the ṢALĀH as an act of one who HOLDS FAST to God. The ṢALĀH is both a leadership-act (D30) and a holding-fast-act (D33).
- 22:77 "bow and prostrate and worship your Lord and do good" - the ṢALĀH is paired with bowing, prostration, worship, and doing good. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who bows, prostrates, worships, and does good.

FINDING: D33 gives the ṢALĀH as an act of EASE: "He has NOT PLACED UPON YOU IN THE RELIGION ANY DIFFICULTY." The ṢALĀH is part of a religion that has NO DIFFICULTY. The ṢALĀH is EASY, not difficult. The ṢALĀH is the religion of Abraham (millata abīkum Ibrāhīma). The ṢALĀH is the act of those named "Muslims" (sammākum al-muslimīna). And the ṢALĀH is paired with HOLDING FAST TO GOD (iʿtaṣimū bi-llāhi): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who holds fast to God. The ṢALĀH is the anti-difficulty act: the religion has no difficulty, and the ṢALĀH is the easy act.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The NO-DIFFICULTY (mā jaʿala ʿalaykum fī al-dīni min ḥarajin) - the religion has no difficulty. HOLD for the final doc's ease-table. (ii) The ABRAHAMIC RELIGION (millata abīkum Ibrāhīma) - the ṢALĀH is the religion of Abraham. HOLD for the final doc's tradition-table. (iii) The MUSLIM NAME (sammākum al-muslimīna) - the ṢALĀH is the act of those named "Muslims." HOLD for the final doc's identity-table. (iv) The HOLD-FAST (iʿtaṣimū bi-llāhi) - the ṢALĀH is paired with holding fast to God. HOLD for the final doc's holding-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), and no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33). All thirty are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D34 (24:37 - ???) next.

## D34 - 24:37 - rijālun lā tulhīhim tijāratun wa-lā bayʿun ʿan dhikri Allāhi wa-iqāmi al-ṣalāti wa-ītāʾi al-zakāti

ARABIC (flow): 24:35 "God is the light of the heavens and the earth. The example of His light is like a niche within which is a lamp; the lamp is within glass; the glass as if it were a pearly star lit from a blessed olive tree, neither of the east nor of the west, whose oil would almost glow even if untouched by fire. Light upon light. God guides to His light whom He wills. And God presents examples for the people, and God is Knowing of all things" -> 24:36 "In houses which God has permitted to be raised, in which His name is mentioned, He is exalted in them, morning and evening" -> **24:37 "MEN WHOM NEITHER TRADE NOR SALE DISTRACTS FROM THE REMEMBRANCE OF GOD AND THE ESTABLISHMENT OF THE SALAH AND THE GIVING OF THE ZAKAH. They fear a Day when hearts and eyes will be overturned"** -> 24:38 "That God may reward them according to the best of what they did and increase them from His bounty. And God provides for whom He wills without account" -> 24:39-40 "And those who disbelieve - their deeds are like a mirage in a lowland... or like darkness in a deep sea."

WORD FORM: lā (not) tulhīhim (distracts them) tijāratun (trade) wa-lā (nor) bayʿun (sale) ʿan (from) dhikri (the remembrance of) Allāhi (God) wa-iqāmi (and the establishment of) al-ṣalāti (the ṣalāh) wa-ītāʾi (and the giving of) al-zakāti (the zakah) + yakhāfūna (they fear) yawman (a Day) tataqallabu (will be overturned) fīhi (in it) al-qulūbu (the hearts) wa-l-abṣāru (and the eyes).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah an-Nur (24). The verse sits in the light-passage (24:35-40): God is the light (24:35), in houses where His name is mentioned (24:36), men whom neither trade nor sale distracts from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH and the giving of the zakah (24:37), God rewards them (24:38), the disbelievers' deeds are like a mirage (24:39-40).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE NON-DISTRACTION (lā tulhīhim tijāratun wa-lā bayʿun): "neither TRADE NOR SALE distracts them." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is NOT distracted by trade and sale. The ṢALĀH is the anti-distraction act: the stander is not distracted by commerce. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who prioritizes God over commerce. The same as D3 (2:177 "those are the ones who have been truthful") and D28 (13:22 "those who are patient, seeking God's face, establish the ṢALĀH and spend").
- THE THREE-ELEMENT FORMULA (dhikr + ṢALĀH + zakah): "neither trade nor sale distracts them FROM THE REMEMBRANCE OF GOD AND THE ESTABLISHMENT OF THE SALAH AND THE GIVING OF THE ZAKAH." The ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE element: (1) remembrance of God (dhikr), (2) establishment of ṢALĀH, (3) giving of zakah. The ṢALĀH is embedded in remembrance and giving: the ṢALĀH is the standing-state between the remembrance (God-ward) and the giving (people-ward).
- "THEY FEAR A DAY WHEN HEARTS AND EYES WILL BE OVERTURNED" (yakhāfūna yawman tataqallabu fīhi al-qulūbu wa-l-abṣāru): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears the Day of Reckoning. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is oriented toward the Hereafter. The same as C7 (14:41 "on the Day the reckoning is established") and C10 (20:15 "the Hour is coming").
- 24:36 "In houses which God has permitted to be raised, in which His name is mentioned, He is exalted in them, morning and evening" - the ṢALĀH is performed in HOUSES where God's name is mentioned. The ṢALĀH is the act of Name-mentioning. The same as A8 (22:40 - houses of Name-mention) and C5 (2:125 - MUSSALLA, the standing-site). The ṢALĀH is the act performed in the house of Name-mention.
- 24:35 (light-verse): "God is the light of the heavens and the earth" - the ṢALĀH is performed in the LIGHT of God. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who stands in God's light. The same as B3 (33:43 "He brings you out of darkness into light") - the ṢALĀH is the act that brings you into the light.

PARALLELS:
- D1 (2:3 - "those who believe in the unseen and establish the ṢALĀH and spend"): D1 gave the ṢALĀH + SPENDING pair. D34 gives the ṢALĀH + DHIKR + ZAKAH three-element formula. The ṢALĀH is both paired with spending (D1) and embedded in dhikr+zakah (D34). The ṢALĀH is the middle element: dhikr (God-ward) -> ṢALĀH (standing) -> zakah (people-ward).
- D3 (2:177 - birr: 10-item character list): D3's 10-item list and D34's 3-element formula are the SAME structure: the ṢALĀH is embedded in a comprehensive character. D34's formula is more focused (dhikr + ṢALĀH + zakah).
- D28 (13:22 - "those who are patient, seeking God's face, establish the ṢALĀH and spend, secretly and publicly, and repel evil with good"): D28 gave the ṢALĀH as the GOD-FACING act. D34 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of one who is NOT DISTRACTED by trade. The ṢALĀH is both God-facing (D28) and undistracted (D34).
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): C1's two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + zakah) and D34's three-element formula (dhikr + ṢALĀH + zakah) are the SAME structure with dhikr added. The ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE element in both.
- B3 (33:41-42 - "remember God with much remembrance; glorify Him morning and evening" -> divine ṢALĀH): D34's "neither trade nor sale distracts them from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH" is the HUMAN counterpart of B3's divine ṢALĀH. The human dhikr + ṢALĀH invites the divine ṢALĀH.
- 24:36 "In houses which God has permitted to be raised, in which His name is mentioned" - the ṢALĀH is performed in houses of Name-mention. The same as A8 (22:40) and C5 (2:125). The ṢALĀH-site is a house of Name-mention.

FINDING: D34 gives the ṢALĀH as the NON-DISTRACTED act: "neither trade nor sale distracts them from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH and the giving of the zakah." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is NOT distracted by commerce. The ṢALĀH is the priority-act: God over commerce. And the ṢALĀH is embedded in a THREE-ELEMENT formula: (1) remembrance of God (dhikr), (2) establishment of ṢALĀH, (3) giving of zakah. The ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE element: dhikr (God-ward) -> ṢALĀH (standing) -> zakah (people-ward). The ṢALĀH is performed in houses of Name-mention (24:36) and in the light of God (24:35). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who stands in God's light, in a house of Name-mention, without distraction from commerce.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The NON-DISTRACTION (24:37) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is not distracted by trade and sale. HOLD for the final doc's priority-table. (ii) The THREE-ELEMENT FORMULA (dhikr + ṢALĀH + zakah) - the ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE element. HOLD for the final doc's formula-table. (iii) The HOUSES OF NAME-MENTION (24:36) - the ṢALĀH is performed in houses where God's name is mentioned. HOLD for the final doc's site-table (A8, C5, D34). (iv) The LIGHT-VERSE (24:35) - the ṢALĀH is performed in the light of God. HOLD for the final doc's light-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-FIVE forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), and non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34). All twenty-five are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D35 (24:56 - establish salah + obey God and His Messenger) next.

## D35 - 24:56 - wa-aqimu-s-salata wa-atu-z-zakata wa-ati'u-r-rasula la'allakum turhamun

ARABIC (flow): 24:55 "God has promised those who have believed among you and done righteous deeds that He will surely grant them succession (istikhlāf) in the land as He granted it to those before them; and He will surely establish for them their religion which He has chosen for them; and He will surely replace for them, after their fear, security. [They will] worship Me, not associating anything with Me. And whoever disbelieves after that - then those are the defiantly disobedient" -> **24:56 "AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND OBEY THE MESSENGER, THAT YOU MAY RECEIVE MERCY"** -> 24:57 "Do not think that those who disbelieve will escape in the land; their refuge will be the Fire, and how evil a destination."

WORD FORM: wa-aqīmū (and establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + wa-ātū (and give) al-zakāta (the zakah) + wa-aṭīʿū (and obey) al-rasūla (the Messenger) + laʿallakum (that you may) turḥamūn (receive mercy).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah an-Nur (24). The verse sits in the succession-passage (24:55-57): God has promised succession in the land and security (24:55), ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND OBEY THE MESSENGER, that you may receive mercy (24:56), the disbelievers will not escape - their refuge is the Fire (24:57).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ṢALĀH + ZAKAH + OBEDIENCE FORMULA: "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and obey the Messenger." The ṢALĀH + ZAKAH pair (C1's two-pillar formula) now has a THIRD element: OBEY THE MESSENGER (aṭīʿū al-rasūla). The ṢALĀH is paired with obedience to the Messenger. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who obeys the Messenger. The same as D26 (9:71 - enjoin right + forbid wrong + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey God and His Messenger). The ṢALĀH is always paired with obedience to God and His Messenger.
- "THAT YOU MAY RECEIVE MERCY" (laʿallakum turḥamūn): the ṢALĀH is the act that earns MERCY (raḥmah). The same as B2 (ṢALAWĀT + raḥmah), B3 (ṢALĀH + raḥmah), B5 (ṢALĀH + sakan). The ṢALĀH always ends in mercy on the God-side. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who seeks mercy.
- 24:55 "God has promised those who have believed... succession in the land... He will establish for them their religion... after their fear, security. [They will] worship Me, not associating anything with Me" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the SUCCESSORS (istikhlāf) in the land. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who has been given succession in the land. The same as D32 (22:41 - if we enable them: establish ṢALĀH). The ṢALĀH is the act of the empowered/successors. And the ṢALĀH is the act of one who WORSHIPS God alone (not associating anything with Him).
- 24:57 "Do not think that those who disbelieve will escape in the land; their refuge will be the Fire" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is NOT the Fire-refuge. The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose refuge is NOT the Fire. The same as A-cluster (fire-delivery for ṢALĀH-loss) and B7 (9:84 - the ṢALĀH is withheld from the disbelievers).

PARALLELS:
- D26 (9:71 - "believing men and women are allies... enjoin right + forbid wrong + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey God and His Messenger"): D26 gave the five-act formula (enjoin + forbid + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey). D35 gives the three-element formula (ṢALĀH + zakah + obey). The ṢALĀH is always paired with zakah and obedience. The ṢALĀH is both the middle act (D26) and the first act (D35).
- D32 (22:41 - "if we enable them: establish ṢALĀH + give zakah + enjoin right + forbid wrong"): D32 gave the first-act-of-the-empowered. D35 gives the act-of-the-successors. The ṢALĀH is both the first act of the empowered (D32) and the act of the successors (D35).
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): the SAME two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + ZAKAH). D35 adds OBEY THE MESSENGER as the third element. The ṢALĀH is always paired with zakah and obedience.
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): the divine ṢALAWĀT is granted to those who ESTABLISH the ṢALĀH (D35). The human ṢALĀH (established) invites the divine ṢALAWĀT. And the ṢALĀH is paired with mercy (D35 "that you may receive mercy").
- 24:55 "God has promised those who have believed... succession in the land" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors. The same as D30 (21:73 - leaders guiding: establish ṢALĀH). The ṢALĀH is the act of the leaders/successors.
- 24:57 "their refuge will be the Fire" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one whose refuge is NOT the Fire. The same as A-cluster (fire-delivery for ṢALĀH-loss).

FINDING: D35 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the SUCCESSORS: "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and obey the Messenger, that you may receive mercy." The ṢALĀH is paired with obedience to the Messenger and earns MERCY. The ṢALĀH is the act of the successors (istikhlāf) in the land - the same as D32 (22:41 - if we enable them: establish ṢALĀH). The ṢALĀH is the act of the empowered/successors. And the ṢALĀH is the act of one whose refuge is NOT the Fire (24:57) - the ṢALĀH is the anti-Fire act.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The SUCCESSION (istikhlāf, 24:55) - the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors. HOLD for the final doc's succession-table. (ii) The OBEDIENCE (aṭīʿū al-rasūla, 24:56) - the ṢALĀH is paired with obedience to the Messenger. HOLD for the final doc's obedience-table. (iii) The MERCY (turḥamūn, 24:56) - the ṢALĀH earns mercy. HOLD for the final doc's mercy-table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-SIX forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), and successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35). All twenty-six are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D36 (24:58 - salah as clock of life) next.

## D36 - 24:58 - salah as clock of life (min qabli salati-l-fajr... wa min ba'di salati-l-'isha)

ARABIC (flow): 24:57 "Do not think that those who disbelieve will escape in the land; their refuge will be the Fire, and how evil a destination" -> **24:58 "O you who believe, let those whom your right hands possess and those who have not reached puberty ask permission of you [before entering] at THREE TIMES: BEFORE THE DAWN SALAH (min qabli ṣalāti al-fajr) and when you put aside your clothing at noon and AFTER THE NIGHT SALAH (wa-min baʿdi ṣalāti al-ʿishāʾi). These are three times of privacy for you; there is no blame on you nor on them after these three. They move among you, some of them among others. Thus God makes clear the signs for you. And God is Knowing and Wise"** -> 24:59 "And when the children among you reach puberty, let them ask permission, as those before them asked permission. Thus God makes clear His signs for you. And God is Knowing and Wise" -> 24:60 "And the post-menopausal women who do not hope for marriage - there is no blame upon them to put aside their outer garments, [being] not flaunting their adornment. But to modestly refrain [from that] is better for them. And God is Hearing and Knowing" -> 24:61 "There is no blame upon the blind, nor upon the lame, nor upon the sick, nor upon yourselves, to eat from your houses... But when you enter houses, greet one another with a greeting from God, blessed and good. Thus God makes clear the signs for you, that you may reason."

WORD FORM: min qabli (before) ṣalāti (the ṣalāh of) al-fajr (dawn) + wa-min baʿdi (and after) ṣalāti (the ṣalāh of) al-ʿishāʾi (night). The ṢALĀH is the TIME-MARKER of the household's privacy rules.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah an-Nur (24). The verse sits in the household-passage (24:58-61): the permission rules for entering houses (24:58), children reaching puberty (24:59), post-menopausal women (24:60), eating from houses and greeting (24:61).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE HOUSEHOLD-CLOCK (ṣalātu al-fajr + ṣalātu al-ʿishāʾ): "BEFORE THE DAWN SALAH... and AFTER THE NIGHT SALAH." The household has THREE times of privacy: (1) BEFORE THE DAWN SALAH, (2) at noon (when you put aside your clothing), (3) AFTER THE NIGHT SALAH. The ṢALĀH is the TIME-MARKER of the household's privacy rules. The household's privacy is defined BY the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the household's daily life.
- THE TEMPORAL ANCHOR OF THE HOUSEHOLD: the ṢALĀH is the act that structures the household's daily routine. The household's privacy rules are anchored to the ṢALĀH: the times of privacy are "before the dawn ṢALĀH" and "after the night ṢALĀH." The ṢALĀH is not just a ritual; it is the CLOCK of daily life. The same as D17 (5:106 - the ṢALĀH anchors legal proceedings) and C14 (17:78 - the ṢALĀH's timed boundaries). The ṢALĀH is the temporal anchor of the community's life.
- THE SALĀM-GREETING (24:61 "greet one another with a greeting from God, blessed and good"): the ṢALĀH is paired with the SALĀM-greeting (the household greeting). The ṢALĀH and the SALĀM are paired: the ṢALĀH is the standing-act; the SALĀM is the greeting-act. The same as B3 (33:43-44 - divine ṢALĀH -> SALĀM-greeting) and B4 (33:56 - SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN).
- 24:59 "when the children among you reach puberty, let them ask permission" - the ṢALĀH is the marker of ADULTHOOD: the children's permission is tied to puberty, and the household's privacy is tied to the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the marker of the transition to adulthood.
- 24:60 "the post-menopausal women" - the ṢALĀH is the marker of the life-cycle: the household's rules are tied to the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the act that structures the life-cycle.

PARALLELS:
- D17 (5:106 - "detain them after the ṢALĀH"): D17 gave the ṢALĀH as a LEGAL anchor (witnesses detained after the ṢALĀH). D36 gives the ṢALĀH as a HOUSEHOLD anchor (privacy rules tied to the ṢALĀH). The ṢALĀH is both a legal anchor (D17) and a household anchor (D36). The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the community's life: legal, household, and personal.
- C14 (17:78 - "establish the ṢALĀH at the decline of the sun until the darkness of the night, and the Quran of dawn"): C14 gave the ṢALĀH's TIMED boundaries. D36 gives the ṢALĀH's HOUSEHOLD timing: the times of privacy are defined by the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH's time is the household's time.
- B3 (33:43-44 - divine ṢALĀH -> SALĀM-greeting): D36's "greet one another with a greeting from God, blessed and good" (24:61) is the HUMAN counterpart of B3's divine SALĀM-greeting. The ṢALĀH and the SALĀM are paired: the ṢALĀH is the standing-act; the SALĀM is the greeting-act.
- B4 (33:56 - SALLIMŪ TASLĪMAN): the ṢALĀH + SALĀM pairing appears again. The ṢALĀH is the standing-toward; the SALĀM is the surrender. The household greeting is the earthly form of the eschatological SALĀM-greeting.
- C5 (2:125 - MUSSALLA, the standing-site): the ṢALĀH is performed in the HOUSEHOLD (houses). The ṢALĀH-site is the household. The same as A8 (22:40 - houses of Name-mention) and D34 (24:36 - houses where God's name is mentioned). The ṢALĀH is performed in the household.

FINDING: D36 gives the ṢALĀH as the HOUSEHOLD-CLOCK: "before the DAWN SALAH... and after the NIGHT SALAH." The household's privacy rules are anchored to the ṢALĀH: the times of privacy are "before the dawn ṢALĀH" and "after the night ṢALĀH." The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the household's daily life. The ṢALĀH is not just a ritual; it is the CLOCK of daily life: legal (D17), household (D36), and personal (C14). The ṢALĀH is the temporal anchor of the community's life. And the ṢALĀH is paired with the SALĀM-greeting (24:61): the ṢALĀH is the standing-act; the SALĀM is the greeting-act.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The HOUSEHOLD-CLOCK (24:58) - the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules. HOLD for the final doc's clock-table. (ii) The SALĀM-GREETING (24:61) - the ṢALĀH is paired with the household greeting. HOLD for the final doc's greeting-table. (iii) The ADULTHOOD-MARKER (24:59) - the ṢALĀH is the marker of the transition to adulthood. HOLD for the final doc's life-cycle-table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-SEVEN forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), and household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36). All twenty-seven are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D37 (27:3 - those who establish salah + they with certainty) next.

## D37 - 27:3 - alladhina yuqimu-s-salata wa yu'tu-z-zakata wa hum bi-l-akhirati hum yuqinun

ARABIC (flow): 27:1 "Ta Sin. These are the verses of the Quran and a clear Book" -> 27:2 "A guidance and good tidings for the believers" -> **27:3 "Those who ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND THEY, WITH REGARD TO THE HEREAFTER, ARE CERTAIN (yūqinūn)"** -> 27:4 "Indeed those who do not believe in the Hereafter - We have made their deeds seem fair to them, so they wander blindly" -> 27:5 "Those are the ones for whom is a painful punishment, and they are the losers in the Hereafter" -> 27:6 "Indeed you are receiving the Quran from [the presence of] a Wise, Knowing God."

WORD FORM: yuqīmūna (establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + yuʾtūna (give) al-zakāta (the zakah) + wa-hum (and they) bi-l-ākhirati (with regard to the Hereafter) hum (are) yūqinūn (certain). The ṢALĀH is established by those who are CERTAIN (yūqinūn) of the Hereafter.

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah an-Naml (27). The verse sits in the opening of Surah 27 (27:1-6): the Quran is a clear Book (27:1), guidance and good tidings for the believers (27:2), those who establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and are certain of the Hereafter (27:3), those who do not believe in the Hereafter wander blindly (27:4-5), the Quran is received from a Wise, Knowing God (27:6).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ṢALĀH + ZAKAH PAIR (AGAIN): "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah" - the SAME two-pillar formula as C1 (98:5), C2 (2:43), etc. The formula is STABLE across suras. D37 adds CERTAINTY (yūqinūn) regarding the Hereafter as the third element.
- THE CERTAINTY (yūqinūn): "they, with regard to the Hereafter, are CERTAIN (yūqinūn)." The ṢALĀH is established by those who have CERTAINTY (yaqīn) of the Hereafter. The same as D1 (2:3 "those who believe in the unseen") and D19 (6:92 "those who believe in the Hereafter believe in it, and they maintain their ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who has certainty of the Hereafter. The ṢALĀH is the act of the Hereafter-certain.
- 27:2 "A guidance and good tidings for the believers" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the believers. The ṢALĀH is the guidance and good tidings for the believers. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who follows the guidance and receives the good tidings.
- 27:4 "those who do not believe in the Hereafter - We have made their deeds seem fair to them, so they wander blindly" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who believes in the Hereafter; the ṢALĀH-loser does not believe in the Hereafter and wanders blindly. The ṢALĀH is the divider between the Hereafter-believer and the Hereafter-denier. The same as A-cluster (fire-delivery for ṢALĀH-loss) and D19 (6:92 - the ṢALĀH-guardian is prepared for death).
- 27:5 "those are the ones for whom is a painful punishment, and they are the losers in the Hereafter" - the ṢALĀH-losers are the losers in the Hereafter. The ṢALĀH is the act that determines the Hereafter-outcome. The same as C1 (98:6-7 - worst vs best creatures) and D4 (2:277 - no fear, no grief).
- 27:6 "Indeed you are receiving the Quran from [the presence of] a Wise, Knowing God" - the ṢALĀH is part of the Quran's guidance, received from the Wise and Knowing God. The same as D12 (4:162 "the firm in knowledge... establishers of the ṢALĀH") and D20 (7:170 "hold fast to the Book and establish the ṢALĀH").

PARALLELS:
- D1 (2:3 - "those who believe in the unseen and establish the ṢALĀH and spend"): D1 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of the unseen-believer. D37 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the Hereafter-certain. The ṢALĀH is both the unseen-believer's act (D1) and the Hereafter-certain's act (D37). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who believes in the invisible (unseen/Hereafter).
- D19 (6:92 - "those who believe in the Hereafter believe in it, and they maintain their ṢALĀH"): D19 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of the Hereafter-believer. D37 repeats it: the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is CERTAIN of the Hereafter. The ṢALĀH is the marker of Hereafter-certainty.
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): the SAME two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + ZAKAH). D37 adds CERTAINTY (yūqinūn) as the third element.
- D4 (2:277 - "those who believe and establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah: no fear, no grief"): D4 gave the ṢALĀH's PROMISE (no fear, no grief). D37 gives the ṢALĀH's CONDITION (certainty of the Hereafter). The ṢALĀH-establisher is both fearless/griefless (D4) and certain of the Hereafter (D37).
- A-cluster (fire-delivery for ṢALĀH-loss): the ṢALĀH-loser does not believe in the Hereafter (27:4) and wanders blindly. The ṢALĀH is the divider: the ṢALĀH-establisher is certain of the Hereafter; the ṢALĀH-loser denies it.

FINDING: D37 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the HEREAFTER-CERTAIN: "those who establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and they, with regard to the Hereafter, are CERTAIN (yūqinūn)." The ṢALĀH is established by those who have CERTAINTY (yaqīn) of the Hereafter. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter. And the ṢALĀH is the divider: the ṢALĀH-establisher is certain of the Hereafter; the ṢALĀH-loser does not believe in the Hereafter and wanders blindly (27:4-5). The ṢALĀH is the act that determines the Hereafter-outcome.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The CERTAINTY (yūqinūn) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter. HOLD for the final doc's certainty-table. (ii) The HEREAFTER-BELIEF (27:4) - the ṢALĀH is the divider between the Hereafter-believer and the Hereafter-denier. HOLD for the final doc's belief-table. (iii) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-EIGHT forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), and Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37). All twenty-eight are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D38 (30:31 - turn to Him + establish salah + be not of the polytheists) next.
