## D38 - 30:31 - munibina ilayhi wa-ittaquhu wa-aqimu-s-salata wa-la takunu min al-mushrikin

ARABIC (flow): 30:29 "Rather, those who do wrong follow their desires without knowledge. So who will guide one whom God has let stray? And they will have no helpers" -> 30:30 "So set your face toward the religion, upright (ḥanīfan) - the fitrah of God upon which He has created mankind. No change should there be in the creation of God. That is the upright religion, but most of the people do not know" -> **30:31 "TURNING TO HIM (munībīna ilayhi) AND FEAR HIM AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND DO NOT BE OF THE POLYTHEISTS (al-mushrikīn)"** -> 30:32 "Those who have divided their religion and become sects, each faction rejoicing in what it has" -> 30:33 "And when harm touches the people, they call upon their Lord, turning to Him; then when He gives them a taste of His mercy, a group of them associate partners with their Lord."

WORD FORM: munībīna (turning, active participle) ilayhi (to Him) + wa-ittaquhu (and fear Him) + wa-aqīmū (and establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + wa-lā (and do not) takūnū (be) min (of) al-mushrikīn (the polytheists).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah ar-Rum (30). The verse sits in the religion-passage (30:29-33): the wrongdoers follow their desires (30:29), set your face toward the religion, upright, the fitrah of God (30:30), turning to Him, fear Him, establish the ṢALĀH, do not be polytheists (30:31), those who divided their religion (30:32), when harm touches, they turn to God (30:33).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE TURNING (munībīna ilayhi): "turning to Him" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who TURNS to God (munīb). The ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act. The same as A19 (fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name) and D28 (seeking God's face). The ṢALĀH is the turning-toward-God.
- THE ṢALĀH + FEAR + NON-POLYTHEISM: "fear Him and establish the ṢALĀH and do not be of the polytheists." The ṢALĀH is paired with FEAR of God and NON-POLYTHEISM. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God and is not a polytheist. The ṢALĀH is the anti-polytheism act. The same as C6 (14:35 "keep me and my sons away from worshipping idols") and C19 (31:13 "do not associate partners with God"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is not a polytheist.
- 30:30 "set your face toward the religion, upright (ḥanīfan) - the fitrah of God upon which He has created mankind" - the ṢALĀH is part of the UPRIGHT religion (ḥanīf), which is the FITRAH (innate nature) of God. The ṢALĀH is the act that follows the fitrah. The same as C1 (98:5 "upright (ḥanīfan)") and 2:135 ("the religion of Abraham, upright"). The ṢALĀH is the fitrah-act.
- 30:32 "Those who have divided their religion and become sects" - the ṢALĀH is the act that UNIFIES; the sectarianism divides. The ṢALĀH is the anti-sectarian act. The same as 6:159 ("those who divided their religion... you have nothing to do with them").
- 30:33 "when harm touches the people, they call upon their Lord, turning to Him; then when He gives them a taste of His mercy, a group of them associate partners with their Lord" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who turns to God in distress and does not associate partners when mercy comes. The ṢALĀH is the act of the one who is consistent in turning to God.

PARALLELS:
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): C1's two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + zakah) and D38's formula (fear + ṢALĀH + non-polytheism) are the SAME structure: the ṢALĀH is paired with a complementary act. The ṢALĀH is both paired with zakah (C1) and with fear/non-polytheism (D38).
- A19 (87:15 - fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name): the human act of standing-toward-the-Name is what the ṢALĀH (established) IS. D38's "turning to Him" (munībīna ilayhi) is the same as A19's fa-ṣallā toward the Mentioned Name. The ṢALĀH is the turning-toward-God.
- D28 (13:22 - "those who are patient, seeking God's face, establish the ṢALĀH"): D28 gave the ṢALĀH as the GOD-FACING act (seeking God's face). D38 gives the ṢALĀH as the TURNING act (munībīna ilayhi). The ṢALĀH is both God-facing (D28) and turning-to-God (D38).
- C6 (14:35 - "keep me and my sons away from worshipping idols"): the ṢALĀH is the alternative to idol-worship. D38's "do not be of the polytheists" is the same: the ṢALĀH is the anti-polytheism act.
- 30:32 "Those who have divided their religion and become sects" - the ṢALĀH is the act that unifies. The same as 6:159 (C12) and D26 (9:71 - believers are allies of one another). The ṢALĀH is the community-unifier.

FINDING: D38 gives the ṢALĀH as the TURNING-TO-GOD act: "turning to Him and fear Him and establish the ṢALĀH and do not be of the polytheists." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who TURNS to God (munīb). The ṢALĀH is the turning-toward-God. And the ṢALĀH is the anti-polytheism act: "do not be of the polytheists." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is not a polytheist. And the ṢALĀH is part of the UPRIGHT religion (ḥanīf), which is the FITRAH (innate nature) of God: "the fitrah of God upon which He has created mankind." The ṢALĀH is the fitrah-act.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The TURNING (munībīna ilayhi) - the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act. HOLD for the final doc's turning-table. (ii) The NON-POLYTHEISM (30:31) - the ṢALĀH is the anti-polytheism act. HOLD for the final doc's anti-polytheism table. (iii) The FITRAH (30:30) - the ṢALĀH is the fitrah-act (innate nature). HOLD for the final doc's fitrah-table. (iv) The UNIFIER (30:32) - the ṢALĀH is the act that unifies; sectarianism divides. HOLD for the final doc's unity-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has TWENTY-NINE forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), and turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38). All twenty-nine are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D39 (31:4 - ???) next.

## D39 - 31:4 - alladhina yuqimu-s-salata wa yu'tu-z-zakata wa hum bi-l-akhirati hum yuqinun

ARABIC (flow): 31:1 "Alif Lam Mim" -> 31:2 "Those are the verses of the Book of wisdom" -> 31:3 "A guidance and mercy for the doers of good" -> **31:4 "Those who ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND THEY, WITH REGARD TO THE HEREAFTER, ARE CERTAIN (yūqinūn)"** -> 31:5 "Those are upon guidance from their Lord, and those are the successful (mufliḥūn)" -> 31:6 "And among the people are those who purchase idle talk to mislead from the way of God without knowledge and take it in ridicule. Those will have a humiliating punishment."

WORD FORM: yuqīmūna (establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + yuʾtūna (give) al-zakāta (the zakah) + wa-hum (and they) bi-l-ākhirati (with regard to the Hereafter) hum (are) yūqinūn (certain). The ṢALĀH is established by those who are CERTAIN (yūqinūn) of the Hereafter. (IDENTICAL wording to D37 27:3.)

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah Luqman (31). The verse sits in the opening of Surah 31 (31:1-6): the Book of wisdom (31:2), guidance and mercy for the doers of good (31:3), those who establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and are certain of the Hereafter (31:4), those are upon guidance and successful (31:5), those who purchase idle talk (31:6).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ṢALĀH + ZAKAH PAIR (AGAIN): "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah" - the SAME two-pillar formula as C1 (98:5), C2 (2:43), etc. The formula is STABLE across suras. D39 adds CERTAINTY (yūqinūn) regarding the Hereafter as the third element. (IDENTICAL wording to D37 27:3.)
- THE CERTAINTY (yūqinūn): "they, with regard to the Hereafter, are CERTAIN (yūqinūn)." The ṢALĀH is established by those who have CERTAINTY (yaqīn) of the Hereafter. The same as D37 (27:3), D1 (2:3 "those who believe in the unseen"), D19 (6:92 "those who believe in the Hereafter believe in it, and they maintain their ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who has certainty of the Hereafter.
- 31:3 "A guidance and mercy for the doers of good" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the DOERS OF GOOD (muḥsinīn). The ṢALĀH is part of the guidance and mercy for the doers of good. The same as 21:73 (D30 - "We inspired to them the doing of good deeds and the establishment of the ṢALĀH") and 2:277 (D4 - "those who believe and do righteous deeds and establish the ṢALĀH").
- 31:5 "Those are upon guidance from their Lord, and those are the successful (mufliḥūn)" - the ṢALĀH-establishers are upon GUIDANCE and are SUCCESSFUL. The ṢALĀH is the act of the guided and successful. The same as 2:5 ("those are upon guidance from their Lord, and those are the successful") and 27:2-3 (D37).
- 31:6 "those who purchase idle talk to mislead from the way of God without knowledge and take it in ridicule" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who does NOT purchase idle talk (l-hw). The ṢALĀH is the alternative to idle talk: standing-toward-God vs idle talk. The ṢALĀH is the anti-idle-talk act. The same as D15 (5:58 - the mockers take the ṢALĀH-call as ridicule).

PARALLELS:
- D37 (27:3 - "those who establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and they, with regard to the Hereafter, are certain"): D39 (31:4) is the IDENTICAL verse to D37 (27:3). Both say: "those who establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and they, with regard to the Hereafter, are certain." The ṢALĀH is the act of the Hereafter-certain in BOTH verses. The same ṢALĀH + ZAKAH + CERTAINTY formula appears in two suras (27 and 31).
- D1 (2:3 - "those who believe in the unseen and establish the ṢALĀH and spend"): D1 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of the unseen-believer. D39 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the Hereafter-certain. The ṢALĀH is both the unseen-believer's act (D1) and the Hereafter-certain's act (D39). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who believes in the invisible (unseen/Hereafter).
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): the SAME two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + ZAKAH). D39 adds CERTAINTY (yūqinūn) as the third element.
- D19 (6:92 - "those who believe in the Hereafter believe in it, and they maintain their ṢALĀH"): D19 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of the Hereafter-believer. D39 repeats it: the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is CERTAIN of the Hereafter. The ṢALĀH is the marker of Hereafter-certainty.
- 31:6 "those who purchase idle talk to mislead from the way of God" - the ṢALĀH is the alternative to idle talk. The same as D15 (5:58 - the mockers take the ṢALĀH-call as ridicule). The ṢALĀH is the anti-idle-talk act.

FINDING: D39 (31:4) is the IDENTICAL verse to D37 (27:3): "those who establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and they, with regard to the Hereafter, are certain." The ṢALĀH is the act of the HEREAFTER-CERTAIN. The same ṢALĀH + ZAKAH + CERTAINTY formula appears in two suras (27 and 31). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who has certainty of the Hereafter, and the ṢALĀH-establisher is upon guidance and successful (31:5). The ṢALĀH is the anti-idle-talk act (31:6).

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The IDENTICAL VERSE (31:4 = 27:3) - the ṢALĀH + ZAKAH + CERTAINTY formula appears in two suras. HOLD for the final doc's repetition-table. (ii) The HEREAFTER-CERTAINTY (yūqinūn) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter. HOLD for the final doc's certainty-table. (iii) The ANTI-IDLE-TALK (31:6) - the ṢALĀH is the alternative to idle talk. HOLD for the final doc's anti-idle-talk table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), and Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39). All thirty are "state" features.

## D40 - 35:18 - wa la taziru waziratun wizra ukhra... wa-aqamu-s-salata wa-man tazakka fa-innama yatazakka li-nafsihi wa-ila Allahi-l-masir

ARABIC (flow): 35:16 "If He wills, He can do away with you and bring a new creation" -> 35:17 "And that is not difficult for God" -> **35:18 "AND NO SOUL BEARS THE BURDEN OF ANOTHER, and if a heavily burdened soul calls for [help with] its burden, nothing of it will be carried, even if it be a near relative. You only warn those who FEAR THEIR LORD IN THE UNSEEN (yakhshawna rabbahum bi-l-ghaybi) AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH. And whoever purifies himself (tazakkā) - he only purifies himself for his own soul. And TO GOD IS THE DESTINATION (al-maṣīr)."** -> 35:19 "And the blind and the seeing are not equal" -> 35:20 "Nor are the darknesses and the light."

WORD FORM: lā taziru (no soul bears) wāziratun (burden) wizra (the burden of) ukhrā (another) + yakhshawna (they fear) rabbahum (their Lord) bi-l-ghaybi (in the unseen) + wa-aqāmū (and they establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + wa-man (and whoever) tazakkā (purifies himself) fa-innamā (he only) yatazakkā (purifies himself) li-nafsihi (for his own soul) + wa-ilā (and to) Allāhi (God) al-maṣīr (is the destination).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah Fatir (35). The verse sits in the warning-passage (35:16-20): God can do away with you (35:16-17), no soul bears another's burden; you warn those who fear their Lord in the unseen and establish the ṢALĀH; whoever purifies himself purifies himself for his own soul; to God is the destination (35:18), the blind and the seeing are not equal (35:19), nor the darknesses and the light (35:20).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE FEAR-IN-UNSEEN (yakhshawna rabbahum bi-l-ghaybi): "those who fear their Lord IN THE UNSEEN." The ṢALĀH is established by those who FEAR THEIR LORD IN THE UNSEEN. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen. The same as D1 (2:3 "those who believe in the unseen") and D37 (27:3 "they, with regard to the Hereafter, are certain"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen.
- THE ṢALĀH + PURIFICATION (tazakkā): "whoever purifies himself (tazakkā) - he only purifies himself for his own soul." The ṢALĀH is paired with PURIFICATION (tazakkā). The same as 92:18 (B6 - "gives wealth to purify himself") and 87:14 (A19 - "the one who purifies himself"). The ṢALĀH and purification are paired: the ṢALĀH is the purified standing.
- "AND TO GOD IS THE DESTINATION (al-maṣīr)": the ṢALĀH is oriented toward the DESTINATION (maṣīr). The same as A4 (4:115 "evil a maṣīr"), A13 (58:8 "evil a maṣīr"), 2:126 (C1 - "evil a maṣīr"), and 24:42 (B1 - "to God is the destination"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose destination is God.
- "NO SOUL BEARS THE BURDEN OF ANOTHER" (lā taziru wāziratun wizra ukhrā): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who bears his OWN burden. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is responsible for his own soul. The ṢALĀH is the individual standing: no one can stand for another. The same as 6:164 ("no soul bears another's burden") and 53:38-39. The ṢALĀH is the individual act.
- 35:19-20 "the blind and the seeing are not equal; nor the darknesses and the light" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the SEEING one, not the blind one. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who sees God. The same as A17 (84:6 "you will MEET Him") and C10 (20:14 "establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance").

PARALLELS:
- D1 (2:3 - "those who believe in the unseen and establish the ṢALĀH and spend"): D1 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of the unseen-believer. D40 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the unseen-God-fearer. The ṢALĀH is both the unseen-believer's act (D1) and the unseen-God-fearer's act (D40). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who relates to God in the unseen.
- D37 (27:3 - "those who establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and they, with regard to the Hereafter, are certain"): D37 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of the Hereafter-certain. D40 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the unseen-God-fearer. The ṢALĀH is both the Hereafter-certain's act (D37) and the unseen-God-fearer's act (D40). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is oriented toward the invisible (Hereafter/unseen).
- A19 (87:15 - "the one who purifies himself (tazakkā)"): A19 gave the success-triplet (purify + mention + stand). D40 gives the ṢALĀH + purification pairing: the ṢALĀH is the purified standing. The same tazakkā word in both.
- B6 (9:99 - "those who believe... take what they spend as ṢALAWĀT of the Messenger"): B6 gave the ṢALĀH-as-currency. D40 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of one who purifies himself for his own soul. The ṢALĀH is both a currency (B6) and a purification (D40).
- 24:42 (B1 - "to God is the destination"): D40's "to God is the destination (al-maṣīr)" is the SAME word. The ṢALĀH is oriented toward the destination (maṣīr) - the same as B1's "to God is the destination." The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose destination is God.

FINDING: D40 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the UNSEEN-GOD-FEARER: "those who fear their Lord in the unseen AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen. And the ṢALĀH is paired with PURIFICATION (tazakkā): the ṢALĀH is the purified standing. And the ṢALĀH is oriented toward the DESTINATION (maṣīr): "to God is the destination." The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose destination is God. And the ṢALĀH is the INDIVIDUAL standing: "no soul bears the burden of another" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who bears his own burden.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The FEAR-IN-UNSEEN (yakhshawna rabbahum bi-l-ghaybi) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen. HOLD for the final doc's unseen-table. (ii) The PURIFICATION (tazakkā) - the ṢALĀH is the purified standing. HOLD for the final doc's purification-table. (iii) The DESTINATION (al-maṣīr) - the ṢALĀH is oriented toward God's destination. HOLD for the final doc's destination-table. (iv) The INDIVIDUAL STANDING (lā taziru wāziratun wizra ukhrā) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who bears his own burden. HOLD for the final doc's individuality-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-ONE forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), and unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40). All thirty-one are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D41 (35:29 - those who establish salah + give zakah) next.

## D41 - 35:29 - inna alladhina yatluna kitaba Allahi wa-aqamu-s-salata wa-anfiqu mimmā razaqnāhum sirran wa 'alāniyatan yarjuna tijāratan lan tabur

ARABIC (flow): 35:27 "Do you not see that God sends down rain from the sky and We produce thereby fruits of various colors; and in the mountains are tracts of white and red, of varying shades, and [some] extremely black" -> 35:28 "And among people and moving creatures and grazing livestock are various colors similarly. Only those fear God, from among His servants, who have knowledge. Indeed God is Exalted in Might, Forgiving" -> **35:29 "Indeed those who RECITE THE BOOK OF GOD AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND SPEND FROM WHAT WE HAVE PROVIDED THEM, SECRETLY AND PUBLICLY, HOPE FOR A TRADE THAT WILL NOT PERISH (tijāratun lan tābūr)"** -> 35:30 "That He may give them their full rewards and increase them from His bounty. Indeed God is Forgiving, Appreciative."

WORD FORM: yatlūna (recite) kitāba (the Book of) Allāhi (God) + wa-aqāmū (and establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + wa-anfaqū (and spend) mimmā (from what) razaqnāhum (We provided them) sirran (secretly) wa-ʿalāniyatan (and publicly) + yarjūna (they hope for) tijāratan (a trade) lan (that will not) tābūr (perish).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah Fatir (35). The verse sits in the knowledge-passage (35:27-30): God's colors in creation (35:27), only the knowledgeable fear God (35:28), those who recite the Book and establish the ṢALĀH and spend, secretly and publicly, hope for a trade that will not perish (35:29), God gives them full rewards and increased bounty (35:30).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ṢALĀH + RECITATION + SPENDING: "those who RECITE THE BOOK OF GOD AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND SPEND from what We have provided them, secretly and publicly." The ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE act: (1) reciting the Book (tilāwah, God-ward), (2) establishing the ṢALĀH (standing), (3) spending (people-ward). The ṢALĀH is embedded in recitation and spending: the ṢALĀH is the standing-state between the recitation (God-ward) and the spending (people-ward).
- "HOPE FOR A TRADE THAT WILL NOT PERISH" (yarjūna tijāratan lan tābūr): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who hopes for a trade that will not perish. The ṢALĀH is the INVESTMENT: the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes. The same as D2 (2:110 - "whatever good you send forth, you will find it with God") and B6 (9:99 - "ṣalawāt of the Messenger" as currency). The ṢALĀH is the eternal trade.
- "SECRETLY AND PUBLICLY" (sirran wa ʿalāniyatan): the spending (and the ṢALĀH) are done both secretly and publicly. The same as D28 (13:22) and D29 (14:31). The ṢALĀH is both secret and public.
- 35:28 "Only those fear God, from among His servants, who have knowledge" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the KNOWLEDGEABLE God-fearer. The same as D12 (4:162 - "the firm in knowledge... establishers of the ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH requires knowledge.
- 35:30 "That He may give them their full rewards and increase them from His bounty. Indeed God is Forgiving, Appreciative" - the ṢALĀH is rewarded with FULL REWARDS and INCREASED BOUNTY. The ṢALĀH is the act that earns the full reward.

PARALLELS:
- D20 (7:170 - "those who hold fast to the Book and establish the ṢALĀH"): D20 gave the ṢALĀH + BOOK pair. D41 gives the ṢALĀH + RECITATION + SPENDING. The ṢALĀH is both paired with holding the Book (D20) and with reciting the Book (D41). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who holds and recites the Book.
- D2 (2:110 - "whatever good you send forth, you will find it with God"): D2 gave the ṢALĀH's SEND-FORTH (tuqaddimū). D41 gives the ṢALĀH's TRADE (tijāratun lan tābūr). The ṢALĀH is both a send-forth (D2) and a trade that never perishes (D41). The ṢALĀH is the eternal investment.
- B6 (9:99 - "ṣalawāt of the Messenger"): B6 gave the ṢALĀH-as-currency. D41 gives the ṢALĀH-as-trade (tijāratun lan tābūr). The ṢALĀH is both a currency (B6) and a trade that never perishes (D41). The ṢALĀH is the eternal commerce.
- D28 (13:22 - "those who are patient, seeking God's face, establish the ṢALĀH and spend, secretly and publicly"): D28 gave the ṢALĀH as the GOD-FACING act. D41 gives the ṢALĀH as the TRADE-act. The ṢALĀH is both God-facing (D28) and trade-oriented (D41).
- D12 (4:162 - "the firm in knowledge... establishers of the ṢALĀH"): D12 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of the knowledgeable. D41 repeats it: "only those fear God, from among His servants, who have knowledge." The ṢALĀH is the act of the knowledgeable.

FINDING: D41 gives the ṢALĀH as the ETERNAL TRADE: "those who recite the Book of God and establish the ṢALĀH and spend, secretly and publicly, hope for a TRADE THAT WILL NOT PERISH." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who hopes for a trade that never perishes. The ṢALĀH is the INVESTMENT: the ṢALĀH is the eternal commerce. And the ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE act: recitation (God-ward) -> ṢALĀH (standing) -> spending (people-ward). The ṢALĀH is both secret and public. And the ṢALĀH is the act of the knowledgeable God-fearer. The ṢALĀH is rewarded with FULL REWARDS and INCREASED BOUNTY.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The ETERNAL TRADE (tijāratun lan tābūr) - the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes. HOLD for the final doc's trade-table. (ii) The RECITATION + ṢALĀH + SPENDING formula - the ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE act. HOLD for the final doc's formula-table. (iii) The KNOWLEDGE (35:28) - the ṢALĀH is the act of the knowledgeable. HOLD for the final doc's knowledge-table. (iv) The FULL REWARD (35:30) - the ṢALĀH is rewarded with full rewards and increased bounty. HOLD for the final doc's reward-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-TWO forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40), and eternal-trade (the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes, D41). All thirty-two are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D42 (42:38 - ???) next.

## D42 - 42:38 - wa alladhina istajaba li-rabbihim wa-aqamu-s-salata wa-amruhum shura baynahum wa mimmā razaqnāhum yunfiqun

ARABIC (flow): 42:36 "So whatever thing you have been given - it is the enjoyment of worldly life. And what is with God is better and more lasting for those who believe and upon their Lord rely" -> 42:37 "And those who avoid the major sins and shameful acts and, when they are angry, they forgive" -> **42:38 "AND THOSE WHO RESPOND TO THEIR LORD (istaǧābū li-rabbihim) AND ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND WHOSE AFFAIR IS [DETERMINED BY] CONSULTATION AMONG THEMSELVES (amruhum shūrā baynahum) AND FROM WHAT WE HAVE PROVIDED THEM THEY SPEND"** -> 42:39 "And those who, when tyranny strikes them, they defend themselves."

WORD FORM: istaǧābū (respond) li-rabbihim (to their Lord) + wa-aqāmū (and establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) + wa-amruhum (and their affair) shūrā (consultation) baynahum (among themselves) + wa-mimmā (and from what) razaqnāhum (We provided them) yunfiqūn (they spend).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah ash-Shura (42). The verse sits in the qualities-passage (42:36-39): what is with God is better (42:36), those who avoid major sins and forgive (42:37), those who respond to their Lord and establish the ṢALĀH and consult among themselves and spend (42:38), those who defend themselves against tyranny (42:39).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE FOUR QUALITIES: (1) RESPOND TO THEIR LORD (istijābah), (2) ESTABLISH THE SALAH, (3) CONSULTATION AMONG THEMSELVES (shūrā), (4) SPEND from what God provided. The ṢALĀH is the SECOND quality. The ṢALĀH is embedded in a four-quality formula: response, standing, consultation, spending.
- "RESPOND TO THEIR LORD" (istaǧābū li-rabbihim): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who RESPONDS to God. The ṢALĀH is the response-act: the ṢALĀH is the standing-toward-God in response to God's call. The same as C10 (20:14 - the ṢALĀH is the response to God's revelation) and D19 (6:92 - the ṢALĀH is the response to the Book). The ṢALĀH is the response to God's call.
- "CONSULTATION AMONG THEMSELVES" (amruhum shūrā baynahum): the ṢALĀH is paired with CONSULTATION (shūrā). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who consults. The ṢALĀH is the community-consultative act: the ṢALĀH is established in a community that consults. The same as D26 (9:71 - believers are allies of one another) and C2 (2:43 - bow with those who bow). The ṢALĀH is the community-act.
- 42:36-37 "what is with God is better and more lasting for those who believe... and those who avoid the major sins and shameful acts and, when they are angry, they forgive" - the ṢALĀH is the act of those who avoid major sins and forgive. The ṢALĀH is the act of the morally upright.
- 42:39 "when tyranny strikes them, they defend themselves" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who defends himself against tyranny. The ṢALĀH is the act of the self-defender.

PARALLELS:
- C10 (20:14 - "listen to what is revealed... establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance"): C10 gave the ṢALĀH as the response to God's revelation. D42 gives the ṢALĀH as the response to their Lord. The ṢALĀH is both the revelation-response (C10) and the Lord-response (D42). The ṢALĀH is the response-act.
- D26 (9:71 - "believing men and women are allies of one another... establish the ṢALĀH"): D26 gave the ṢALĀH as the community-bond. D42 gives the ṢALĀH as the consultative-act. The ṢALĀH is both a community-bond (D26) and a consultative-act (D42). The ṢALĀH is the community-act.
- C1 (98:5 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah"): C1's two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + zakah) and D42's four-quality formula (response + ṢALĀH + consultation + spending) are the SAME structure with response and consultation added. The ṢALĀH is the MIDDLE quality.
- D3 (2:177 - birr: 10-item character list): D3's 10-item list and D42's 4-quality formula are the SAME structure: the ṢALĀH is embedded in a comprehensive character. D42's formula is more focused.
- D41 (35:29 - "those who recite the Book of God and establish the ṢALĀH and spend"): D41 gave the ṢALĀH + recitation + spending. D42 gives the ṢALĀH + response + consultation + spending. The ṢALĀH is both the recitation-act (D41) and the response-act (D42).

FINDING: D42 gives the ṢALĀH as the RESPONSE-ACT: "those who respond to their Lord and establish the ṢALĀH and whose affair is consultation among themselves and from what We have provided them they spend." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who RESPONDS to God. The ṢALĀH is the response-act: the ṢALĀH is the standing-toward-God in response to God's call. And the ṢALĀH is paired with CONSULTATION (shūrā): the ṢALĀH is the community-consultative act. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who consults. The ṢALĀH is both a response-act (D42) and a community-act (D26).

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The RESPONSE (istijābah) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who responds to God. HOLD for the final doc's response-table. (ii) The CONSULTATION (shūrā) - the ṢALĀH is paired with consultation. HOLD for the final doc's consultation-table. (iii) The FOUR QUALITIES (42:38) - response + ṢALĀH + consultation + spending. HOLD for the final doc's qualities-table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-THREE forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40), eternal-trade (the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes, D41), and response-consultative (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who responds to God and consults, D42). All thirty-three are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D43 (58:13 - ???) next.

## D43 - 58:13 - a-ashfaqtum an tuqaddimu bayna yaday najakum sadaqatin fa-idh lam taf'alu wa-taba Allahu 'alaykum fa-aqimu-s-salata wa-atu-z-zakata wa-ati'u Allaha wa rasulahu

ARABIC (flow): 58:11 "O you who believe, when you are told to make room in the assemblies, then make room; God will make room for you. And when it is said: rise up, then rise up. God will raise those who have believed among you and those who were given knowledge, by degrees. And God is Acquainted with what you do" -> 58:12 "O you who believe, when you confer privately with the Messenger, present a charity before your private conversation. That is better for you and purer. But if you do not find [the means], then indeed God is Forgiving, Merciful" -> **58:13 "Are you afraid of presenting [charities] before your private conversation? But when you do not do it and God has turned to you in forgiveness, then ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND OBEY GOD AND HIS MESSENGER. And God is Acquainted with what you do"** -> 58:14 "Have you not seen those who took as allies a people upon whom God has become angry? They are not of you nor of them, and they swear to [the] falsehood while they know."

WORD FORM: a-ashfaqtum (are you afraid) an tuqaddimū (to present) bayna yaday (before) najākum (your private conversation) ṣadaqātin (charities) + fa-idh (but when) lam tafʿalū (you do not do it) wa-tāba (and God has turned to you in forgiveness) Allāhu ʿalaykum + fa-aqīmū (then establish) al-ṣalāta (the ṣalāh) wa-ātū (and give) al-zakāta (the zakah) wa-aṭīʿū (and obey) Allāha (God) wa rasūlahu (and His Messenger) + wa-Allāhu (and God is) khabīrun (Acquainted) bimā (with what) taʿmalūn (you do).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Mujadila (58). The verse sits in the private-conversation-passage (58:11-14): make room in assemblies (58:11), present charity before private conversation (58:12), are you afraid of presenting charity? when you do not and God forgives, establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and obey God and His Messenger (58:13), those who take as allies a people upon whom God is angry (58:14).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ṢALĀH + ZAKAH + OBEDIENCE FORMULA: "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and obey God and His Messenger." The ṢALĀH + ZAKAH pair (C1's two-pillar formula) now has a THIRD element: OBEY GOD AND HIS MESSENGER (aṭīʿū Allāha wa rasūlahu). The ṢALĀH is paired with obedience to God and His Messenger. The same as D26 (9:71 - enjoin right + forbid wrong + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey God and His Messenger) and D35 (24:56 - ṢALĀH + zakah + obey God and His Messenger).
- "GOD HAS TURNED TO YOU IN FORGIVENESS" (tāba Allāhu ʿalaykum): the ṢALĀH is established AFTER God's turning to you in forgiveness. The ṢALĀH is the act of the FORGIVEN: God forgives (tawbah), and then the people establish the ṢALĀH. The same as B5 (9:103 - "your ṣalāh is their tranquility" for the repentant) and C9 (19:59 - the ṢALĀH-loser can repent and be saved). The ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven.
- "ARE YOU AFRAID" (a-ashfaqtum): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is NOT afraid of presenting charity. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is generous, not fearful. The same as D4 (2:277 - "no fear, no grief") and D8 (4:77 - "fear God, not people"). The ṢALĀH is the anti-fear act.
- "AND GOD IS ACQUAINTED WITH WHAT YOU DO" (wa-Allāhu khabīrun bimā taʿmalūn): the ṢALĀH is seen by God. The same as A17 (84:15 - "his Lord was EVER SEEING of him") and D2 (2:110 - "God is Seeing of what you do"). The ṢALĀH is a visible standing, seen by God.
- 58:14 "Have you not seen those who took as allies a people upon whom God has become angry? They are not of you nor of them" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is NOT the ally of the angry. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is allied with God and His Messenger (D14 - 5:55).

PARALLELS:
- D26 (9:71 - "believing men and women are allies... enjoin right + forbid wrong + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey God and His Messenger"): D26 gave the five-act formula (enjoin + forbid + ṢALĀH + zakah + obey). D43 gives the three-element formula (ṢALĀH + zakah + obey). The ṢALĀH is always paired with zakah and obedience. The ṢALĀH is both the middle act (D26) and the first act (D43).
- D35 (24:56 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and obey the Messenger, that you may receive mercy"): D35 gave the ṢALĀH + ZAKAH + OBEDIENCE formula. D43 repeats it: ṢALĀH + zakah + obey God and His Messenger. The ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven in both (D35 "God has turned to you in forgiveness" - wait, that's D43; D35 is 24:56 "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah and obey the Messenger, that you may receive mercy").
- B5 (9:103 - "your ṣalāh is their tranquility"): B5 gave the ṢALĀH as the healing act for the repentant. D43 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the forgiven: "when you do not [present charity] and God has turned to you in forgiveness, then establish the ṢALĀH." The ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven.
- C9 (19:59 - "they lost the ṢALĀH and followed desires"): C9 gave the ṢALĀH-loss. D43 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the forgiven: the ṢALĀH-loser can be forgiven and then establish the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is restorable.
- D14 (5:55 - "your ally is only God and His Messenger and those who believe - those who establish the ṢALĀH"): D14 gave the ṢALĀH as the ALLY-MARKER. D43 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of one who is NOT the ally of the angry (58:14). The ṢALĀH is both an ally-marker (D14) and a non-ally-of-the-angry marker (D43).

FINDING: D43 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the FORGIVEN: "when you do not [present charity] and God has turned to you in forgiveness, then ESTABLISH THE SALAH AND GIVE THE ZAKAH AND OBEY GOD AND HIS MESSENGER." The ṢALĀH is the act of the FORGIVEN: God forgives (tawbah), and then the people establish the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven. And the ṢALĀH is paired with obedience to God and His Messenger: the ṢALĀH is the act of one who obeys. The ṢALĀH is the anti-fear act: "are you afraid of presenting charity?" The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is not afraid. And the ṢALĀH is seen by God: "God is Acquainted with what you do."

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The FORGIVEN (tāba Allāhu ʿalaykum) - the ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven. HOLD for the final doc's forgiveness-table. (ii) The OBEDIENCE (aṭīʿū Allāha wa rasūlahu) - the ṢALĀH is paired with obedience to God and His Messenger. HOLD for the final doc's obedience-table. (iii) The ANTI-FEAR (a-ashfaqtum) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is not afraid. HOLD for the final doc's fear-table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-FOUR forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40), eternal-trade (the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes, D41), response-consultative (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who responds to God and consults, D42), and forgiven (the ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven, D43). All thirty-four are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see D44 (62:9 - Friday call for salah) next.

## D44 - 62:9 - idha nudiya li-s-salati min yawmi-l-jumu'ati fa-s'aw ila dhikri Allahi wa dharu-l-bay'a

ARABIC (flow): 62:7 "They will never wish for it ever, because of what their hands have put forth. And God is Knowing of the wrongdoers" -> 62:8 "Say: indeed the death from which you flee - it will meet you. Then you will be returned to the Knower of the unseen and the witnessed, and He will inform you of what you used to do" -> **62:9 "O you who believe, WHEN THE CALL IS MADE FOR THE SALAH ON THE DAY OF FRIDAY, then HASTEN TO THE REMEMBRANCE OF GOD AND LEAVE THE TRADE. That is better for you, if you know"** -> 62:10 "AND WHEN THE SALAH IS FINISHED, THEN DISPERSE THROUGH THE LAND AND SEEK THE BOUNTY OF GOD, AND REMEMBER GOD MUCH, THAT YOU MAY BE SUCCESSFUL" -> 62:11 "And when they see a trade or diversion, they disperse to it and leave you standing. Say: what is with God is better than diversion and trade. And God is the Best of providers."

WORD FORM: idhā (when) nūdiya (is called) li-l-ṣalāti (for the ṣalāh) min yawmi (on the day of) al-jumuʿati (Friday) + fa-sʿaw (then hasten) ilā (to) dhikri (the remembrance of) Allāhi (God) + wa-dharū (and leave) al-bayʿa (the trade) + dhālikum (that is) khayrun (better) lakum (for you) in (if) kuntum (you) taʿlamūn (know).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Jumu'a (62). The verse sits in the Friday-passage (62:7-11): the wrongdoers never wish for death (62:7-8), WHEN THE CALL IS MADE FOR THE SALAH ON FRIDAY, hasten to the remembrance of God and leave the trade (62:9), when the ṢALĀH is finished, disperse and seek God's bounty (62:10), when they see trade or diversion, they disperse and leave you standing (62:11).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE ṢALĀH-CALL (nūdiya li-l-ṣalāti): "when the CALL is made for the ṢALĀH." The ṢALĀH has a CALL (nidāʾ). The ṢALĀH is ANNOUNCED: there is a public call to the ṢALĀH on Friday. The same as D15 (5:58 - "when you call to the ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH is a CALLED act: the call summons the community to the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the community's summoned act.
- "HASTEN TO THE REMEMBRANCE OF GOD" (fa-sʿaw ilā dhikri Allāhi): the ṢALĀH IS the remembrance of God. The ṢALĀH is the dhikr-act: when the call is made, you hasten to the remembrance of God, which IS the ṢALĀH. The same as C10 (20:14 - "establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance") and D34 (24:37 - "neither trade nor sale distracts them from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH IS the remembrance.
- "LEAVE THE TRADE" (wa-dharū al-bayʿa): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who LEAVES THE TRADE. The ṢALĀH is the anti-trade act: when the call is made, you leave the trade and hasten to the ṢALĀH. The same as D34 (24:37 - "neither trade nor sale distracts them from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH is the priority over trade.
- 62:10 "AND WHEN THE SALAH IS FINISHED, THEN DISPERSE THROUGH THE LAND AND SEEK THE BOUNTY OF GOD" - the ṢALĀH is the TEMPORAL BOUNDARY of the day: when the ṢALĀH is finished, you disperse and seek God's bounty. The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the economic life: the ṢALĀH marks the time of gathering and the time of dispersing. The same as D17 (5:106 - the ṢALĀH anchors legal proceedings) and D36 (24:58 - the ṢALĀH is the clock of the household). The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the community's economic life.
- 62:11 "when they see a trade or diversion, they disperse to it and leave you standing" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who does NOT disperse to trade or diversion. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who STAYS standing (qāʾim). The same as A17 (84:6 "you will MEET Him") and C13 (3:39 - Zakariyya standing-praying in the miḥrāb). The ṢALĀH is the standing-act.

PARALLELS:
- D15 (5:58 - "when you call to the ṢALĀH, they take it as ridicule"): D15 gave the ṢALĀH-CALL as the identity marker. D44 gives the ṢALĀH-CALL as the Friday summons: when the call is made, you hasten to the remembrance of God and leave the trade. The ṢALĀH is both a mocked call (D15) and a summons to the remembrance (D44).
- C10 (20:14 - "establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance"): C10 gave the ṢALĀH as the remembrance. D44 gives the ṢALĀH as the dhikr-act: when the call is made, you hasten to the remembrance of God, which IS the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH IS the remembrance.
- D34 (24:37 - "neither trade nor sale distracts them from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH"): D34 gave the ṢALĀH as the non-distracted act. D44 gives the ṢALĀH as the anti-trade act: when the call is made, you leave the trade. The ṢALĀH is both non-distracted (D34) and anti-trade (D44).
- D17 (5:106 - "detain them after the ṢALĀH"): D17 gave the ṢALĀH as a LEGAL anchor. D44 gives the ṢALĀH as an ECONOMIC anchor: when the ṢALĀH is finished, you disperse and seek God's bounty. The ṢALĀH is both a legal anchor (D17) and an economic anchor (D44). The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the community's life: legal, economic, household, and personal.
- D36 (24:58 - "before the DAWN SALAH... and after the NIGHT SALAH"): D36 gave the ṢALĀH as the HOUSEHOLD clock. D44 gives the ṢALĀH as the ECONOMIC clock. The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the community's life: household, economic, legal, and personal.

FINDING: D44 gives the ṢALĀH as the ECONOMIC-CLOCK: "when the call is made for the ṢALĀH on Friday, hasten to the remembrance of God and leave the trade; when the ṢALĀH is finished, disperse through the land and seek God's bounty." The ṢALĀH is the TEMPORAL BOUNDARY of the economic life: the ṢALĀH marks the time of gathering and the time of dispersing. The ṢALĀH is the CLOCK of the community's economic life. And the ṢALĀH IS the remembrance: when the call is made, you hasten to the remembrance of God, which IS the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the dhikr-act. And the ṢALĀH is the anti-trade act: you leave the trade and hasten to the ṢALĀH. The ṢALĀH is the priority over trade. The ṢALĀH is the standing-act: those who disperse to trade or diversion leave the stander standing (qāʾim).

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The ṢALĀH-CALL (nūdiya li-l-ṣalāti) - the ṢALĀH is a called act. HOLD for the final doc's call-table. (ii) The REMEMBRANCE (dhikr) - the ṢALĀH IS the remembrance. HOLD for the final doc's remembrance-table. (iii) The ANTI-TRADE (wa-dharū al-bayʿa) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who leaves the trade. HOLD for the final doc's anti-trade table. (iv) The ECONOMIC-CLOCK (62:10) - the ṢALĀH is the temporal boundary of the economic life. HOLD for the final doc's clock-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-FIVE forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40), eternal-trade (the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes, D41), response-consultative (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who responds to God and consults, D42), forgiven (the ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven, D43), and economic-clock (the ṢALĀH is the temporal boundary of the economic life, D44). All thirty-five are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - D-cluster COMPLETE (44/44). Next cluster E1 (29:45 - the salah is the transformation check: it prevents immorality).

## E3 - 4:142 - inna-l-munafiqina yukhadi'una Allah wa-huwa khadi'uhum wa-idha qamu ila-s-salati qamu kusala yurawna-n-nasa wa-la yadhkuruna Allah illa qalila

ARABIC (flow): 4:140 "And it has already come down to you in the Book that when you hear the verses of God being denied and mocked at, then do not sit with them until they speak of something else, for indeed you would then be like them. Indeed God will gather the hypocrites and the disbelievers in Jahannam all together" -> 4:141 "Those who wait [and watch] for you; if you gain a victory from God, they say: were we not with you? But if the disbelievers gain a share, they say: did we not overpower you and protect you from the believers? So God will judge between you on the Day of Resurrection, and God will never make for the disbelievers a way [of authority] over the believers" -> **4:142 "Indeed the hypocrites want to deceive God, but God deceives them. And when they RISE TO THE SALAH, they RISE LAZILY (qiyāman kusālā), SHOWING OFF TO THE PEOPLE (yurāʾūna al-nās), AND THEY DO NOT REMEMBER GOD EXCEPT A LITTLE (wa-lā yadhkurūna Allāha illā qalīlan)"** -> 4:143 "They are in doubt, between that - neither for these nor for these. And whomever God lets go astray, never will you find for him a way" -> 4:144 "O you who believe, do not take the disbelievers as allies instead of the believers. Do you wish to give God against you a clear authority?"

WORD FORM: qāmū (they rise) ilā (to) al-ṣalāti (the ṣalāh) + qāmū (they rise) kusālā (lazily) + yurāʾūna (they show off) al-nās (to the people) + wa-lā (and do not) yadhkurūna (they remember) Allāha (God) illā (except) qalīlan (a little). The ṢALĀH is approached LAZILY (kusālā), with SHOW (riyāʾ), and with LITTLE remembrance (qalīlan).

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah an-Nisa' (4). The verse sits in the hypocrite-passage (4:140-144): do not sit with those who mock the verses (4:140), the hypocrites wait and watch (4:141), the hypocrites rise to the ṢALĀH lazily, showing off to people, remembering God little (4:142), they are in doubt (4:143), do not take the disbelievers as allies (4:144).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE LAZINESS (kusālā): "when they RISE TO THE SALAH, they RISE LAZILY (qiyāman kusālā)." The ṢALĀH is approached LAZILY by the hypocrites. The same as D25 (9:54 - "they do not come to the ṢALĀH except while they are lazy"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who rises LAZILY. The ṢALĀH is the act of the lazy.
- THE SHOW (riyāʾ, yurāʾūna al-nās): "showing off TO THE PEOPLE." The ṢALĀH is done for SHOW (riyāʾ). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who shows off to people, not to God. The same as A13 (58:8 - the mockers take the ṢALĀH-call as ridicule) and D15 (5:58 - the mockers take the ṢALĀH-call as ridicule). The ṢALĀH is the act of the show-off.
- THE LITTLE REMEMBRANCE (wa-lā yadhkurūna Allāha illā qalīlan): "they do not remember God EXCEPT A LITTLE." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who does NOT remember God. The ṢALĀH is the act of one whose remembrance is LITTLE. The same as C10 (20:14 - "establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance") and D34 (24:37 - "neither trade nor sale distracts them from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who remembers God MUCH, not little.
- "GOD DECEIVES THEM" (huwa khādiʿuhum): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is DECEIVED by God. The ṢALĀH is the act of the deceived. The same as 4:140 ("indeed you would then be like them") and 4:141 ("those who wait [and watch] for you"). The ṢALĀH is the act of the hypocrite who is deceived.
- 4:140 "when you hear the verses of God being denied and mocked at, then do not sit with them" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who does NOT sit with the mockers. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who separates from the mockers. The same as 9:67 (E-cluster - the hypocrites) and 9:84 (B7 - "do not s-l-l on them").

PARALLELS:
- D25 (9:54 - "they do not come to the ṢALĀH except while they are lazy"): D25 gave the ṢALĀH's LAZINESS. E3 repeats it: "when they rise to the ṢALĀH, they rise lazily (qiyāman kusālā)." The ṢALĀH is the act of the lazy. The same laziness (kusālā) in both verses.
- C10 (20:14 - "establish the ṢALĀH for My remembrance"): C10 gave the ṢALĀH as the remembrance. E3 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of one who does NOT remember God: "they do not remember God except a little." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who remembers God MUCH (C10) vs LITTLE (E3). The ṢALĀH is the divider: the ṢALĀH-establisher remembers God much; the ṢALĀH-hypocrite remembers God little.
- D34 (24:37 - "neither trade nor sale distracts them from the remembrance of God and the establishment of the ṢALĀH"): D34 gave the ṢALĀH as the act of one who is not distracted from remembrance. E3 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of one who is distracted from remembrance: "they do not remember God except a little." The ṢALĀH is the divider: the ṢALĀH-establisher is not distracted (D34); the ṢALĀH-hypocrite is distracted (E3).
- A13 (58:8 - "when you call to the ṢALĀH, they take it as ridicule"): A13 gave the ṢALĀH-call as the mockers' target. E3 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the show-off: "showing off to the people." The ṢALĀH is both mocked (A13) and shown off (E3) - two forms of the ṢALĀH-hypocrisy.
- 4:140 "do not sit with those who mock the verses" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who separates from the mockers. The same as 9:67 (E-cluster - the hypocrites) and 9:84 (B7 - "do not s-l-l on them"). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is NOT the mocker.

FINDING: E3 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the HYPOCRITE: "when they rise to the ṢALĀH, they rise LAZILY, showing off to the people, and they do not remember God except a little." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who rises LAZILY (kusālā), with SHOW (riyāʾ), and with LITTLE remembrance (qalīlan). The ṢALĀH is the act of the lazy, the show-off, and the little-rememberer. And the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is DECEIVED by God: "God deceives them." The ṢALĀH is the act of the deceived. The ṢALĀH is the divider: the ṢALĀH-establisher remembers God much (C10, D34); the ṢALĀH-hypocrite remembers God little (E3).

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The LAZINESS (kusālā) - the ṢALĀH is approached lazily by the hypocrite. HOLD for the final doc's laziness-table. (ii) The SHOW (riyāʾ) - the ṢALĀH is done for show. HOLD for the final doc's show-table. (iii) The LITTLE REMEMBRANCE (qalīlan) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who remembers God little. HOLD for the final doc's remembrance-table. (iv) The DECEIVED (khādiʿuhum) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is deceived by God. HOLD for the final doc's deception-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-SIX forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40), eternal-trade (the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes, D41), response-consultative (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who responds to God and consults, D42), forgiven (the ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven, D43), economic-clock (the ṢALĀH is the temporal boundary of the economic life, D44), and hypocrite-lazy-show-little (the ṢALĀH is the act of the lazy, show-off, little-rememberer, E3). All thirty-six are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see E4 (8:35 - the mockers' salah) next.

## E4 - 8:35 - wa ma kana salatuhum 'inda-l-Bayti illa makā'an wa tasdiyah

ARABIC (flow): 8:33 "And God would not punish them while you are among them, and God would not punish them while they seek forgiveness" -> 8:34 "And why should God not punish them while they obstruct access to the Sacred Mosque? And they were not its allies [and protectors]; its allies are only the righteous, but most of them do not know" -> **8:35 "AND THEIR SALAH AT THE HOUSE WAS NOT BUT WHISTLING (makāʾ) AND HAND-CLAPPING (taṣdiyah). So taste the punishment for what you disbelieved"** -> 8:36 "Indeed those who disbelieve spend their wealth to obstruct access to the way of God, so they will spend it; then it will be a [cause of] regret upon them; then they will be overcome. And those who disbelieve will be gathered to Jahannam."

WORD FORM: wa-mā (and not) kāna (was) ṣalātuhum (their ṣalāh) ʿinda (at) al-Bayti (the House) illā (except) makāʾan (whistling) wa-taṣdiyatan (and hand-clapping) + fa-dhūqū (so taste) al-ʿadhāba (the punishment) bimā (for what) kuntum (you) takfurūn (disbelieve). The ṢALĀH of the disbelievers is WHISTLING and HAND-CLAPPING.

CONTEXT: MADANI, Surah al-Anfal (8). The verse sits in the House-passage (8:33-36): God would not punish them while the Prophet is among them (8:33), they obstruct access to the Sacred Mosque (8:34), THEIR SALAH AT THE HOUSE WAS NOT BUT WHISTLING AND HAND-CLAPPING (8:35), the disbelievers spend wealth to obstruct the way of God (8:36).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE MOCKERY (makāʾ + taṣdiyah): "their ṢALĀH at the House was not but WHISTLING and HAND-CLAPPING." The ṢALĀH of the disbelievers is WHISTLING and HAND-CLAPPING. The ṢALĀH is the act of the mockers: they turn the ṢALĀH into whistling and clapping. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who mocks the ṢALĀH. The same as A13 (58:8 - the mockers take the ṢALĀH-call as ridicule) and D15 (5:58 - the mockers take the ṢALĀH-call as ridicule). The ṢALĀH is the act of the mocker.
- "AT THE HOUSE" (ʿinda al-Bayti): the ṢALĀH is performed AT THE HOUSE (the Sacred House). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is at the House. The same as C5 (2:125 - MUSSALLA, the standing-site at the House) and A8 (22:40 - the houses of Name-mention). The ṢALĀH is the act performed at the House.
- "TASTE THE PUNISHMENT FOR WHAT YOU DISBELIEVED" (fa-dhūqū al-ʿadhāba bimā kuntum takfurūn): the ṢALĀH is the act that determines the punishment: the ṢALĀH-mocker is punished for his disbelief. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is punished. The same as A-cluster (fire-delivery for ṢALĀH-loss) and B7 (9:84 - the ṢALĀH is withheld from the disbelievers).
- 8:34 "they obstruct access to the Sacred Mosque; its allies are only the righteous" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the RIGHTEOUS allies of the House. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is an ally of the House. The same as D14 (5:55 - the ṢALĀH-establishers are God's allies) and D24 (9:18 - the ṢALĀH-establishers maintain God's mosques). The ṢALĀH is the act of the House's ally.
- 8:36 "those who disbelieve spend their wealth to obstruct access to the way of God" - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who does NOT obstruct the way of God. The ṢALĀH is the act of one who opens the way to God.

PARALLELS:
- A13 (58:8 - "when you call to the ṢALĀH, they take it as ridicule"): A13 gave the ṢALĀH-call as the mockers' target. E4 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the mocker: "their ṢALĀH was not but whistling and hand-clapping." The ṢALĀH is both mocked (A13) and turned into mockery (E4) - two forms of the ṢALĀH-hypocrisy.
- D15 (5:58 - "when you call to the ṢALĀH, they take it as ridicule and amusement"): D15 gave the ṢALĀH-call as the mockers' target. E4 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the mocker: "their ṢALĀH was not but whistling and hand-clapping." The ṢALĀH is both mocked (D15) and turned into mockery (E4).
- C5 (2:125 - MUSSALLA, the standing-site at the House): the MUSSALLA is the standing-site at the House. E4's "their ṢALĀH at the House" is the same site: the ṢALĀH is performed at the House. The site (C5) and the act (E4) are paired: the ṢALĀH is performed at the House.
- D14 (5:55 - "your ally is only God and His Messenger and those who believe - those who establish the ṢALĀH"): D14 gave the ṢALĀH as the ALLY-MARKER. E4 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the House's ally: "its allies are only the righteous" (8:34). The ṢALĀH is both an ally-marker (D14) and a House-ally marker (E4).
- D24 (9:18 - "the mosques of God are only to be maintained by those who... establish the ṢALĀH"): D24 gave the ṢALĀH as the mosque-maintenance act. E4 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the House's ally: "its allies are only the righteous" (8:34). The ṢALĀH is both a mosque-maintenance act (D24) and a House-ally act (E4).

FINDING: E4 gives the ṢALĀH as the act of the MOCKER: "their ṢALĀH at the House was not but whistling and hand-clapping." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who mocks the ṢALĀH: they turn the ṢALĀH into whistling and clapping. The ṢALĀH is the act of the mocker. And the ṢALĀH is performed AT THE HOUSE: the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is at the House. And the ṢALĀH is the act of the House's ally: "its allies are only the righteous" (8:34). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is an ally of the House. And the ṢALĀH is the act that determines the punishment: "taste the punishment for what you disbelieved." The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is punished.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The MOCKERY (makāʾ + taṣdiyah) - the ṢALĀH is turned into whistling and clapping by the mockers. HOLD for the final doc's mockery-table. (ii) The HOUSE (ʿinda al-Bayti) - the ṢALĀH is performed at the House. HOLD for the final doc's site-table (C5, A8, E4). (iii) The HOUSE-ALLY (8:34) - the ṢALĀH is the act of the House's ally. HOLD for the final doc's ally-table. (iv) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-SEVEN forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40), eternal-trade (the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes, D41), response-consultative (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who responds to God and consults, D42), forgiven (the ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven, D43), economic-clock (the ṢALĀH is the temporal boundary of the economic life, D44), hypocrite-lazy-show-little (the ṢALĀH is the act of the lazy, show-off, little-rememberer, E3), and mocker-whistling-clapping (the ṢALĀH is turned into whistling and clapping by the mockers, E4). All thirty-seven are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see E6 (23:2 - the humble in salah) next.

## E6 - 23:2 - alladhina hum fi salatihim khashi'un

ARABIC (flow): 23:1 "Successful are the believers" -> **23:2 "Those who are HUMBLE (khāshiʿūn) in their SALAH"** -> 23:3 "And those who turn away from idle talk (laghw)" -> 23:4 "And those who are doers of the zakah" -> 23:5-7 "And those who guard their private parts, except from their wives or what their right hands possess..." -> 23:8 "And those who are faithful to their trusts and their covenants" -> 23:9 "And those who GUARD their SALAWAT (plural)" -> 23:10-11 "Those are the inheritors who will inherit the Firdaws; they will abide therein eternally."

WORD FORM: khāshiʿūn (humble, active participle) + fī (in) ṣalātihim (their ṣalāh) - the ṢALĀH is the act of the HUMBLE: "those who are humble IN their ṢALĀH." The ṢALĀH is the act that PRODUCES humility (khushūʿ).

CONTEXT: MAKAN, Surah al-Mu'minun (23). The verse sits in the success-passage (23:1-11): successful are the believers (23:1), those who are humble in their ṢALĀH (23:2), those who turn away from idle talk (23:3), those who are doers of the zakah (23:4), those who guard their private parts (23:5-7), those who are faithful to trusts and covenants (23:8), those who guard their ṢALAWĀT (23:9), those are the inheritors of Firdaws (23:10-11).

GRAMMAR & LEXICON:
- THE HUMILITY (khushūʿ): "Those who are HUMBLE (khāshiʿūn) in their ṢALĀH." The ṢALĀH is the act of the HUMBLE: the ṢALĀH produces humility (khushūʿ). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is humble in it. The same as D5 (2:45 "heavy except on the humble") and D15 (17:109 "it increases them in humility"). The ṢALĀH and humility are paired: the ṢALĀH produces humility, and the humble find the ṢALĀH light.
- THE IDLE-TALK-AVOIDANCE (23:3 "those who turn away from idle talk (laghw)"): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who turns away from idle talk. The ṢALĀH is the anti-laghv act. The same as D16 (5:91 - Satan averts from the ṢALĀH through intoxicants and gambling) and D34 (24:37 - "neither trade nor sale distracts them"). The ṢALĀH is the act that opposes idle talk, trade, and Satan's aversion.
- THE ṢALĀH + ZAKAH PAIR (23:4 "those who are doers of the zakah"): the SAME two-pillar formula (ṢALĀH + ZAKAH) as C1 (98:5), C2 (2:43), etc. The ṢALĀH and the zakah are the TWO acts of the successful.
- THE TRUST-FAITHFULNESS (23:8 "those who are faithful to their trusts and their covenants"): the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is faithful to trusts and covenants. The ṢALĀH is the act of the faithful. The same as C3 (2:83 - "establish the ṢALĀH and give the zakah; then you turned away") - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who does NOT turn away.
- 23:10-11 "Those are the inheritors who will inherit the Firdaws; they will abide therein eternally" - the ṢALĀH-humble are the INHERITORS of Firdaws (the highest garden). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who inherits Firdaws.

PARALLELS:
- D5 (2:45 - "heavy except on the humble"): D5 gave the ṢALĀH's WEIGHT (heavy except for the humble). E6 gives the ṢALĀH's HUMILITY (those who are humble in their ṢALĀH). The ṢALĀH is heavy (D5) and produces humility (E6). The weight and the humility are paired: the ṢALĀH is heavy for the proud, but the humble find it light, and the ṢALĀH produces more humility.
- D15 (17:109 - "it increases them in humility"): E6 confirms: the ṢALĀH increases humility. The ṢALĀH is the act that PRODUCES humility (khushūʿ). The same as 23:2 ("humble in their ṢALĀH").
- A17 (84:6 - "toil toward your Lord and you will MEET Him"): the meet-word (mulāqāh) - the ṢALĀH is for those who orient toward MEETING. E6's humble ones are those who orient toward meeting God.
- B2 (2:157 - ṢALAWĀT upon the patient + raḥmah): the divine ṢALAWĀT is granted to those who are HUMBLE in their ṢALĀH (E6). The human ṢALĀH (humble) invites the divine ṢALAWĀT.
- 23:2 "those who are humble in their ṢALĀH" - the ṢALĀH is the act of the humble. The same as 2:45 ("heavy except on the humble") - the ṢALĀH is heavy for the proud, light for the humble, and produces humility in the humble.

FINDING: E6 gives the ṢALĀH's HUMILITY-DIMENSION: "Those who are HUMBLE (khāshiʿūn) in their ṢALĀH." The ṢALĀH is the act of the HUMBLE: the ṢALĀH produces humility (khushūʿ). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who is humble in it. And the ṢALĀH-humble are the INHERITORS of Firdaws (23:10-11). The ṢALĀH is the act of one who inherits the highest garden. The ṢALĀH is paired with: turning away from idle talk (23:3), doing zakah (23:4), guarding private parts (23:5-7), being faithful to trusts and covenants (23:8). The ṢALĀH is embedded in a comprehensive ethical character.

CARRIED FORWARD: (i) The HUMILITY (khushūʿ) - the ṢALĀH produces humility. HOLD for the final doc's humility-table. (ii) The IDLE-TALK-AVOIDANCE (23:3) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who turns away from idle talk. HOLD for the final doc's anti-idle-talk table. (iii) The TRUST-FAITHFULNESS (23:8) - the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is faithful to trusts and covenants. HOLD for the final doc's faithfulness-table. (iv) The FIRDAWS-INHERITANCE (23:10-11) - the ṢALĀH-humble are the inheritors of Firdaws. HOLD for the final doc's reward-table. (v) The ṢALĀH now has THIRTY-EIGHT forms: physical (standing, C13), temporal (timed, C14), vocal (moderated voice, C15), preservative (guarded, C16), personal (commanded to the self, C19), purifying (purification-package, C20), eased (adjusted to capacity, C21), adaptive (shortened/adapted to circumstances, D1), patient (borne with divine presence, D6), pure (gated on purity, accessible via tayammum, D7), washed (wuḍūʾ, D13), bowing (rukūʿ, D14), called (publicly announced, D15), enemy-targeted (Satan averts from it, D16), legal (anchors legal proceedings, D17), fear-paired (ṢALĀH + taqwā, D18), Book-holding (holds fast to the Book, D20), peace-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = peace, D22), brotherhood-condition (repent + ṢALĀH + zakah = brothers in religion, D23), mosque-maintaining (ṢALĀH-establisher maintains God's mosques, D24), laziness-targeted (hypocrite comes lazily, D25), community-bond (believers are allies who establish ṢALĀH, D26), directed (has a qibla, D27), God-facing (seeks God's face, repels evil with good, D28), urgent (must be done before the Day, D29), leadership (ṢALĀH-establisher is a leader who guides by God's command, D30), heart-trembling (heart trembles when God is mentioned, D31), first-act-of-the-empowered (ṢALĀH is the first act when given authority, D32), no-difficulty (the religion has no difficulty; the ṢALĀH is easy, D33), non-distracted (neither trade nor sale distracts from the ṢALĀH, D34), successional (the ṢALĀH is the act of the successors, D35), household-clock (the ṢALĀH is the time-marker of the household's privacy rules, D36), Hereafter-certain (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who is certain of the Hereafter, D37), turning-to-God (the ṢALĀH is the turning-to-God act, D38), Hereafter-certain-repeat (31:4 = 27:3, D39), unseen-God-fearer (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who fears God in the unseen, D40), eternal-trade (the ṢALĀH is the trade that never perishes, D41), response-consultative (the ṢALĀH is the act of one who responds to God and consults, D42), forgiven (the ṢALĀH is the act of the forgiven, D43), economic-clock (the ṢALĀH is the temporal boundary of the economic life, D44), hypocrite-lazy-show-little (the ṢALĀH is the act of the lazy, show-off, little-rememberer, E3), mocker-whistling-clapping (the ṢALĀH is turned into whistling and clapping by the mockers, E4), and humble (the ṢALĀH is the act of the humble, E6). All thirty-eight are "state" features.

VERDICT: studied - see E7 (23:9 - those who guard their salah) next.
